# Resonus — Master Review &amp; Fix Report

> One self-contained document, compiled 2026-10-22: executive summary, the full
> fix brief for "what can be removed" and "what can be written cleaner", the six
> complete agent analyses (architecture, dead code, core-file quality, playlists,
> podcasts, albums/artists, wishlist) and — in Appendix H — the former
> hygiene review (`docs/CODE-HYGIENE-FINDINGS.md`, 2026-10-21, pre-merge,
> **removed from the repo**) reproduced where its content is not already
> superseded by the fresh analyses.
>
> Baseline: branch `main` @ `b1075627` (includes podcast PR #273).
> 281 files, ~76k LOC in `src/`. **Nothing has been applied or committed** —
> this is an execution plan.
>
> **Read the "Update after the tree moved on (2026-10-29)" section first.** This
> document is carried by branch `review-2026-10-22` and opened as PR #297 against
> upstream `main`; that branch adds nothing but this file (`git diff main...review-2026-10-22`
> = this document). The `main` it will merge into is `e587bd5b` — **17 commits and 27
> changed `src/` files past the draft's baseline**, so the claims below were written
> against code that no longer looks like the tree this PR lands in.

## How to work with this report (agent protocol)

1. **Never delete or restructure before verifying.** Run the item's `Verify`
   command first; if the output contradicts the claim, stop and report — do not
   "adapt" the claim to the finding.
2. One work package (WP) = one focused change, landed and checked before the next.
   Items marked `⚠ BEHAVIOR` change runtime behavior and need extra care + tests.
3. After **every** WP run the full check suite, no regression allowed:
   ```
   npm run typecheck     # tsc --noEmit
   npm test              # 176 / 49 as drafted; measured 2026-10-29: 188 / 52 / 0 fail
   pnpm i18n:check       # must still pass
   pnpm i18n:status      # D7's stale items AND the 8 new keys' missing entries in fr/it/pt-BR/tr/uk
   pnpm icons            # only after D4
   ```
4. Never touch anything in the **Do-not-delete guard list** (Part 1 §G) — several
   apparent-dead items have dynamic consumers and deleting them breaks the build.
5. Where an item says "maintainer decision", answer it before editing and record
   the decision in the commit message.

## Two pre-merge claims corrected against current code (verified 2026-10-22)

| Hygiene-review claim (pre-merge) | Current state | Action |
|---|---|---|
| "`api/podcasts.ts` never imports `assertCanRequest`; refreshAll/subscribe fire live in offline mode" | **Already fixed** by PR #273: `api/podcasts.ts:11` imports `assertCanRequest`, `get()` gates at `:98` with `assertCanRequest(!isManualOffline())` — and the comment there documents the deliberate design (publisher feeds stay reachable in auto-offline; only manual offline says no) | **Do not "fix again"** — Part 2 R11 carries this as a status note |
| "`ndLogin` returns cached JWT at `:54` before `assertCanRequest()` at `:56`" | **Still present, verified**: `ndJson` (`api/navidrome.ts:323`) runs `for (const fresh of [false, true])`; the fresh=false iteration returns the cached token inside `ndLogin` **without** running the gate, and then `ndJson` live-fetches with it | Real bug — Part 2 R12, fix by moving the gate above the cache check — **fixed 2026-10-22, PR #284** (`navidrome-gate-before-jwt`, `418f2670`) |

### Second pass — per-file re-verification (2026-10-29, all seven api/store/component clusters)

The rows above are the headline moves. Re-running every item's own `Verify` command per file added these:

- **C14 is wider than the draft's two anchors.** Four timeout tokens now coexist: `subsonic.ts:444`,
  `jellyfin.ts:69`, `navidrome.ts:45 ND_TIMEOUT_MS` (introduced by `c74a03cd`), `podcasts.ts:64 TIMEOUT_MS = 20_000` —
  and **three** copies of the deadline machinery, not one: `subsonic.ts:548–549` (`request()`, which adopted only
  the read race `untilAborted:594`), `jellyfin.ts:198–199` (`request()`), `jellyfin.ts:257–258` (`makeAuth`) —
  plus `backend.ts:75 reachable()`'s fourth spelling (`setTimeout(… 4000)`, `:68`). The finding should now say
  "one deadline helper used by all of them", and it must cover **`podcasts.ts:118 await res.text()`**, which still
  awaits a body read that never hears the abort signal — the exact hang `c74a03cd` was written against, still open
  at the podcast-feed read.
- **The deadline work introduced a new i18n/defect surface that no finding covers.** `DeadlineError`'s constructor
  message is a hard-coded English literal (`deadline.ts:19`), so localising it is impossible by construction —
  every caller re-types the sentence, and `navidrome.ts:52 ndWithin` does it **untranslated** (`navidrome.ts` has
  no `tg`/`useT` import at all, and no Navidrome string is a locale key). Still bare on the way out:
  `subsonic.ts:1010 \`Network error (${res.status})\`` and `:1018 sub.error?.message` (R15's remaining half —
  the draft's `:1013` is the `.catch` mapping, not the throw), `subsonic.ts:961 'Created playlist not found'`,
  `jellyfin.ts:227`/`:283`. Worse, `jellyfin.ts:227` puts an **HTTP status into the `code` slot** — documented at
  `subsonic.ts:465–:470` as Subsonic's own error code and compared against `ERR_NOT_FOUND` at `:611`/`:874`, so one
  field now carries two vocabularies; `add status?: number` rather than overload `code`. Downstream there is no
  type branch for a deadline at all: `backend.ts:19` re-exports only `COVER, normalizeUrl, SubsonicRequestError`,
  while `auth.ts:351` and `player.ts:1291`/`:1367` branch on `SubsonicRequestError` only — and `podcasts.ts:122–:125`
  collapses an aborted read into `FeedError('http')`, `backend.ts:79` swallows `Error('timeout')`, so the timeout-vs-refusal
  split survives in exactly two places (`subsonic.ts:1013–:1015`, `navidrome.ts:52`).
- **R15 remainder, re-anchored:** `retryWithCanonicalId:504` is still called only from `request()` (`:611–:613`),
  `requestPost` grep = 0 in `src/`, `savePlayQueue`'s catch-all is now `:1564`.

  - **New module, not covered by any finding:** `src/lib/distribution.ts` — two constants (`DISTRIBUTION`/`SELF_UPDATES` from `EXPO_PUBLIC_DISTRIBUTION=fdroid`). Clean and small; nothing to fix.
- Outside `src/`: F-Droid store listing + fastlane scripts, new plugins (`withAbiVersionCode`, `withGradleMemory`), and the `buildFromSource` list in `package.json` extended to more modules. **D5 (`expo-web-browser` removable) is still true in the current tree** (dep `package.json:38`, plugin entry `app.json:68`, no code refs); its "verify at SDK bump" caveat covers the wider source-build list.
- Commit `c9745c25` moved `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md` into `.gitignore` — Part 4's statement about them stays true; no other finding affected.

No test (store layer has none) |
| **C9** — Jellyfin `toSong` drops `UserData.PlayCount` | [#283](https://github.com/juananzzz/resonus/pull/283) | `jellyfin-song-play-count` | `5a530733` | `playCount: it.UserData?.PlayCount` added. **`played` NOT added** — the report said "add `playCount`/`played`", but `played` is a field of `Album` (`api/subsonic.ts:223`+); `Song` (`:86`) never had it, so mapping it would have been a type error. Checked against Jellyfin's own source (not just the app): `ItemFields.cs` has **no `UserData` entry**, so `Fields=` cannot ask for it and no request parameter has to be added; `DtoOptions()` keeps `EnableUserData = true`, `AddAdditionalDtoOptions` writes it only `if (enableUserData.HasValue)`, and `ItemsController.cs` (the `Users/{userId}/Items` route the app calls) builds `new DtoOptions { Fields = fields }` before that call — so the count was arriving and being dropped. The current parameter is named `enableUserData`; the old `includeUserData` no longer exists on `master` |

Re-verified on `main` @ `e587bd5b` before shipping: anchors `:1785`/`:1334` (report `:1754`/n.a.), **no call site reached the 300 default** (all 18 `songCoverUrl(` sites pass a size — one more than the draft's 17; wrappers in `data.ts`/`backend.ts` forward, `upnp.ts:399`/`mirrorCovers.ts:320`/`carAutoTree.ts:86` pass tiers) → zero behavior change, the future-caller trap gone. Juni 2025" where it was "June 15, 2025". ## Findings corrected against the current tree

- **R19i's severity is off by ~32× — do not "fix" it as a behaviour bug.** The asymmetry is real (`albumsByIds` `downloadsDb.ts:578` builds one `IN (…)` with one `?` per id, unbounded, while `songsByIds` `:594` chunks at a hardcoded 400), but the refusal point is far away: expo-sqlite vendors SQLite with `#define SQLITE_MAX_VARIABLE_NUMBER 32766` (`node_modules/expo-sqlite/vendor/sqlite3/sqlite3.c:14262`), and a probe of a stock sqlite3 build accepted 30,000 placeholders in this exact query shape. So the empty artist screen needs ≈32k album ids, not the ">~1000 downloaded albums" the item claimed; on the Android/iOS builds the limit is unverified (`libsql` ships prebuilt, no visible defines). Keep it latent, close it with the DB-layer merge (Part 3 §2d). **Two traps for whoever chunks it later:** the ordering is SQL-side today (`ORDER BY added_at DESC, name COLLATE NOCASE`), so chunking must re-sort across chunks and reproduce SQLite's ASCII-only `NOCASE` fold, or the artist's album list silently changes order.

- **R24b (recent searches leaking across profiles) — real bug, not reachable on command, so not shipped.** The mixing is exactly as described: `store/recentSearches.ts:66` tracked its key in a module `let` that only `hydrate` consulted, while `add`/`remove`/`clear` resolved the key at the write (`persist()` `:68`), so a write after a profile switch files the previous profile's list under the new profile's key and wipes it. What the item leaves out is how often that can happen — all three writers need a finger (`app/(tabs)/search.tsx`: `clear` on the Pressable at `:317`, `remove` at `:341`, `add` at `:398/437/463/517`; no subscription, no timer), `switchProfile` is only offered on the profile screen (`app/login.tsx:204`), there is exactly one `hydrate()` call site (`_layout.tsx:247`, inside the chain that hydrates eight stores), and the key only differs between a **server account and the local-files profile** (`storageKey()` reads `auth` first, so a server account that goes offline keeps its own key). The race therefore needs a profile press on the login screen plus a search tap inside the hydration window, with the finger elsewhere. Keep the finding for what it is worth: this is the last store in the cluster still hand-tracking its key while `pins.ts:49`, `lastPlayed.ts:75`, `settings.ts:1338` and `autoDownloads.ts:41` carry `profileScopeGuard` — **close it with the store-cluster merge, not as its own PR.**

- **C10 (Jellyfin `makeAuth` throws the wrong error type) — the duplication is real, the stated consequence is not reachable, so it ships as hygiene at most, never as a bug fix.** The two halves that hold: `src/api/jellyfin.ts:234 makeAuth` does hand-roll the `AbortController` + `setTimeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS)` + `catch { if (aborted) … }` scaffolding that `request()` already has (`:197–231`), and it does throw bare `Error` where `request()` throws `SubsonicRequestError(…, true)` for the same two cases — timeout at `:214`, no connection at `:216`, versus `makeAuth`'s `:259`/`:261`. What does **not** follow is the consequence the item claims for `store/auth.ts:341`: that `try` wraps `ping(profile)` at `:336`, and `ping` (`backend.ts:57 = api(auth).ping(auth)` → `jellyfin.ts:290`) goes through `request()`, so it already throws the right type and the offline-mode branch fires correctly. `makeAuth` has exactly one entry point — `store/auth.ts:268 login:`, reached through `backend.ts:53` — and nothing type-branches its error: `login.tsx:196` renders `e instanceof Error ? e.message : t("Couldn't sign in")`, and `makeAuth`'s strings are already `tg()`-wrapped, so there is no i18n defect either. The two other type branches in the file (`jellyfin.ts:1227`, `:1240`) sit in playlist ops, not in `makeAuth`'s chain; `player.ts:1261`/`:1336` are about `playbackReport`. Verdict: **type-consistency + factoring hygiene (it belongs to the same sweep as R15's `requestPost()` sibling and Group A's `apiError()` helper — a shared fetch/timeout factor used by `request()` and `makeAuth`), zero behaviour payoff** — anyone shipping it must say so, because the tempting description ("unreachable Jellyfin locks you out instead of going offline") is not what happens.

- **R19d (local `updatePlaylist` drops `public`) — the code is as described, but nothing is "replayed and lost": the parameter is dead, and the severity is Info, not ⚠ BEHAVIOR.** True part: `localQueries.ts:1090` accepts `{ name?, comment?, public? }` while the spread at `:1099–1101` writes only `name`/`comment`, and `toPlaylist` (`:954`) never emits the field — the local record has no such concept. The claim that fails is which path swallows a real user action, and both branches say the opposite: a **server account** offline does not go through the local function at all — `data.ts:1774` queues the patch in `offlineQueue.setPlaylist` and reconnect replays it through `Subsonic.updatePlaylist` **with** `public` (`data.ts:1461–1470` for a newly created playlist, `:1474–1480` for an existing one, → `subsonic.ts:952–964` sends the `public` param), so the server applies it. A **local-files profile** does reach `Local.updatePlaylist` (`data.ts:1767`) — and there the toggle that could send a changed `public` is hidden exactly where it would matter: `app/playlist/[id].tsx:622 hidePublic={offline}` (rendered at `PlaylistEditSheet.tsx:170`), documented at `PlaylistEditSheet.tsx:35` "Hides the public playlist toggle (e.g. in local mode, no server)", and a local profile is always offline. So the field is unreachable, hence unobservable. Honest fix: drop `public` from the local layer's signature — or keep it and say in one comment line that the local layer has no such concept. Part 5 already had this right (its item on `localQueries.ts:1090-1106`: "consistent with hidden toggle offline (`[id].tsx:615`), so no user-visible defect; flagged so it isn't mistaken for support. **Info**") — it is Part 2's and Appendix H's ⚠ wording that contradicts it. **Do not ship as a behaviour fix; close it with the local-playlist cluster.**

## Executive summary

### 1. Real bugs worth fixing now (verified in current code)

| # | Location | Problem |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `src/store/player.ts:3094` | `queueStorageKey()` asks **offline mode before profile** → every server account writes its queue into one shared `resonus.queue.offline` bucket. (verified `:3096 if (offline) return 'resonus.queue.offline'`) |
| 2 | `src/store/player.ts:4766` | `reset()` hand-enumerates ~30 module globals; ~20 survive a profile switch (`warmedIds`, stall counters, `pendingSeek`, `queuedNext`…) → new account silently skips pre-warming. One `clearPlaybackState()` over a grouped object fixes it |
| 3 | `src/api/jellyfin.ts:878` | Playlist edit sheet offers a **description edit that is silently dropped** for Jellyfin (verified: body only maps `Name`/`IsPublic`) yet a success toast fires |
| 4 | `api/navidrome.ts:323` `ndJson` | `ndLogin` returns the **cached JWT before** `assertCanRequest()` → warm token = live fetch in offline mode (verified current; see Part 2 R12) — **fixed, #284** |
| 5 | `src/api/subsonic.ts:982/1528` | `reorderPlaylist`/`savePlayQueue` re-implement `request()` inline: no timeout, plain Spanish `Error`, migration retry skipped → reordering fails against stale Navidrome ids — **timeout half shipped (`c74a03cd`, `api/deadline.ts`); anchors now `:993`/`:1538`, see Part 2 R15 and the 2026-10-29 update** |
| 6 | `src/lib/podcastDb.ts` | No played-state column in the episode schema → no mark-as-played/resume for podcasts (feature gap AND data-model gap) |
| 7 | `src/store/downloads.ts` ×22 sites | DB→DB→memory tier cascades hand-copied 22× with 22 swallowed empty catches (`lib/localQueries.ts`) |
| 8 | `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:288` | The Library "All" tab never refetches podcasts on pull-to-refresh (react-query keys copied from the other four tabs) — **CLOSED by #303 (`all-tab-refresh-podcasts`, `2bc3e097`): AllTab refetches all four queries on a pull, spinner counts them; the five-tab duplication stays open as its own job** |
| 9 | `src/api/data.ts:394/438/475/764` | The four genre endpoints have **no offline branch** while every sibling list in the layer does |
| 10 | `src/lib/localQueries.ts:1283` | Memory search tier builds artist keys with `s.artist \|\| ''` while the DB tier uses `UNKNOWN_ARTIST` → unknown-artist songs never reach tier 3 |
| 11 | `src/app/favorites.tsx:306/:334` | Two identically-worded dialogs with different delete semantics (filtered vs unfiltered `songIds`) — one confirms deleting only what's on disk, the other deletes DB rows for all |
| 12 | `src/lib/localQueries.ts:733` | `getArtistInfo` stub returns `{similarArtists: []}` but `radioBuild.ts:157` treats it as real → offline radios lose the whole similar-artist tier silently |
| 13 | `src/store/playCounts.ts:10` | Only persisted collection that is not profile-scoped and unbounded — "Most played" mixes song ids across accounts (song ids are only unique per account) |

### 2. Top refactor opportunities (lines that would disappear)

1. `lib/localQueries.ts` — DB→memory tier cascade ×22 with per-function order vocabularies (~450 lines) → one `tieredQuery(tiers)` + one comparator table.
2. `store/settings.ts:1596` — 418-line hand-written `applySaved` validation while `autoSetters` (`:1546`) already derives the same per-key table (~250 lines).
3. `store/downloads.ts:1207` — `downloadPodcastGroup` is a near-verbatim 130-line copy of `downloadGroup` (~170 lines).
4. `src/components/TrackListView.tsx:229` — 1039-line component, 35 props, four parallel index spaces, play button drawn twice (~250 lines; split into row/selection/header parts).
5. `src/app/player.tsx` — 1674-line component with a 578-line return, import-time storage side effect (~200 lines).
6. `src/api/data.ts` — the multi-library dispatch ladder hand-written 11× → one `perLibrary(fetchOne, merge)` combinator (~60 lines).
7. `src/store/downloads.ts:192` — seven module cache vars + two in-flight markers, hand-tagged dir keys, no eviction (~140 lines → one LRU helper).
8. `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:288` — five tab components repeat the same keys/hooks/FlatList scaffolding (~180 lines).
9. Capability checks: `serverType !== 'jellyfin'` string-compare in ~11 screens → extend `backend.ts` predicates (`supportsRating/supportsSharing/supportsFolders/…`).
10. Seven sheets re-implement `SheetModal` (~350 dup lines in `MediaMenuSheet.tsx` etc.) and the download `Dialog` quartet is written in 8 places — one shared each.
11. `useSelectionMenu.tsx:63` — serial star request per selected song (40 round trips) → batched with the existing `slots.ts` limiter.
12. `store/player.ts` (4 898 LOC, 93 module singletons) → 15 named sub-modules (sleep timer, cast sync, autoplay, queue persistence, warm-up…).

### 3. What can be deleted outright (grep-verified, whole repo)

- `artistsLabel` (`src/i18n/index.ts:129`) — zero call sites → delete it **plus** the orphan `PLURALS.artist` table. (verified)
- `castStart` (`src/store/castMedia.ts:51`) and `castMediaAvailable` (`:14`) — never referenced.
- `WORD_RISE_PX` (`src/lib/lyricSweep.ts:35`) — zero imports; `KaraokeWords.tsx` re-declares the same value privately.
- `PodcastEpisode.description` field (`src/api/subsonic.ts:1959`) — written and persisted, never read (leftover from PR #273). ⚠ open PR #278 would **consume** the stored notes at merge — see D6's coordination note before deleting.
- 4 dead keys in `icon-map.json` (`cloud-outline`, `ellipsis-vertical`, `headset`, `log-out-outline`).
- ~69 needless `export` keywords (biggest cluster: 14 in `store/settings.ts`) + 16 stale podcast locale keys × 5 locale files.
- `expo-web-browser` dependency + plugin entry — zero references anywhere.
- Do **not** delete: `+native-intent.ts` (expo-router dynamic call), `settings/network.tsx` (reachable via dynamic `router.push`), `index.js` (expo entry), `src/stubs/material-symbols.js` (metro.config uses it to strip ~6 MB fonts).
- Clean bill of health: all 176 tests pass; no commented-out blocks; no orphan modules.

### 4. Where component separation is wrong (worst 8)

1. Hooks return **UI**: `useGridColumns.tsx:54-122` embeds a whole `GridSheet`; sort hooks return `SortSheet` nodes; `useSelectionMenu.tsx:156-177` returns a `Dialog`. Hooks should return data; JSX belongs in components.
2. `useSelectionMenu.tsx:63-81` runs the star/unstar **API loop and toasts** inside a hook → actions belong in store, dialog in components.
3. `src/api/` is not just network clients: `data.ts` reads auth/offline/settings stores directly and `podcasts.ts` talks to SQLite + the player store → split network client from orchestration/persistence halves.
4. `src/lib/` is supposed to be pure — ~1/3 of its modules import store singletons and 4 fire user-facing toasts (`playRadio.ts`, `favoritesCache.ts`…).
5. `app/player.tsx:139/617` does disk persistence (LAYOUT blob) inside a screen; `app/_layout.tsx:245-311` hand-wires the hydration DAG of ~15 stores in the root screen → `store/bootstrap.ts`.
6. `app/(tabs)/library.tsx` creates playlists + cache invalidation inside a screen handler; `app/podcast/[id].tsx:45-90` puts background feed-reread business inside a `queryFn`.
7. Profile identity is re-implemented in three places (`libraries.ts profileKeyOf`, `auth.ts profileScopeId`, `player.ts queueStorageKey`) — the queue bug in §1 is a symptom.
8. Selection state machine hand-copied 4× (`genre/[name].tsx:177`, `browse/songs.tsx:221`, `artist/songs/[id].tsx:171`, `TrackListView`); search box hand-drawn 9×; press feedback inline 82× bypassing `usePressFeedback`.

### 5. Feature gaps — playlists, podcasts, albums, artists

**Playlists**: content search missing; stars gated to Navidrome ≥0.64 although the write path works everywhere; no m3u export/import; zero pagination (O(n) per-song round trips on big playlists, reorder rewrites the full id list last-writer-wins); Jellyfin edit sheet lies (see §1); no playlist cover upload for Jellyfin; auto-download never prunes files for songs pulled out of a kept playlist.

**Podcasts**: show notes parsed+stored but never rendered → **open PR #278 already fixes this** (episode rows will read notes + `Show more`/`Show less` + length · publish date); no played-state/resume (schema lacks the column); no in-show search/sort/multi-select; no new-episode badge or background refresh-all → **partially in flight via PR #278** (per-show `new` arrival mark from `PodcastEpisode.firstSeenAt`/`PodcastChannel.newSinceAt` + `Refresh (… ago)` clock; unread-across-shows, foreground refresh-all and the Home badge still missing); no auto-download of new episodes for kept shows (the machinery exists for playlists/favorites — and PR #278 brings exactly the clocks such a rule needs); no storage cap/prune for episode downloads; no RSS authentication (private feeds impossible); no Podcast 2.0 chapters/transcripts despite existing TTML/LRC machinery; podcasts absent from cast/UPnP/CarPlay pipelines.

**Albums/Artists**: starred albums not offered as browse order although the pipeline supports it; genre album-sort menu is Navidrome-only (Jellyfin already carries the sorts); album playCount/last-played dead data (only used for ordering, never shown); album rating settable but never visible in the list UI; no star/pin indicator on any browse tile although `useFavoriteIds` exists; artists can't be pinned at all; artist A–Z disagrees across screens (`name` vs `sortName`; Jellyfin never maps `SortName`); release kind drives artist shelves but not the album's own header; genre counts are global but the list under them is library-scoped; `favorites-add.tsx` can't favourite albums/artists and never surfaces starred-album songs.

### 6. Features a music app should still add (prioritized)

Podcast subscriptions + new-episode badge · smart/affinity shuffle (affinity pool already built for mixes) · listening-statistics screen (playCounts trusted, history capped at 100) · OpenSubsonic role credits/featuring UI (already parsed, never rendered) · album-edition collapsing via musicBrainzId · M3U/XSPF playlist import (`expo-document-picker` already a dep) · download resume via HTTP Range · per-server storage quota view · EQ preset import/export · cross-profile media matching · Chromecast (only UPnP/Sonos today) · iOS parity (EQ/gapless/cast/CarPlay — `audio-eq` module is Android-only) · mid-track resume position for podcasts · chapters/M4B.

# PART 1 — Code that can be removed (grep-verified repo-wide)

The dead-code agent's method (reproducible): extract every
`^export (function|const|let|class|interface|async function)` (979 unique names),
word-boundary scan each across `src test scripts modules patches plugins android
ios-widget app.json`; check re-export syntax (`export {…} from`, bare `export {}`),
`require(...)` property access and dynamic `await import(...)` separately.
Full evidence and method: Part 4.

## D1 — `artistsLabel` + its orphaned plural table  `⚠ small, certain`

- **Location:** `src/i18n/index.ts:129` (function), `src/i18n/index.ts:62` (`PLURALS.artist` entry, 9 language forms).
- **Verify:** `grep -rn "artistsLabel" src test scripts modules patches plugins` → exactly 1 hit (the declaration). Also `grep -rn "countLabel(" src` to see that no other wrapper consumes the `artist` forms.
- **Fix:** delete the function **and** the `artist:` entry in `PLURALS` — cascade: once `artistsLabel` is gone, nothing reads that table row.
- **Done:** grep returns 0; `pnpm i18n:check` + `npm run typecheck` pass.

## D2 — `castMediaAvailable` and `castStart` in `store/castMedia.ts`  `certain`

- **Location:** `src/store/castMedia.ts:14` (`export const castMediaAvailable = !!native;`), `src/store/castMedia.ts:51` (`export function castStart(...)`).
- **Verify:** `grep -rn "castStart\b|castMediaAvailable\b" src` → only the two declarations (confirmed 2026-10-22). Live members to KEEP: `initCastMedia`, `castUpdate`, `castSetVolumeLevel`, `castSetState`, `castStop` (imported by `player.ts:89`, `upnp.ts:18`, `jukebox.ts:34`).
- **Why dead:** native proves redundancy — `modules/cast-media/android/**/CastMediaModule.kt:48` `update` falls through to `startService(ctx, info)` at `:52` when nothing is running, and every caller uses `castUpdate`.
- **Fix:** delete both functions/consts. KEEP the `CastNowPlaying` type — `castUpdate` uses it.
- **Done:** typecheck passes; grep 0 hits.

## D7 — stale locale entries in 5 locale files (provably unreachable)

- **Where:** `src/i18n/locales/*.json` — counts: `fr` 16, `it` 35, `pt-BR` 16, `tr` 17, `uk` 16 stale entries. The same 16 podcast strings exist in fr/pt-BR/uk/tr/it (leftovers of PR #273, whose cleanup commit `59ba627` removed the matching **en** keys): `This podcast's feed could not be read…`, `Play latest`, `Episodes`, `Untitled episode`, `No audio available`, `Pull down to read the feed again.`, `Unsubscribed from “{name}”`, `Tap + to subscribe to a show.`, `Open website`, `Unsubscribe`, `Couldn't read that feed.`, `Search for a podcast`, `Couldn't search for podcasts.`, `No podcast found for “{term}”….`, `Search for a show, or paste the address…`, `Subscribe`. `tr` additionally: `Navigation bar blur`. `it` additionally 18 reworded settings/share strings (`The shade of the greys…`, `Blur behind the bars`, `Share Resonus link`, `Check out {name} by {artist} on {server}`, `Open link`, …).
- **Why provably unreachable:** a translation is looked up by its English base key; if a base key were in use, `i18n:check` would fail on the en.json side — and it passes. Base keys not in en.json can never be produced by any `t(...)`/`tg(...)` call.
- **Verify:** `pnpm i18n:status <locale>` → section "Stale (delete these)".
- **Fix:** delete exactly those entries from the 5 locale JSON files. `context.jsonc` needs **no** changes (i18n-check reports zero orphan notes).
- **Done:** `pnpm i18n:status` shows no stale entries; `pnpm i18n:check` passes.

# PART 2 — Code that can be written cleaner

Ranked by impact (≈ lines that disappear) and grouped by risk class.
**A** = mechanical single-sourcing, same behavior · **B** = structural refactors ·
**C** = bug fixes that fall out of the cleanups (⚠ BEHAVIOR).
All line numbers below are CURRENT (`b1075627`), re-verified, unless marked
*(pre-merge)* — that term means: taken from the former hygiene review, which is
reproduced in Appendix H (re-grep before fixing — its numbers predate PR #273).

## Group A — single-sourcing (behavior-neutral unless noted)

### R1 — One `SearchBox` component (11 hand-drawn copies) → ~260 lines gone  `⚠ contains live a11y bug`

- **Copies (all verified current):** `TrackListView.tsx:809–830` (+styles `:1320` height 44), `library.tsx:1346–1369` (+styles `:1524/:1528/:1534`), `PlaylistsBrowser.tsx:177–201`, `PodcastAddSheet.tsx:97–113`, `podcasts.tsx:202`, `radio.tsx:194`, `browse/songs.tsx:328`, `browse/albums.tsx:223`, `browse/artists.tsx:256`, `genres.tsx:100`, `favorites-add.tsx:175`.
- **Shape:** `SearchBox({ query, onChange, onFocus, searchable, animated })` in `src/components`. Each copy is the same trio: `Icon name="search" size={18}` + `TextInput` + `close-circle` "Clear" `Pressable`.
- **Provable drift to fix while extracting:** Clear `Pressable` has `hitSlop={8}` in `TrackListView.tsx:823` but the same control elsewhere has a bigger target (≈42 px vs 52 px) — one component picks one value, closing a real accessibility inconsistency. The `height: 44` + pill-radius comment becomes single-sourced too.
- **Done:** 11 call sites reduced; screenshots of all 11 surfaces still show one search box each; typecheck.

### R2 — Seven sheets re-implement `SheetModal` (~350 dup lines)

- **Evidence:** `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:228–473` (whole modal + backdrop styles at `:541…`) and six more sheets rebuild the open/close animation, the grabber bar and the backdrop instead of composing the shared `SheetModal`; `hooks/useGridColumns.tsx:91` proves `SheetModal` is the right base (it already uses it correctly).
- **Fix:** make the seven sheets compose `SheetModal` (props: content, openRef, dismiss handler); delete per-sheet backdrop/animation copies.
- **Implementation status (2026-10-22, local branch `sheets-on-sheet-modal`, NOT committed — testing first):**
  - **Base extractor done:** `SheetModal.tsx` extended — `export type CloseSheet = (after?: () => void) => void`; `openRef` now **optional**: without it the sheet is open on mount and hides by unmounting once `onClosed` lets the store data go (`closeNow` only clears internal state in ref mode) — that is the store-sheet pattern (`MediaMenuSheet`/`ShareSheet`/`PlaylistPicker`/`ArtistPicker`); `modal?: (close: CloseSheet) => ReactNode` adds extra `Modal` children over the sheet (`Dialog`s, `DateTimePicker`). All six existing consumers untouched/compiling.
  - **Converted:** `MediaMenuSheet` (scaffold + backdrop/sheet/grabber styles deleted, dialogs threaded via a `dialogs` const into `modal`; component-level `withSongs`/`toggleFavorite`/`askExport` take `close: CloseSheet`; `dismiss(() => { closeNow(); X })` → `close(X)`; `runExport`'s bare `closeNow()` kept deliberately — it must NOT play the exit animation). `ShareSheet` (same, non-Android `DateTimePicker` into `modal`; `share`/`pickDate`/`onDatePicked` thread `close`). Suite green: typecheck ✓, 176/49 tests ✓, i18n ✓. Net −89 lines so far.
  - **False positive:** `ColorPickerDialog.tsx` is **not** a bottom sheet (centered card, `animationType="fade"`, no `useBottomSheetAnim`) — excluded from R2.
  - **Remaining, need further `SheetModal` modes:** `OutputSheet` (single detector but `pan.enabled(atTop)` + `sheet` style adds `maxHeight: '84%'`); `ArtistPickerSheet`/`PlaylistPickerSheet` (two detectors — header keeps its own always-on `makePan()`, list gets `pan.enabled(atTop)`; `ArtistPicker` handle is `surfaceHighlight`, not the grabber); `SongInfoSheet.tsx:218-…`; `SongMenuSheet.tsx:371-…` (two detectors, its list scrolls). Re-run `npm run typecheck` + `npm test` + `pnpm i18n:check` after every sheet.

### R3 — The download `Dialog` quartet written 8× (divergent delete paths!) `⚠ BEHAVIOR`

- **Sharpest instance:** `src/app/favorites.tsx:306–320` vs `:334–345` — two dialogs with **identical title/message** but `confirmDeleteDl` deletes `songIds.filter((sid) => !!files[sid])` (only what is on disk) while `confirmRemoveDl` calls `void deleteSongs(songIds)` unfiltered. Collapse to one confirm flag + one delete path.

### R7 — Three `SORT_LABEL` tables over one overlapping vocabulary

- **Locations (verified current):** `hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:21`, `hooks/useSongSort.tsx:23`, `hooks/useServerSort.tsx:31`. `alpha→'Alphabetical'` ×3, `artist` ×2, `year` ×2, `added` ×2; `recent→'Default'` collides with `server→'Default'` while `recent→'Recently played'` lives in the same table. One label table keyed by the union of sort keys, in `store/sortPrefs.ts` (which already owns `SongSortField`/`AlbumSortField`/`SortField`).
- Related foot-gun: `hooks/useBrowseSort.tsx:28` casts browse-order strings (`ArtistSort`, `AlbumListType`, `SongListSort`) into `SortField`-typed prefs with `as unknown as SortField` — cross-key collisions possible. Widen `SortField` to a union or key prefs by `${key}:${field}`.

### R8 — Error-message conventions: 3 dialects reach the UI verbatim

- **Locations (verified current):** `api/subsonic.ts:560` `'Server took too long to respond'`, `:562` `'Could not connect to the server'`, `:587` `'Unexpected server response'`, `:1891` — bare English, module never imports `tg`; `api/jellyfin.ts:214/216` wrap **the identical strings** in `tg(…)`; `api/navidrome.ts:52/65/96/175/336` hardcode Spanish (`'Sin contraseña guardada'`, `'No se pudo conectar con el servidor'` ×4 more). All reach the UI (`ErrorBoundary.tsx:28` renders `error.message`, `login.tsx:196` renders `e.message`).
- **Fix:** one `apiError(i18nKey, network?, code?)` helper in `src/api/` returning a `SubsonicRequestError` with `tg()`-translated message; delete the Spanish literals.
- **Also (verified current):** `components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:27/:32` hardcoded Spanish (`Algo ha fallado`, `Reintentar`) — import `tg`/`useT` for both.

### R9 — Profile identity: three implementations of one concept `⚠ BEHAVIOR`

- **Locations (verified current):**
  1. `store/libraries.ts:23` `profileKeyOf()` — byte-identical to `auth.ts:206` `profileScopeId()` but returns `null` for **Jellyfin** → every Jellyfin profile shares one cache entry. Consumed at `api/data.ts:338`, `:1866`, `:2108`.
  2. `auth.ts:206` `profileScopeId()` — the intended identity (used correctly by `store/settings.ts`, `pins.ts:49`, `lastPlayed.ts:75`, `autoDownloads.ts:41` via `profileScopeGuard()`).
  3. `store/player.ts:3094` `queueStorageKey()` — third spelling, wrong order (see R10).
- **Fix:** delete `profileKeyOf`, export/import `profileScopeId` in all three consumers; express the Jellyfin restriction as a separate capability check, not a `null` identity.
- **Done:** one definition of profile identity repo-wide.

### R10 — `queueStorageKey()` asks mode before profile — **critical bug, verified current** `⚠ BEHAVIOR`

- `store/player.ts:3096` reads `if (offline) return 'resonus.queue.offline';` **before** consulting the profile → every server account that drops into offline mode writes its queue into the shared bucket the local-files profile also uses; switching profiles restores **another account's queue** (song ids are only unique per account — the same file admits it at `:4797–4799`). `playHistory.ts:24–41` documents exactly this hazard and refuses it; the rule was never carried to the queue.
- **Fix:** derive from `profileScopeId()` (`\`${KEY}.\${hashKey(profileScopeId())}\`` like the seven scoped stores do), delete the offline-first branch.
- **Test note:** `test/queue.test.ts` exists — extend it with a two-profile offline case before fixing, so the fix is proven by test.

### R11 — `api/podcasts.ts` netGate — **already fixed, do not touch** `status note`

- The pre-merge hygiene claim ("only module that never imports `assertCanRequest`") was **stale**. Verified 2026-10-22: `api/podcasts.ts:11` imports `assertCanRequest`; `get()` gates at `:98` with `assertCanRequest(!isManualOffline())`, and the comment at `:94–97` documents the deliberate split: publisher feeds and the iTunes directory are "nobody's server" and stay reachable when the app merely lost its own server; only manual "use no network" forbids them. Do NOT re-gate or "improve" this.

### R12 — `api/navidrome.ts` gate order: warm JWT skips the gate (verified 2026-10-22: `ndJson` fresh=false path returns cached token before the gate, then live-fetches) `⚠ BEHAVIOR`

- `ndJson` (`:323`) runs `for (const fresh of [false, true])`; the fresh=false iteration calls `ndLogin(auth, false)` which returns the cached token at `:54` **before** `assertCanRequest()` at `:56` runs, and `ndJson` then live-fetches with that token ungated. Today masked by upstream `isOffline()` guards in `data.ts` — exactly the "something always does" failure mode the gate was written to eliminate. Move the gate above the cache check in `ndLogin`.
- **Fixed 2026-10-22: PR #284** (branch `navidrome-gate-before-jwt`, commit `418f2670`). The claim checked out exactly as written; one line moved. Note the gate is synchronous up to the login request here, so — unlike `subsonic.ts`'s re-assert after waiting — nothing can flip the mode between the gate and the gate's own fetch.


## Group B — structural refactors (biggest line-diet wins)

### R13 — `store/player.ts` (4 898 LOC, 60 module `let` singletons, 22 of them timers)

Split into 15 named concerns: sleep timer (`:176` region), remote/cast sync (`:911–1179`), autoplay (`:1590–1893`), queue persistence (`:3063–3366`), warm-up (`:1492–1552`), radio extension, gapless/crossfade, scrobbling, stall recovery, background retry, lock-screen control, layout attach…

**R13a — `reset()` (`:4766–4836`) has drifted — critical bug.** It hand-enumerates ~30 globals; **~30 survive a profile switch**, incl. `warmedIds` (`:1493`), `warmChain` (`:1552`), `radioRound` (`:1775`), `queuedNext` (`:1917`), `pendingSeek` (`:2581`), the `stall*` quadruple (`:2594–2597`), `gaveUp` (`:2668`), `bgRetryTimer/bgWaiting/bgRounds` (`:2673–2678`), `lastStatusAt` (`:2878`), `queueDirty` (`:3157`), `lastOwnerCheck` (`:3392`) — reachable from the two `await get().reset()` callsites (`player.ts:4417`/`:4479`). Consequences: `warmUpcoming()` (`:1514` `if (warmedIds.has(song.id)) continue`) silently skips pre-warming for the new account; `bgRounds` carried over = fewer retry rounds; `lastOwnerCheck` caches "queue is ours" across **different accounts' queues**.
**Fix shape:** declare per-playback singletons in ONE object literal; `reset()` does `Object.assign(playback, initialPlaybackState())` — a singleton added inside the object is reset for free, a new module-`let` can no longer hide from `reset()`.
**R13b —** group the ~60 singletons into 4 named owners (playback / warm / autoplay-radio / sync); aggregate ~400 lines easier and deletes the drift class outright.
**R13c —** `HISTORY_MAX = 100` (`:1179`) caps **entries**, not songs — each undo entry holds two full `Song[]` refs (`queue` + `originalQueue`) → up to 100 album-sized queues retained. Cap total songs, or store `{source, sourceHref}` and re-fetch.

### R14 — `api/data.ts` (2 125 LOC) — two ladders → combinators

- **Multi-library dispatch ladder written 11×** at `:375` `getArtists`, `:487` `getAlbumsByGenre`, `:647` `getSongList`, `:806`, `:889` `getAppearsOn`, `:921` `getMostPlayedSongs`, `:950` `getRandomSongs`, `:1212` `getStarred`, `:1532` `searchAlbums`, `:1544` `searchSongs`, `:1555` `search`. Each is `if (!ids) …; if (ids.length === 1) return one; return Promise.all(ids.map(…)).then(/* ad-hoc merge */)`. Only the merge rule varies (flat-dedupe / {artists,albums,songs} split / sort-by-playCount / shuffle). One `perLibrary(fetchOne, merge = dedupeById)` combinator + named merges (`mergeStarred`, `mergeSearch`, `mergeRankedByPlayCount`, `mergeShuffled`) — ~60 lines and the merges become comparable in one place.
- **Two prefetch schedulers, two parallel counter sets:** albums `:1001–1026` (`prefetchingAlbums`/`lastAlbumPrefetch`/`ALBUM_PREFETCH_PER_RUN = 8`) vs playlists `:1031–1099` (`prefetchingPlaylists`/`lastPlaylistPrefetch`/`PREFETCH_PER_RUN = 5`), identical `if (busy) return; quiet-check; cooldown-check; busy=true; try{batches}finally{busy=false}` — and the shared constants `PREFETCH_COOLDOWN_MS:1038` / `PREFETCH_QUIET_MS:1050` are declared **below** the album scheduler that uses them (`:1007/:1008`). One `schedulePrefetch(kind, whatIsStale, perRun, concurrency)` — ~70 lines, both counter pairs vanish.
- **Naming foot-cannon:** `data.ts:1334` `flushOfflineQueue(auth: SubsonicAuth)` shadows module helper `auth()` (`:40`) — rename parameter `profile` (matches `store/auth.ts:363`).

### R15 — `api/subsonic.ts` — one `requestPost` sibling (fixes a real bug) `⚠ BEHAVIOR` — **timeout half already shipped 2026-10-23 (`c74a03cd`, `api/deadline.ts`): only error type + migration retry + factoring remain, anchors now `:993`/`:1538`**

- `reorderPlaylist` (`:982`) and `savePlayQueue` (`:1528`) bypass `request()` and re-implement the POST inline (`fetch(\`${auth.serverUrl}/rest/createPlaylist.view\`, {method:'POST'…})` at `:982`, error at `:987`/`:990`). Three losses vs `request()`: **no timeout** (request() wraps every GET in AbortController + `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` `:546–566`; these POSTs have no `signal` → a non-answering server leaves a promise that never settles — and `savePlayQueue` runs **continuously in the background** via player.ts periodic sync; the file's header comment `:9–14` documents exactly this failure class), **wrong error type** (plain Spanish `Error('Error de red (502)')` — callers branching on `e instanceof SubsonicRequestError && e.network` (`store/auth.ts:341`) take the wrong branch; the literal renders in every locale), **no migration retry** (request()'s `ERR_NOT_FOUND` path `:590–593` retries with `canonicalId(id)`; skipped here → reordering against a migrated Navidrome library fails permanently). `savePlayQueue` additionally swallows everything (`:1533–1535` `catch {}`).
- **Fix shape:**
  ```ts
  async function requestPost(auth, endpoint, params: URLSearchParams) {
    assertCanRequest(); // withTimeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS) signal; netTally;
    const res = await fetch(`${auth.serverUrl}/rest/${endpoint}`,
      { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': … }, body: params.toString(), signal });
    return unwrapSubsonic(await res.json()); // the :586–596 block extracted
  }
  ```
  `savePlayQueue` reports failure instead of swallowing. While at it: add `status?: number` to `SubsonicRequestError` (`:470`) so retry logic can one day distinguish server-vs-proxy 404s.

### R16 — Role lookups with opposite contracts

- `subsonic.ts:1857 hasJukeboxRole` swallows every failure (`:1863 catch { return false }`); `:1903 hasShareRole` deliberately rethrows network failures (`:1918`) with an eight-line comment justifying why a cached "you cannot share" must not outlive the hiccup. That reasoning is correct for **both** roles — `hasJukeboxRole` has the same bug papered over with `false`. One `getUser(auth): Promise<UserRole>` + `role('jukeboxRole')`/`role('shareRole')` readers with three-valued `boolean | null` ("no answer" ≠ "no permission"), same contract style as `serverAtLeast` (`:657`) already argues for.

### R17 — `store/downloads.ts` (1 674 LOC) — one download pipeline, one cache

- **R17a — `downloadPodcastGroup` (`:1207–1335`) is a near-verbatim copy of `downloadGroup` (`:971–1199`) — ~130 lines of duplicated worker/retry/cancel/progress machinery** (verified side-by-side: identical `active[groupKey]` guard, byte-identical "Wi-Fi only" block incl. comment, identical progress coarsening `fraction - cur.fraction > 0.05`, identical 24-line worker pool `:1288–1311` vs `:1139–1169`, byte-identical finally `:1326–1334` vs `:1190–1198`; only 3 real differences: fetch-one, cancel-guarded `ensureAlbum` absent, delete-on-completion target). One `downloadItems(groupKey, items, opts)` — items carry `{ url, dest, commit }`, opts carry the gate (`if (offline) return` vs `isManualOffline()` — already TWO spellings of one gate, `:977` vs `:1212`), the prepare step, the statusOk rule and the toast keys. Note the file itself comments "this is that same rule, said here" at `:974–976` — the duplication is admitted in prose.
- **Prerequisite:** `downloadGroup` is 230 lines with six concerns and nested closures (`ensureAlbum`/`ensureArtist` at `:1019–1073` capture `dir/auth/groupKey/tasks/trackArt/albumDone/artistDone`) — hoist `fetchSong`/`ensureAlbum`/worker-loop to module functions with explicit `Slot`/`Group` owners first, then the fold becomes possible at all.
- **R17b — cache vars (`:192–212`): seven module cache vars + two in-flight markers, hand-tagged dir keys, no eviction.** `cachedFull` (`:212`) is validated against **nothing** (`:278` recomputes only when falsy) — the invariant is enforced by a `cachedFull = null` line 400 lines away (`:276`). One keyed `Map` cache (dir-keyed, size 2) + one in-flight `Map` — deletes the "which variable is stale" class; ~140 lines.
- **R17c — the "involved albums" dedupe map is written identically 4×** at `:1439` (`downloadSongs`), `:1449` (`downloadPlaylist`), `:1498` (`downloadGenre`), `:1511` (`downloadFavorites`) (+ `[albumFromSong(song)]` spelling at `:1435`). One `involvedAlbums(songs)` next to `albumFromSong` (`:717`).
- **R17d — `deleteSongs` (`:1528`) vs `removeEpisodeDownloads` (`:1340`)** — same 3-step shape (patch files, delete on disk, drop from DB, invalidate), different DB/return/invalidate-key; the doc comment at `:1338` already admits it. One `removeDownloads(ids, drop, invalidateKeys)`.
- **R17e — hydrate guard dance:** `Promise.all` over two DB reads + two `if (run !== hydrateRun) return;` guards at `:1402` and `:1421`, the second inside a detached IIFE (`:1409–1423`) nobody awaits — `dlBitRates` settles with whichever run finishes last, and the IIFE's rejection is invisible. Adopt a small `hydrateGuard()` helper (a `src/lib/profileScope.ts` module owning the run-counter pattern) — deletes both hand-rolled shapes.

### R18 — `store/settings.ts` (2 310 LOC): one declarative settings table

- **R18a — `applySaved` (`:1596–2014`) is 418 lines of hand-written per-key validation** (193 `parsed.` touches, ~26 migration branches: `blurBars→blurMiniPlayer` `:1631`, `maxBitRate→maxBitRateCellular` `:1639`, the `showQueueButton/showDevicesButton/showSpeedButton` blob keys `:1609–1620`). The file already derives the per-key table for booleans via `autoSetters` (`:1546–1560`) and consumes it at `:1622–1629`. Add `VALIDATE: Record<PersistedKey, Validator>` (`numberFinite`, `oneOf(OPTIONS)`, `cappedStringMap(500)`…) + `MIGRATE: Array<fn>` (26 named functions) and `applySaved` becomes table-driven: ~250 lines gone, the 26 migrations become individually nameable/testable. Matters because `applySaved` is shared by the startup read (`:2243`) AND the imported-file path (`:2208`).
- **R18b — four descriptions of the same settings:** `DEFAULTS` (`:1365`), `PERSISTED_KEYS` (`:1546`, derived — good), `snapshot` (`:1357`), `exportedSettings` (`:2300` `{...snapshot}` + two `delete`s), plus a 22-line anonymous intersection at `:1600–1621` re-listing migrated keys. A single `describeSettings()` → `{ default, persisted, validate, migrate }` lets all four read one table (folds into R18a).
- **R18c — `settings.ts:2208` erases keys by passing `null`** (`applySaved({ ...saved, customFontFamily: null, customFontUri: null }, set)`) and it only works because those validators happen to reject `null` (`:1996` region). Change one validator to accept `null` and the settings-import path silently imports another phone's font. Pass an omission or `applySaved(raw, set, omit: Set<Key>)`.

### R19 — `src/lib/localQueries.ts` (1 379 LOC) — the biggest single win in the repo

- **R19a — the DB→DB→memory tier cascade is hand-copied 22×** (exact sites: `:403 :415 :483 :513 :536 :582 :590 :640 :659 :717 :757 :771 :823 :848 :874 :982 :1153 :1268 :1324 :1332 :1355 :1363`) with **22 empty `catch {}`** ("Falls through to the catalog in memory."). A corrupt downloads DB answers with the in-memory catalog and records nothing — invisible even though `diagnosticsReport.ts` has a channel for it. One `tiers(label, ...tiers)` resolver owning the fall-through and `noteTierFailure()`; ~450 lines (≈130 try/catch wrappers + ≈120 `dir`/`src` preambles).
- **R19b — one ordering vocabulary encoded three times per function** (`getAlbumList` `:388–472`: inline downloads-DB table `:394` {newest,random,alphabeticalByArtist}, memory predicate `:397`, `LOCAL_ALBUM_ORDER:331` (different vocab), `switch(type)` `:421–470`; `getSongList` `:742–814` repeats the shape). Net bug: `byYear` missing from the DB table → for a server account offline, every `getAlbumList('byYear')` page re-sorts the whole 15k-album catalog in JS **per page request** (`:471` slice after full sort), same at `:813` for songs. Fix: one order table per source derived from one vocabulary + sort once per (source, order).
- **R19c — memory-search sentinel bug (`:1283`/`:1294`)** `⚠ BEHAVIOR`: DB branch keys artist hits with `normKey(a.artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST)` (`:1233`) but memory branch uses `normKey(s.artist || '')` — artist ids in this layer ARE `normKey(artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST)` (`toAlbum` `:309`, `getAppearsOn` `:714/:728`), so tier 3 ("artist that has a matching song") never fires for the Unknown Artist bucket offline. Pass the sentinel; ~10 lines.
- **R19d — `updatePlaylist` (`:1090`) silently drops `public`** `⚠ BEHAVIOR`: signature accepts `{name?, comment?, public?}` but the spread `:1099–1101` applies only name+comment; `data.ts:1763–1770` queues a patch **including** `public` → offline "make public" is replayed and lost. Apply it (store + surface in `toPlaylist` `:954`) or reject explicitly with a comment. **Corrected 2026-10-22 — severity is Info, not BEHAVIOR: the queued patch is replayed to the server *with* `public` (`data.ts:1461–1470`/`:1474–1480` → `subsonic.ts:952–964`), and the only local path (`data.ts:1767`) hides the toggle (`app/playlist/[id].tsx:622 hidePublic={offline}`). Dead parameter — see "Findings corrected" up top; not shipped.**
- **R19e — genre endpoints have no offline branch** `⚠ BEHAVIOR`: `:394 getGenres`, `:438 getAlbumsByGenre`, `:475 getGenreArt`, `:764 getSongsByGenre` while every sibling (`getAlbumList`, `getArtists`, `getSongList`, `getStarred`, `getPlaylists`) has one. Offline the genre screen answers "Couldn't load genres" + retry instead of local tags — kept out of reach only by the Home chip filter (`index.tsx:730 OFFLINE_KEYS`), a UI coincidence not a contract. Add the branch (`localQueries` has the tags) or state the online-only contract (folder-nav header at `:815` shows that pattern).
- **R19f — one fallback rule written three times with three answers** (`getAlbum` `:491–570`: DB `first.album ?? ''` `:504` yields EMPTY title vs local/memory `|| tg('Unknown album')`; `getArtist` `:598–685`: `name ?? rows[0]?.artist ?? ''` vs `albums[0]?.artist || artistId` vs `… || (auth ? '' : artistId)` — raw-id allowed only in local mode per comment `:668–679`). One `fallbackName(kind, rows, isLocalProfile)`; blank/gibberish titles offline are user-visible today.
- **R19g — module-scope caches hand-tagged 4×:** `favCache/favCacheKey` (`:51/:52`), `playlistCache/playlistCacheKey` (`:909/:910`), `loadingPromise: Promise<any>` (`:148` — erases the `… | undefined` shape of `ensureScanCatalog`, unchecked at `rescan()` `:296`), `scanning` (`:252`). One `profileCache<T>(key, load)` deletes all four pairs (~80 lines).
- **R19h — `resetLocalLoading()` (`:125`) nulls handles but not the running scan:** the abandoned scan's result still lands and `queryClient.invalidateQueries()` still fires for a now-stale catalog; its `finally` at `:194` fights the reset. Thread an `AbortController` into `ensureScanned`/`loadFolderSongs`.
- **R19i — chunking bug `⚠ BEHAVIOR`** (lives in `downloadsDb.ts`): `albumsByIds` builds one `IN (…)` with one `?` per id (`ids.map(() => '?').join(',')`) unbounded, while sibling `songsByIds` chunks at a hardcoded `400` (`:594`) and `PARAM_CHUNK = 400` exists at `downloadsDb.ts:315` (and `localDb.ts:479` uses it properly at `:485`). An artist with >~1000 downloaded albums throws a SQLite parameter error → lands in the `catch` at `localQueries.ts:640` → the artist screen answers EMPTY. Export `PARAM_CHUNK` once; chunk `albumsByIds` with the existing `chunked()` helper (`:317`).
  - **Corrected 2026-10-22 (measured, see "Findings corrected against the current tree" up top):** the ">~1000" figure is wrong by ~32× — the vendored build's limit is `SQLITE_MAX_VARIABLE_NUMBER 32766` and a stock-build probe took 30,000 placeholders, so the empty screen needs ≈32k ids. Latent hygiene, **not a PR now**: close it with the DB-layer merge. Anyone chunking it must re-sort across the chunks (the order is SQL-side today: `ORDER BY added_at DESC, name COLLATE NOCASE`) and reproduce SQLite's ASCII-only `NOCASE` fold.


### R20 — `src/components/TrackListView.tsx` (1 546 LOC): the god-component

- **T1 — `:229–1268` is a 1 039-line component with 35 props** (`Props` `:109–227`, of which the `return` block is `:695–1268`, 574 lines JSX) and NO sub-component boundary between. Split along the props' own implication: list (rows/paging/filter/selection) + `Header` component (cover/gradient/title/meta/genres/actions, absorbing the ~140-line header block at `:801–1119`) + `DockedPlayButton` + `SearchableInList` (the third copy of the search box, see R1). ~250 lines off one file.
- **T2 — four parallel index spaces re-derived at six sites:** shown-index (FlatList), original `songs` index (`origIndex`), server index (`playlistIndices[i]`), row key (`rowKeys[i]` = `id#n`) — plus a fifth combination in `runSelectionAction` (`:447–458`). Sites: `rowKeys` built `:418–425`; `filtered` carries orig index `:634–643`; `keyExtractor` `:768` re-derives; `renderItem` `:1124` re-derives again and feeds `discHeaders?.[origIndex]` `:1127`, `position` `:1133`, `playlistIndices[origIndex]` `:1139`, `rowKey` `:1143/:1160`. The file's own history (`#132` id#n key fix; comment at `:766` "the same song can be in the list twice… same key for both") records this as a twice-hit bug magnet. **Fix:** one `Row` model built once (`{ song, origIndex, serverIndex, key }` via one `useMemo`), FlatList data IS the row; no re-derivation anywhere. ~40 lines + deletes the class.
- **T3 — the play button is drawn twice with mirrored a11y props** — header copy `:1076–1096` (`accessibilityElementsHidden={overBar && !selecting}`) and dock copy `:672–693` (`pointerEvents={overBar && !selecting ? 'auto' : 'none'}` + **inverted** `accessibilityElementsHidden={!overBar || selecting}`); opacity swaps at `:611–620`/`:622–630`/`:794–798`. One `PlayButton({ at: 'header'|'dock', active, onPress })` where `active = overBar && !selecting` is computed once — the invariant "exactly one exposed" stated once. ~40 lines, high a11y-risk otherwise.
- **T4 — `overBar` has three owners** (`useState(:346)`, `overBarRef(:347)` copy, `useLayoutEffect :623–630`, scroll listener `:794–798` with the same 3-line dance twice) → extract `setOverBarIfChanged`.
- **T5 — perf:** bare `useLayoutEffect` `:542–578` (no deps array) re-runs **every render**, doing two `measureInWindow` native queries (`:566`, `:572`) plus a rAF self-bounce (`:558`) whose only purpose is forcing another render — an engineered effect→setState→render→effect loop on a list screen's layout path. Use RN's `onLayout` on `headerRootRef` once; derive the button offset from constants (`styles.playSlot` is `PLAY_SIZE` inside `styles.actions`, gaps are tokens). ~40 replaced, perf severity.
- **T6 — invisible second `Text` drawn to count lines** (`:936–968`, opacity 0, `onTextLayout` `:941–948`, style `:1428–1436`): the description is text-shaped twice per render. Derive the count from one element + measured budget or `numberOfLines` + chevron. ~30.
- **T7/T8/T9 — naming/magic sweep:** `searchBar` at `:330` is a BOOLEAN ("box is drawable") while `styles.searchBar` is a style — rename `canSearch`; `band = SEARCH_H * 4` `:509` magic multiplier (contract at `:744–750` says the gap is `SEARCH_H`, not 4×); `OVERSCROLL_H = 1200` `:83` hard-codes a screen height where `useScreenSize()` is already in scope (`:268`).
- **T10 — the 35-prop API leaks render decisions** (`wideCover: { height, render(width,height) => ReactNode }`, `footer?`, `addAction.icon: keyof Icon.glyphMap`…). Fold cover variants into a discriminated union `{ kind: 'server'|'wide'|'favoritesArt', height? }`; icon choice becomes semantic tokens.

### R21 — `src/app/player.tsx` (1 928 LOC): the god-screen

- **V1 — 1 465-line component** (`:265–1729`, `return` `:1152–1728` = 577 JSX lines, zero extraction between, while tiny `CircleButton:161`/`SleepButton:199` ARE extracted). Split: `PlayerHeader` / `PlayerControls` / `LyricsPane` / `StarRow`, one shared `layout` object down (V2's shape).
- **V2 — module-global layout cache with import-time storage read (`:135–147`)** `⚠`: `getItem(LAYOUT_KEY).then(… lastLayout = v)` runs **at import time**, races first render, hand-rolled "whoever got there first wins" (`:141 if (!raw || lastLayout) return`). Writes at `:616/617` from inside the component's effect; reads `:507`/`:531`. Four defects: module init side effect; **two stacked player instances clobber each other's layout** (key is a module global, and `:266–267` documents that a stack keeps a second mount); validation accepts `0`/negatives (`typeof n === 'number'`); `JSON.stringify(next) === JSON.stringify(lastLayout)` change-test on a hot path. **Fix:** one `useMeasuredLayout(key, measure)` hook owning `{for,…}` + storage + validation (`every(Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)`) — module global dies. ~120.

Gesture writes SV (`:724`, `:771`), React mirror read at `:894` (`paneRel(k)`) — a swipe that commits without `setSpins` moves panels without changing which songs they show; inverse desync = song swap with static panels. Fix: one SV + React mirror derived via a `useWatch`-style subscription (or `spins` written only at the native→JS hand-off, one assignment). ~40, med-high severity.
- **V4 — orphaned doc comment at `:183–192`** documents the carousel (`usePaneStyle`, `:250`) but sits above `SleepButton` (`:199`) — a reader grepping "infinite strip" lands on the sleep timer. Move to `:249`.
- **V5 — `SWIPE_SHARE = 0.25` declared twice** (`player.tsx:120`, `MiniPlayer.tsx:44`, both used as `screenW * SWIPE_SHARE` at `:753`/`:125`) → one `theme/motion` token.
- **V6 — layout math with settings-driven slots inline** (`:493–608`, three `useEffect`s `:530–536`/`:596–608`/`:611–618`, `laidOut`/`coverStable` two-step reveal `:527`/`:607`): move to a `usePlayerLayout(screenH, insets, options)` hook; screen reads `layout.coverH`. ~100.
- **V7 — podcast-heart logic inlined at 3 sites** (`:799–810` early-return branch with its own error toast `:807`, `:956` `heartedSong(...)`, `:958`, two `FavoriteButton.podcast` props `:1327`/`:1550`) → one `toggleHeart(song, channel)` in `api/podcasts.ts` returning `{added} | throws`; one call site (the 13th `Couldn't complete the action` clone, see R3).
- **V8 — `sourceLabel` at `:977`** `song.url && !song.vod ?` reimplements `streamReachable` (`subsonic.ts:219`, documented as "the one rule the rows, queue marking and the player's source decision all read"). Related **P5 (player.ts):** "is this a podcast?" asked by TWO fields — `podcastChannelId` (`streamReachable` callers `:334`/`:448`) vs `vod` (`:858` `liveInfo`, `:887`, `:2175`, `:3994`; `player.tsx:1044` `(!song.url || song.vod)`). Their only producer `api/podcasts.ts:412 episodeToSong` happens to set both — agreement by accident. Fix: one exported `isPodcastEpisode(song)` + `streamReachable(song)`, zero inline `song.url && !song.vod` (9+ sites). `P6`: `localSourceFor`'s cellular branch (`:394`) grew an episode special case (`song.podcastChannelId || cellular`) — with P5 it reads `(isPodcastEpisode(song) || cellular)` and stops looking like an exception to a quality rule.

### R24 — Store-cluster profile-scoping bugs (⚠ BEHAVIOR each; small diffs, real consequences)

- **`store/playCounts.ts:10` `KEY = 'resonus.localPlayCounts'`** is the only persisted collection NOT partitioned per profile (`pins.ts:25`, `lastPlayed.ts:44`, `settings.ts:67`, `autoDownloads.ts:38` all use `${KEY}.${hashKey(profileScopeId())}`) — "Most played" mixes song ids across accounts although ids are only unique per account (`player.ts:4772–4774` says so). It's also the only unbounded one: `bump` (`:31`) adds one entry per song ever played, no cap, whole blob re-serialized per save. Adopt scope suffix + cap like `lastPlayed.ts:98–99` (cap 300).
- **`store/recentSearches.ts:66` `currentKey` consulted only inside `hydrate`; `add/remove/clear` call `persist()` without ownership check** → items loaded for profile A get written under profile B's key after a switch. Use `profileScopeGuard()` (already used by `settings.ts:1338`, `lastPlayed.ts:75`, `pins.ts:49`, `autoDownloads.ts:41`) and gate `persist()` on `scope.owns(key)` as `pins.ts:58` does. **Not shipped 2026-10-22 — reachability checked, see "Findings corrected" up top: every writer is a `Pressable`, so the race needs a search tap right after a profile press on the login screen. Close with the store-cluster merge.**
- **`store/upnp.ts:292 upnpDisconnect()` resets its own state (`:304–308`) but not `lastQueueSignature`/`lastPlayMode`/`inFlightSync`** which live in `upnpRemoteSync.ts:19–38` (reset only via exported `resetUpnpRemoteSyncState():34`, called from `player.ts:3612/3639`). The silent paths (`upnp.ts:314 if (!silent)`, from `player.ts:4755`, `OutputSheet.tsx:241`) skip it → a signature describing a renderer that no longer exists. Move the reset INTO `upnpDisconnect()`, drop the exported free function.
- **Unbounded UI caches:** `components/Cover.tsx:92` `memo = new Map<...>` keeps hits "for good", never prunes expired misses → unbounded for a 15k-song library per session; same at `PlayingElsewhereCard.tsx:53 const taken = new Set<string>()` never pruned. LRU-cap or periodic prune on the existing `MISS_TTL` sweep; clear `taken` per offers refresh.

## Group C — Drive-by fixes (one-liners; each grep-verified current unless noted)

| ID | Location | Fix (one line each) | ⚠ |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | `browse/artists.tsx` vs `library.tsx` | Artist A–Z disagrees: `library.tsx:408` sorts by `name` vs `browse/artists.tsx:207` by `sortName` (`librarySort.ts:160–167`); Jellyfin `toArtist` (`jellyfin.ts:383–394`) never maps `SortName` — map it and pick one rule | |
| C2 | `hooks/useServerCover.ts:102` gates password dialog on `if (!auth.ndPassword)` while every other native-API entry treats cleartext `password` as equivalent (`navidrome.ts:51`, `data.ts:511 canListNative`, `usePlaylistStars.ts:45`) | export one `canUseNativeApi(auth)` from `navidrome.ts`, use in all four places | |
| C3 | `store/player.ts:334/:448` vs `:858/:887/:2175/:3994` | see R21-V8 (single podcast predicate) — do together with R21 | ✓ |
| C4 | `hooks/useSelectionMenu.tsx:63–68` serialises one star request per selected song (`for (const s of which) await star(...)`) — 40-song multi-select = 40 round trips | batch with `Promise.all` under the existing `slots.ts` limiter | |
| C5 | `lib/navidromeRepair.ts:220/:224` `await remapMirrorIds(...).catch(() => {})` + `clearSongCacheFor(auth).catch(() => {})` — a failed remap (incl. `CatalogCollisionError` thrown as plain `Error` by `mirrorDb.ts:839` while `downloadsDb.ts:782` has a named class) is indistinguishable from a clean repair, yet the UI verdict says "repaired" | collect into the returned `Verdict` (`failed: true`); export ONE error class, throw it in both | ✓ |
| C6 | `src/app/login.tsx:64` `SORTED_LANGUAGES = [...LANGUAGES].sort((a,b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))` reintroduces the locale-dependent sort that `settings/language.tsx:17–23` documents as a bug ("choosing Русский jumped it… under the finger") | export `LANGUAGE_OPTIONS` from `@/i18n/languages`, import in both | |

'es-ES' : 'en-US')` — German/Russian/Turkish dates render US-style, while `settings/cache.tsx:36` and `podcast/[id].tsx:39` pass the app language through | one `formatDate` in `@/lib/format` with one "omit year when current" rule (there are also two `dayLabel`/`shortDate` implementations with different year rules) | ✓ **CLOSED 2026-10-30 — #304 (`history-date-locale`, `8f3a6957`): the one-token locale fix shipped (`lang` passes through; `es` ≡ `es-ES` measured). The proposed `@/lib/format` unification is NOT part of it — the three helpers' option sets differ on purpose, that needs product decisions and stays open** |
| C8 | `components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:27/:32` hardcoded Spanish (`Algo ha fallado`, `Reintentar`), never imports `useT` — the screen ignores 12 locales | use `tg()` for both | ✓ |
| C9 | `api/jellyfin.ts` `toSong` (`:347`) maps `starred` from `UserData` but omits `playCount`/`played` while `toAlbum` (`:377–378`) maps both and `JfItem` (`:110`) declares all three → `SongInfoSheet.tsx:208` shows nothing for Jellyfin songs and `data.ts` playCount sorting silently degrades | add `playCount: it.UserData?.PlayCount, played: it.UserData?.LastPlayedDate` | ✓ |
| C10 | `api/jellyfin.ts:234 makeAuth` hand-rolls AbortController+timeout scaffolding (`request()` has it at `:197–219`) and throws bare `Error` (`:259–267`) where `request()` throws `SubsonicRequestError(…, true)` for the same two cases (`:214`/`:216`) → **corrected 2026-10-22: the claimed consequence at `store/auth.ts:341` is unreachable** (that `try` wraps `ping`, which uses `request()` and already throws the right type; `makeAuth` is called only from `auth.ts:268 login:`, and `login.tsx:196` renders the message without type-branching) | factor a shared `fetchWithTimeout()`; use `SubsonicRequestError` — **type-consistency only, no behaviour payoff; not shipped** | — |

Re-anchor before touching: defaults now at `subsonic.ts:1787`/`jellyfin.ts:1336`. The Jellyfin sibling carried the identical `= 300` (found while re-verifying, ships together); `jellyfin.ts:659` literal `600` → `COVER.card`. Do not re-open** |
