# PART 3 — Architecture & component separation — full analysis

# Resonus — Architecture map & boundary findings (fresh analysis, main @ b1075627, post-PR #273)

Scope: 281 files, ~76k LOC under `src/`. All file:line references verified against the **current** tree (podcast PR included). This document analyses *layering* — who owns state, network, rendering — and where that ownership breaks down.

---

## 1. The layering as it actually is

The nominal layer order (`api → data → store → lib → hooks → components → app`) is **not** a one-way stack. The dependency graph is a tangle:

| Nominal layer | What it actually is today | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `src/api` | Network clients for subsonic-class servers **plus** a global query-cache orchestration layer (`data.ts` with implicit auth, prefetching, mirroring, offline-queue flushing) **plus** a fully local subsystem (`podcasts.ts`) that talks to SQLite and to the player store | `src/api/data.ts:39` `isOffline()` reads `useAuthStore.getState()`; `:40` `auth()` returns `useAuthStore.getState().auth!`; `:6` imports store/auth; `:27` imports store/offlineQueue; `:31` store/settings; `:32` store/songCache; `:35` `import * as Local from '@/lib/localQueries'`; `src/api/podcasts.ts:12` imports `profileScopeId` from store/auth, `:13` `usePlayerStore` from store/player |
| `src/store` | Zustand singletons = app-wide state (intended), **but** many are mini-engines: file download engine (`downloads.ts` slots/retries), DB catalogs (`downloads.ts:257 getDownloadsCatalog`), feed persistence via `podcasts.ts`, undo-commit machinery (`toast.ts`), plus `player.ts` hosts the whole playback engine | see §2 |
| `src/lib` | Intended: pure helpers. Reality: ~1/3 of lib modules import store singletons (`lib/playRadio.ts:12-14` auth/player/toast; `lib/radioBuild.ts:33-34` radios/settings; `lib/diagnosticsReport.ts:15-21` 7 stores; `lib/navidromeRepair.ts:28-35` 7 stores; `lib/localQueries.ts:8-14` 4 stores; `lib/haptics.ts:15` settings) and lib modules fire **user-facing feedback** (`lib/playRadio.ts:14` toast, `lib/remoteFavorite.ts:10` toast, `lib/playShuffle.ts:12` toast, `lib/favoritesCache.ts:18` `showUndoToast`) |
| `src/hooks` | React/useQuery wrappers — but many **return UI** (`hooks/useGridColumns.tsx:17` imports `SheetModal`, `:54-122` a full memoized JSX component; `hooks/useSelectionMenu.tsx:23-24` imports `Dialog`/`SelectionBar`, `:156-177` returns a `Dialog` node; `useAlbumSort.tsx:18`, `useSongSort.tsx:13`, `useServerSort.tsx:20` import `SortSheet`), and **run business logic** (`useSelectionMenu.tsx:63-81` star/unstar API loop + toasts) |
| `src/components` | UI, mostly right — but several components *are* the business: `components/AudioQualityBadge.tsx:6-11` subscribes 6 stores and re-derives “which file is playing at what quality” (`:30-38`) |
| `src/app` | expo-router screens — but the screens contain persistence (`app/player.tsx:70` imports `@/lib/storage` `getItem/setItem`, `:139` reads `LAYOUT_KEY`, `:617` writes it), init orchestration (`app/_layout.tsx:245-261` hydrates 12 stores, `:276-279` mirror/offlineQueue loads, `:291` prune, `:311 restoreQueue`), business rules (`app/(tabs)/library.tsx` `createPlaylist` + cache invalidation inside screen handler, and `app/podcast/[id].tsx:45-90` background feed re-read logic inside a `queryFn`) |
| `src/theme`, `src/i18n` | Clean. `theme/index.ts` is tokens + `themed()`; no state. |
| `src/stubs` | native module stubs; fine. |

### Modules living in the wrong layer

1. **`src/lib/podcastFavorites.ts:10` `export function usePodcastFavoriteIds`** — a react-query *hook* living in the `lib` layer. Every other useQuery wrapper hook is in `src/hooks`. Importers (`src/app/podcasts.tsx:43`, `src/components/SongMenuSheet.tsx:23`, `src/components/MiniPlayer.tsx:21`, `src/components/PodcastMenuSheet.tsx:39`) all import it from `@/lib/podcastFavorites`. Fix: move to `src/hooks/usePodcastFavoriteIds.ts`; leave `favoriteIds()` (the plain api accessor) in `api/podcasts.ts`.
2. **`src/hooks/useEpisodeUnavailable.ts:17`** exports a *pure rule* `episodeUnavailable(offline, files, id)` beside the subscribing hook, and that pure half is the part consumed by list code — the pure/function half belongs in `lib` (e.g. `lib/availability.ts`), the hook is a thin wrapper. Same split appears inside `hooks/useLyrics.ts:23-40` (`enabledProviders`, `lyricsQueryOptions`, `canFetch` are pure policy used by non-hook callers) and `hooks/useScreenSize.ts:41` `TABLET_WIDTH`, `:77 centredPadding`, `:101 columnsFor` (pure geometry in a “hook” module), and `hooks/useDominantColor.ts:78 toneOf / :140 tintForMode / :187 dominantColorOf / :200 coverColorOf / :260 isPlainTint` — five pure color functions that `lib/radioBuild.ts:31` and `lib/homeWidget.ts:15` import **from the hooks layer**. Fix: one `lib/color.ts` (pure) + a thin `hooks/useDominantColor.ts` over it.
3. **`src/store/mediaMenu.ts:2` `import type Icon from '@/components/Icon'`** — the store layer importing a type from the UI layer. Move `glyphMap` key type (or a plain `icon: string` vocabulary) to `src/theme` or a `lib/iconKeys.ts`; keep only the drawing in components.
4. **`src/store/player.ts:56` `import { prefetchLyrics } from '@/hooks/useLyrics'`** — the store layer calling into the React-hooks layer (`useLyrics.ts:44+` `prefetchLyrics` reads `useAuthStore.getState()` and `queryClient.prefetchQuery`). Neither store nor hooks is the right home: this is *playback-side prefetch policy*; move to `lib/lyricsPrefetch.ts` (or the player module itself) with the query options defined once and shared by both the hook and the prefetch path.
5. **`src/api/podcasts.ts:432 queueEpisode`** calls `usePlayerStore.getState().playQueue(...)` — an api-layer module mutating global playback state. And **`:279 podcastCover`** is a rendering-input helper (“synchronous because whoever draws a cover asks while rendering”). Fix: the api layer should only return `Song` (`episodeToSong` at `:400` is fine); the queueing belongs in a screen/lib glue function; `podcastCover` belongs in `lib` next to cover resolution (`api/data.ts` `coverArtUrl`).
6. **`src/lib/query.ts`** is a *view-cache* registry that hard-codes consumer query keys (`:47 invalidatePodcastLists` knows `['podcastChannels']`, `['podcastRecentEpisodes']`, etc.) and is fired from the api layer (`api/podcasts.ts:16`) and screens (`app/podcast/[id].tsx:36` `invalidatePodcastCaches`). Cache-key strings cross three layers by convention. Fix: one `src/views` module per data family exporting `{key, query, invalidate}`, or at least exported key constants imported (not re-typed) everywhere.

### Circular-import risk (currently latent only, via dynamic imports)

- `src/api/data.ts:1373` uses `await (await import('@/lib/navidromeRepair'))...` and `src/api/subsonic.ts:510` `await import('@/lib/navidromeRepair')` — dynamic imports to *break* a cycle: `api/subsonic → lib/navidromeRepair → store/player (:33 usePlayerStore) → api/backend (:31 in player) → api/subsonic (:10 import * as Subsonic)`. The cycle only survives because Metro/TS evaluation order happens to be stable; any future static edge from those lib/store modules back into `api/data.ts` (which already imports 8 store modules at `data.ts:6-32`) will decide module-init order arbitrarily and can TDZ-crash. Fix: extract repair *orchestration* out of lib: `navidromeRepair` should return data (`Verdict`), and `store/player.ts` should apply it (`remapQueueIds` already exists as that primitive at `player.ts:3183`), so no module needs a dynamic import.

---

## 2. God files — distinct concerns and concrete split boundaries

### 2.1 `src/store/player.ts` — 4 898 LOC, 93 module-level mutable singletons, 47 store actions

Sections (line ranges verified):

| Concern | Lines (current) | Proposed module |
|---|---|---|
| Sleep timer + fade-to-black | `:138-233` (`startSleepFade:176`, `fireSleepTimer:219`) | `store/playerSleep.ts` |
| Dual `AudioPlayer` pool + audio mode | `:234-316` (`players:234`, `ensureAudioMode:287`) | `store/audioPool.ts` |
| Source selection (file/cache/stream/transcode/bitrate) | `:316-569` (`downloadedUri:316`, `streamTargetFor:353`, `sourceFor:424`, `ensureTranscodeOffsetSupport:572`) | `store/playbackSource.ts` |
| Gapless stream offset bookkeeping | `:484-604` (`setStreamOffset:493`, `applyLoop:508`) | same as above |
| Artwork warming / animated clips | `:662-800` (`warmArtwork:691`, `publishAnimatedClip:720`) | `lib/artworkWarm.ts` (this is prefetch side-effect, not state) |
| Lock-screen audio metadata + favorite lock | `:774-910` (`itemMetadataFor:774`, `applyLockScreen:808`, `lockFavoriteSent:826`) | `store/lockScreen.ts` |
| Remote playback (UPnP/Jukebox remote control) | `:911-1179` (`remoteKind:911`, `remotePlay:917`, `bringPlaybackToPhone:939`, `remoteLoadIndex:982`) | already half-owned by `store/upnp.ts` / `store/castMedia.ts` / `store/jukebox.ts` — this remote-control block should move into a `store/remotePlayback.ts` |
| Play history + scrobbling rules | `:1179-1346` (`HISTORY_MAX:1179`, `pushHistory:1202`, `ensurePlaybackReportSupport:1248`, `maybeScrobbleThreshold:1303`) | `store/scrobbling.ts` (today only `store/playCounts.ts`, `store/playHistory.ts`, `store/lastPlayed.ts` exist — the *reporting* rules sit here) |
| Lyrics prefetch + boot quiet period | `:1347-1453` (`NEXT_LYRICS_DELAY_MS:1348`, `startBootQuiet:1367`) | `lib/lyricsPrefetch.ts` (see §1 finding #4) |
| Upcoming-track warming/preload | `:1454-1589` (`cacheUpcoming:1454`, `warmUpcoming:1499`) | `store/playbackWarm.ts` |
| Autoplay / radio candidate generation | `:1590-1893` (`similarArtistCandidates:1626`, `radioCandidates:1667`, `extendWithArtistCatalog:1737`, `fetchAutoplay:1855`) | `store/autoplay.ts` (sister to `lib/radioBuild.ts`, which today *also* builds radios — the two halves of “radio” sit in different layers) |
| Advance: next index, scheduleNext, transitions | `:1894-2091` (`nextIndex:1894`, `scheduleNextSource:1955`, `onTrackTransition:2033`) | keep as the *core* of `player.ts` |
| ReplayGain + speed application | `:2092-2197` (`gainFactor:2108`, `applySpeed:2180`) | `store/playbackAudioFx.ts` |
| Crossfade / handoff / fades | `:2198-2511` (`cutCrossfade:2223`, `handoffToNewSource:2312`, `startCrossfade:2419`) | `store/crossfade.ts` |
| Pause fade + pending seek | `:2512-2591` | same module |
| Stall detection | `:2592-2660` (`STALL_PROBE_MS:2592`, `maybeDetectStall:2599`) | `playbackRecovery.ts` |
| Playback error recovery + background retry | `:2661-2877` (`gaveUp:2668`, `bgRetryRound:2714`, `tryResumeAfterOutage:2847`) | same |
| Status/position/duration handling | `:2878-3062` (`onStatus:2905`, `handleSleepAtSongEnd:3063`) | core |
| Queue persistence + away marker + adopt-server-queue | `:3063-3366` (`safeKey:3089`, `queueStorageKey:3094`, `saveQueueLocal:3193`, `checkBackgroundDeath:3307`, `adoptNewerServerQueue:3333`) | `store/queuePersistence.ts` |
| Server-queue sync timers | `:3367-3623` (`syncQueueNow:3435`, `periodicSync:3500`, `initRemoteIntegration:3624`) | same as queuePersistence or `remotePlayback` |
| `PlayerState` interface (`:3780-3996`) + zustand singleton (`:3997-4898`, 47 actions) | | keep as the assembly file |

Why it matters: with ~93 module-level `let`s (`warmedIds:1493`, `stallSince:2594`, `gaveUp:2668`, `queuedNext:1917`, `radioRound:1775`, …) there is no unit that can be tested, reset, or ported; `reset()` drift already bites (documented at `player.ts:4777`-area; `warmedIds` survives a profile switch and silently skips prewarming). Splitting by concern turns the enumeration into “call every module’s `clear()`”.

Also inside: **`queueStorageKey():3094`** asks `if (offline) return 'resonus.queue.offline'` *before* the profile — every account’s offline listening lands in one shared queue bucket, while `store/playHistory.ts` documents the opposite rule (“asking about the mode first put every account’s offline listening into one shared bucket”) and `auth.ts:206 profileScopeId()` is the canonical identity. `store/libraries.ts:23 profileKeyOf()` is a byte-twin of `profileScopeId` (null for Jellyfin). Three identity implementations, two of them wrong; one helper in `auth.ts` + capability checks is the fix.

### 2.2 `src/store/settings.ts` — 2 310 LOC

Concerns: (a) setting *vocabulary*: ~860 lines of exported types/options/labels/defaults (`ACCENT_OPTIONS:36`, `BITRATE_OPTIONS:77`, `GRID_DEFAULT_COLUMNS:197`, `HOME_CHIP_KEYS:525`, `DEFAULT_HOME_BUTTONS:676`, `APP_FONT_FAMILY:836` — a `Platform.select` theme mapping inside the settings store…); (b) normalize/deserialize functions (`normalizeExploreSections:467`, `normalizeHomeSections:489`, `normalizeBottomTabs:630`, `normalizeLyricsProviders:730`…); (c) persistence codec (`persist:1348`, `snapshot:1357`, `DEFAULTS:1365`, `PERSISTED_KEYS:1546`); (d) profile/reaction logic (`applyChosenAccent:1570`, `applyFactoryLook:1582`, `applySaved:1596` — a ~400-line field-by-field loader); (e) the singleton (`:2016`) with dozens of hand-written `setX: (v) => { set({v}); persist(snapshot(get)); }` setters; (f) export/import protocol (`exportedSettings:2300`).

Concrete split: `store/settingsVocab/<domain>.ts` (playback, library, look, home/explore, lyrics — each owning types + option tables + defaults + normalize); `store/settingsPersist.ts` (snapshot/applySaved/persist with the profileScopeGuard ownership check); one `store/settings.ts` assembly that creates the singleton and delegates setters (or a `defineSettings` factory generating the `set* + persist` boilerplate). Why it matters: adding one preference today means touching the same 2.3k-line file in 3–5 distant places (type, default, options table, normalize, setter), and `applySaved` re-enumerates fields the `DEFAULTS` already lists — silent loss when one copy drifts.

### 2.3 `src/api/data.ts` — 2 125 LOC

Concerns: (a) implicit singleton access `isOffline():39`, `auth():40` (the “api layer with hidden global auth” anti-pattern — every other api module threads `auth` explicitly, cf. `api/backend.ts:6` doc); (b) catalog/mirror resolution (`loadMirror:90`, `resolveSongs:118`); (c) cover-URL policy (`coverArtUrl:181`, `songCoverUrl:235`); (d) server vocabulary translation (`ND_ALBUM_LIST:288`, `ND_ALBUM_SORT:413`, `ND_SORT:525`); (e) the multi-library dispatch ladder, hand-written ~10× (`ids.length === 1` at `:305, :375, :487, :647, :806, :889, :921, :950, :1212`); (f) derived list synthesis (`derivedSongList:567`); (g) prefetch schedulers ×2 (`prefetchStarredAlbums:1005`, `prefetchPlaylistDetails:1059`, parallel flag sets `:1001-1003` / `:1031-1051`); (h) offline-queue flush (`flushOfflineQueue:1334`, `flushOutbox:1348`) — mutating *store* state from api; (i) library-size cache (`loadLibrarySizes:1870`); (j) merge helpers (`mergeAlbums:2037`, `mergedAlbumPage:2081`). Split: `data/merge.ts`, `data/prefetch.ts`, `data/offlineFlush.ts` (→ store), `data/covers.ts`, `data/sortVocab.ts`, `api/data.ts` keeps one-liner list entry points via a `perLibrary(auth, fetchOne, merge)` combinator.

### 2.4 `src/app/player.tsx` — 1 928 LOC

One 1 460-line `PlayerScreen()` function (`:265-1730`) plus inline helpers (`CircleButton:161`, `SleepButton:199`) plus a 200-line style block (`:1731`). Distinct concerns inside the function: cover-viewer pane + animated/GIF backgrounds (`:407-458` area), gradient theming, lyrics/artist card sizing (`:366-378`), gesture handling (tap/double-tap/swipe thresholds `:118-127`), **screen-owned persistence** (`:70` `getItem/setItem` from `@/lib/storage`, `:138 LAYOUT_KEY`, `:139` module-level read, `:617` write of measured layout), **playback math in the screen** (`:1602` `Math.max(0, positionSec - seekButtonsSec)` and `:1660-1661` end-cap + `positionSec + seekButtonsSec` — duplicating clamp rules the `store/player.ts` `seekTo` already owns for offset reasons; `stopAndClear().then(undo)` toast dance at `:1629`), star/rate plumbing (`:799-823` imperatively using `usePlayerStore.getState()` and `useToast.getState()`), `outputOpen` / `atTop` / `spins` screen state. Split: `components/PlayerCover.tsx` (background/animated cover), `components/PlayerHeader.tsx`, `components/PlayerControls.tsx` (transport buttons), keep a slim `app/player.tsx`; move layout memo (`playerLayout` blob) to a `store/layoutMemo.ts` or `lib/measured.ts`; add a `store/player.ts` action `seekBy(sec)` that owns the ±n-seconds clamp so the screen just calls it.

### 2.5 `src/components/TrackListView.tsx` — 1 546 LOC

`TrackListView` (`:229`) takes **35 props** (verified prop block `:229-266`: `favorite`, `podcastFavIds`, `download`, `selection`, `onSort`, `searchable`, …) — it is one component answering: header/cover-viewer, gradient tint (`useCoverTint:34`), collapse animation, search box (`:803-823` + style `:1311`), play/shuffle affordances, selection menu integration, sort menu integration, FlatList row rendering, plus `DiscHeader` (`:1271`) and a ~270-line styles block. Props like `favorite` (boolean), `podcastFavIds: Set`, `download` (store accessor?) are *raw composited values* — the component still decides heart semantics. Split: `TrackListHeader` (cover + title + actions), `TrackListSearchBar` (dedupe with browse screens, see §4), `TrackListRows` (rows + disc headers), selection/sort integration moved to a `useTrackListMenus` hook or composed props bundle (`{menu, selection}` objects instead of 35 flat props — TypeScript `Props` object helps but the boundary is the issue: 5 screens pass different subsets).

### 2.6 `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx` — 1 614 LOC

Five tab components + a screen body: `PlaylistsTab:265`, `ArtistsTab:385`, `PodcastsTab:460`, `NoResults:640`, `AlbumsTab:651`, `AllTab:766`, plus `Loader:1031`, `LayoutToggle:1036`, `GridCard:1054`, `gridListProps:1118`, `LibraryScreen:1133` (~320-line body with 8 `useState`: `segment`, `owner`, `creating`, `sortOpen`, `searchOpen`, `query`, `chipHeight` + BackHandler/`useFocusEffect` search-closing machinery at `:1180-1230`), and screen-level business: `onCreate` `:1264` calls `createPlaylist` + `queryClient.invalidateQueries({queryKey:['playlists']})` + toast — creating a playlist (api call, cache-key string, user feedback) inside a screen. Also duplicates grid math: `useGridMetrics():138` (`columnsFor(width, CARD_IDEAL, 3, 6)`) vs `hooks/useGridColumns.tsx` used by the browse screens. Split: one `components/LibrarySegment.tsx` per data kind (or a registry keyed by segment with `{label, icon, query, render}`), move create-playlist dialog business into `store/playlists.ts` action or `api` glue + one `dialogs.ts` helper; reuse `useGridColumns`.

### 2.7 `src/app/(tabs)/index.tsx` — 1 315 LOC (Home)

14 inline components in the screen file: `QuickTile:103`, `QuickGrid:134`, `SectionHeader:294`, `AlbumSection:313`, `SongListCarousel:374`, `MostPlayedSongsSection:449`, `RandomSongsSection:483`, `PlaylistsSection:512`, `PodcastEpisodesSection:548`, `shuffled:592` (another Fisher–Yates copy), `ArtistSection:617`, `DiscoverSection:677`, `RadiosSection:728`, `CHIPS table:784`, `HomeChips:811`, `ScanningPanel:880`, `HomeHeaderButton:958`, `HomeScreen:996`. Home owns section *data-fetching composition* (`useQuery` per section, paging constants `MOST_PLAYED_SONGS:355`, `SONG_PAGE_SIZE:358`, `SONG_PAGE_MAX:364`, `RANDOM_SONGS_COUNT:477`, `DISCOVER_OFFSET:674`, `DISCOVER_POOL:675`, `ARTISTS_DELAY_MS:614`). Fix: `home/sections.ts` — a table of `{key, section: (opts) => Component}`; move each section to `components/Home<X>Section.tsx`; the HomeScreen becomes a ~120-line composition driven by `settings.homeSections`.

### 2.8 `src/app/(tabs)/explore.tsx` — 1 315 LOC, **one 1 250-line function**

`ExploreScreen:68` contains everything: availability predicates per section (`available:108` with `serverType !== 'jellyfin'` at `:107`), layouts map `:90`, per-section inline JSX (search box again, headers, chips), plus its own BackHandler-style focus machinery. There is no section abstraction at all — worse structural offender than Home. Split like §2.7 with a shared `BrowseSectionsRegistry` reused by Home/Explore/Library (they render the same data kinds: albums, artists, songs, playlists, genres, radios, podcasts).

### 2.9 `src/store/downloads.ts` — 1 674 LOC

Concerns: podcast download scope/dirs (`PODCAST_ROOT:83`, `podcastScope:85`), concurrency slot engine (`slotsInUse:101`, `takeSlot:109`), retry engine (`ATTEMPTS:128`, `worthRetrying:132`), catalog cache orchestration + hydration-race guard (`hydrateRun:215`), catalog builders (`getDownloadsCatalog:257`, `deriveArtists:223`), mirror note-down (`noteDownloadedArtist:342`, `noteDownloadedAlbum:416`), cover sweep maintenance (`sweepTrackCovers:446`), file URL/format mapping (`FORMAT_EXT:540`, `fileUrlFor:557`), local song/album conversion (`toLocalSong:574`), lyrics caching for downloads (`cacheLyricsForDownload:731`), size measurement (`measureMissing:762`), art downloads (`:793-863`), then the zustand singleton (`DownloadsState:864`). Split: `store/downloadsEngine.ts` (download/retry/slots), `store/downloadedCatalog.ts` (catalog + shelf cache + `localQueries` interplay), `store/downloadsMirror.ts` (mirror notifications + cover sweeps), keep singleton thin.

### 2.10 `src/api/subsonic.ts` — 1 968 LOC

Vocabulary (types `Song:86`…`Starred:1304` ~350 lines), auth/salt/encoding (`makeAuth:386`, `hexEncodeUtf8:360`), transport (`buildUrl:430`, `request<T>:525`, `retryWithCanonicalId:502` with its dynamic repair import), endpoint wrappers, playlist/playqueue ops. Acceptable as a backend client, but: types should live in a `src/api/vocab.ts` (they’re imported as `@/api/subsonic` types by ~40 modules and mutated by nobody — `data.ts:164-166` already re-exports them), and `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS:442` duplicates `jellyfin.ts:68`.

---

## 3. Coupling smells (state, prefs, feedback)

1. **Screens → store internals is the norm and mostly acceptable** (zustand singletons as the app-state layer), **but** the *imperative* reads are the smell: 73 occurrences of `X.getState()` in `src/app`, e.g. `app/player.tsx:799` `const current = usePlayerStore.getState()` and `:808/:810/:820/:823` calling `useToast.getState().show(...)` inside press handlers, `app/queue.tsx:418-421` `usePlaylistPicker.getState().open(server)`, `app/artist/[id].tsx:393`. Business sequences (get state → call API → toast → invalidate) are assembled *in press handlers*. Fix: named store actions (`favoriteSong(id)`, `createPlaylistNamed(name)`) so handlers are one-liners.
2. **Store → UI concerns:** `store/toast.ts` is not just state — the undo machinery `pendingCommit` (`:16-56` `showUndoToast`) implements deferred destructive commits with UI-timing semantics (“when the toast expires or another replaces it”); `store/player.ts:4029`-area `playQueue` calls `useToast.getState().show(tg('Nothing here is downloaded'))` — a *user-facing message with translation lookup* inside the state layer, and lib modules depend on it for feedback (`lib/favoritesCache.ts:18` `showUndoToast`). Fix: store actions return verdicts (`{ok:false, why:'nothingDownloaded'}`), rendering + i18n + toasts belong to `components/Toast.tsx` + a thin feedback glue module.
3. **lib → store singletons** (the engine layer depending on the state layer): 15 lib modules import stores (§1). Consequence: these “lib” functions are untestable without the whole app state and can be called from anywhere at any time. The clean fix: lib takes inputs (`auth`, `offline`, `files`) and returns data — cf. `lib/podcastFeed.ts` and `lib/ttml.ts` which are genuinely pure.
4. **hooks → components:** three sort hooks + selection-menu + grid-columns hooks *render or return components* (`useAlbumSort.tsx:18 SortSheet`, `useGridColumns.tsx:17 SheetModal` + full `GridSheet:54-122` component). A `useXxx` that returns `{node: ReactNode}` hides a component behind a function-call API and breaks the “hooks = state/derivation, components = UI” contract this codebase otherwise respects (`hooks/useDebounce.ts` is pure derivation; good). Split rule: hooks return `{value, open, set, menu: ComponentProps}`; components mount the sheet.
5. **components taking raw prefs instead of composed values:** `components/AudioQualityBadge.tsx:14-40` subscribes `settings.maxBitRate/maxBitRateCellular/streamFormat/streamFormatCellular/streamLosslessOnly/preferDownloads` + `downloads.files/dlBitRates` + `songCache.entries` + `networkType.cellular` + `auth.offline` + `player.localSourceFor` and re-implements the “what is actually playing” rule (`:31-33`) already owned by the player (`localSourceFor` comment at `:24-27` admits the rule belongs to the player). `components/TrackRow.tsx:22` reads `settings.swipeAction` and re-resolves swipe semantics; `components/LyricsCard.tsx:55` reads 5 lyrics prefs. Fix: a `store/playbackQuality.ts` (or player field) exposing one composed `{badgeLabel, source}` value, and a `useSwipes` composed value, so components read one composed fact and the app has exactly one place where “download wins over cache over stream” is decided.
6. **Duplicate state between store and screens:** `app/player.tsx` keeps measured layout state (`pageH:508`, `coverBoxH:518`, `coverStable:554`) persisted to disk under its own key (`:138 LAYOUT_KEY`, written `:617`) — persistence + screen-owned state; `app/(tabs)/library.tsx` persists its own search/segment state purely screen-side (session-only but re-typed per screen: three screens re-implement the selection state machine — `app/genre/[name].tsx:177`, `app/browse/songs.tsx:221`, `app/artist/songs/[id].tsx:171` all declare `justLongPressed = useRef` + `toggleSelect` + `runSelection` verbatim; TrackListView owns a *fourth* copy via `useSelectionMenu`). Fix: one `useSelection(shown)` hook/component used by all four call sites.
7. **i18n inside state:** `store/player.ts:63` imports `tg` and composes translatable strings inside a store action (`Nothing here is downloaded` in `playQueue`); `store/settings.ts` similarly ships user-facing copy. The i18n boundary should stop at `app/`+`components/`.
8. **Prefs vocabulary as UI keys in hooks:** `hooks/useBrowseSort.ts:28` casts browse-order strings into `SortField`-typed prefs (namespace collision documented in hygiene-review; still present at current code).
9. **Capability checks by string compare** still 10+ sites: `explore.tsx:107` `auth.serverType !== 'jellyfin'`, plus the doc-verified set in sheets/screens; `api/backend.ts:261 supportsPlaybackReport` / `:333 supportsTranscodeOffset` exist but unused everywhere else. One `can.*` module in `api/backend.ts` (imported by screens *and* store) is the concrete fix; today the same fact is expressed as string comparisons in screens and as predicates in backend.
10. **Store → lib → store cycles:** `store/player.ts:84 imports lib/radioBuild` which at `:33` imports `store/radios` and at `:31` `hooks/useDominantColor`; radio state thus round-trips store→lib→store→hooks→api/data (which at `:6` imports `store/auth`). Legal at runtime but nobody can reason about “which module owns radio”. Fix: `store/radios.ts` owns defs, `lib/radioBuild.ts` stays pure (takes `RadioDef[]` in), the *fetch* part goes to `api/data`-level; hooks only subscribe.

---

## 4. `src/components` — boundary check, duplication

The component *set* is right (rows, sheets, skeletons, browse frame); the duplication is in the **bottom-sheet family** and the **search-box bar**, and several components do 2–3 jobs at once.

1. **Seven sheets hand-roll SheetModal’s scaffold**: `ArtistPickerSheet.tsx:57-100`, `ColorPickerDialog.tsx:38-87`, `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:228-473`, `OutputSheet.tsx:289-429`, `PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:160-218`, `ShareSheet.tsx:156-228`, `SongInfoSheet.tsx:218-…`, `SongMenuSheet.tsx:371-…` each re-declare `GestureHandlerRootView style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}` + `backdrop: { ...StyleSheet.absoluteFill, backgroundColor: colors.backdrop }` (`ArtistPickerSheet.tsx:106`, `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:541`, `OutputSheet.tsx:435`, `PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:291`, `ShareSheet.tsx:244`, `SongInfoSheet.tsx:293`) and use `useBottomSheetAnim`’s raw `backdropStyle/sheetStyle` — while `SheetModal.tsx:20/64` already wraps exactly this (`useGridColumns.tsx:91` uses `SheetModal` correctly, proving the abstraction is available). ~350 duplicated lines. Fix: export `SheetModal` as the composition base for all bottom sheets.
2. **`useBottomSheetAnim` writes the closing animation twice** — `dismiss` at `:44` and the gesture `onEnd` at `:81` re-implement `progress.value = withTiming(0, TIMING_OUT, (f) => { if (f) scheduleOnRN(after) })`. Fix: internal `closeSheet(fn)`.
3. **`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:42 function Action({icon,label,onPress})` ≡ `SongMenuSheet.tsx:105`** — same component twice; `RadioMenuSheet`, `SelectionBar.tsx:109`, `OutputSheet.tsx:49` are variants. One `MenuAction` shared component (fixes the magic `ACTION_H` at `SongMenuSheet.tsx:71` too).
4. **Search box is hand-drawn 9×**: `browse/albums.tsx:222-257` (+style `:360`), `browse/artists.tsx:255-288` (`:359`), `browse/songs.tsx:327` (`:565`), `radio.tsx:194` (`:402`), `genres.tsx:99` (`:165`), `podcasts.tsx:201` (`:375`), `PlaylistsBrowser.tsx:179` (`:227`), `TrackListView.tsx:818` (`:1320`), `PodcastAddSheet.tsx:95` — identical `TextInput` (`autoCapitalize="none"`, `returnKeyType="search"`) + search icon + clear `Pressable`, and `height: 44` per file. Fix: `components/SearchBox.tsx` owning role/label/a11y and a `browseStyles` token module.
5. **Download `Dialog` quartet written in 8 places**: `album/[id].tsx:350` + `:362`, `playlist/[id].tsx:624/670/683`, `artist/[id].tsx:804/816`, `genre/[name].tsx:691`, `favorites.tsx:306-357` (two near-identical “Remove download?” dialogs `:306` filtered vs `:334` unfiltered — a real divergence), `artist/songs/[id].tsx:454`, plus `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:518`, `SongMenuSheet.tsx:811`. Fix: one `confirmDownloadDialog(name, onYes)` helper module next to `Dialog.tsx`.
6. **Components doing multiple jobs:** `TrackListView` (§2.5, 35 props, 4 jobs); `SettingsUI.tsx` (951 lines = settings-screen *protocol* + style tokens + row/field/list widgets — fine as the protocol module, but the reorder-list widgets from §6 belong inside it, not in screens); `KaraokeWords.tsx:299-348` base/mask double-render (doc-verified, still present); `LyricsCard.tsx` mixes lyric layout + background theming + store subscriptions (`:48` player, `:55` 5 prefs).
7. **Theme hygiene leaks** (still present): `shadowColor: '#000'` (`GenreCard.tsx`, `TrackListView.tsx:1371`), `MiniPlayer.tsx:305` hardcoded `boxShadow`, `GlobalTabBar.tsx:62-73` rgba ramps, `gap: 4`/`marginTop: 2` literals (no `spacing.xxs` in `theme/index.ts` scale — `xs: 4` floor). One `elevated(level)` themed helper + `spacing.xxs`.
8. **Press feedback**: inline `pressed && { opacity: … }` appears **82×** across `src/components` while `hooks/usePressFeedback.ts` is used ~4×; values drift (0.6/0.7/0.85 vs `PRESSED = 0.62` defined in the hook). Fix: make every pressable use the hook or one `motion.pressOpacity` token.
9. **Unduplicated, correct abstractions to note**: `BrowseFrame.tsx` (shared by browse screens + `podcasts.tsx:29` uses it — good), skeletons share nothing but each copy-pastes the same pulse (`AlbumRowsSkeleton.tsx:21`, `ArtistGridSkeleton.tsx:21`, `GenreGridSkeleton.tsx:22`, `TrackListSkeleton.tsx:33`) → one `useSkeletonPulse()` hook.

---

## 5. `src/hooks` — abstraction verdict & duplicates

Verdict: **inconsistent as a layer.** The pure derivation hooks (`useDebounce.ts:4`, `useInsets.ts:13`, `useScreenSize.ts` (mixed with pure exports), `useFavoriteIds.ts`, `useHistoryTimes.ts`) are a good React abstraction — memoized derivation from store state, no UI. The **UI-returning hooks** (`useSelectionMenu.tsx`, `useGridColumns.tsx`, `useAlbumSort.tsx`, `useSongSort.tsx`, `useServerSort.tsx`) are not hooks anymore in spirit: they return sheets and run API business. The codebase treats them as “hook-per-concern”, and hygiene-review says the project “wants” that — but then the contract should be “hooks derive values and *menu props*, components mount them” (see §3.4 fix), or these five move to `components/` with an `XMenu` name.

Duplicate families:
- **Four sort hooks** (`useAlbumSort.tsx`, `useSongSort.tsx`, `useServerSort.tsx`, `useBrowseSort.ts`) share ~30 lines of identical plumbing (`useSortPrefs` read, `setPref`, `useState` fallback, `openRef`, `useMemo`, `<SortSheet options…/>`) and carry **three overlapping `SORT_LABEL` tables** (`useAlbumSort.tsx:21`, `useSongSort.tsx:23`, `useServerSort.tsx:31` — `alpha`→'Alphabetical' ×3). Fix: one `useSortMenu(persistKey, fields, fallback)` + one label table in `store/sortPrefs.ts` (which already owns the field types).
- **`useGenreArt.ts:17`** hand-copies `createSlots` from `lib/slots.ts` (`let inFlight` + `waiting` acquire/release verbatim; `subsonic.ts:455` already uses `createSlots(2)`). Delete 21 lines.
- **`useTabBar.ts:54 COMPACT_BELOW = 500` + `:58 useWindowDimensions().height < COMPACT_BELOW`** re-implements `useScreenSize().short` (`useScreenSize.ts:57`), rationale comment written twice.
- **`useScreenBottomPadding.ts:17` and `:33`** compute the identical base (`withBar ? barHeight + gap + insets.bottom : …`), differing only in the tail — and the comment says the calc mirrors a *third* copy in `GlobalMiniPlayer`. One `miniPlayerBottom()` internal.
- **`useDominantColor.ts` (7 functions) and `useAnimatedArtwork.ts` vs `useAnimatedCover.ts`** — the latter two are unrelated features sharing a name (fetch vs sniff); rename, don’t merge. `dominantColorOf/coverColorOf` (pure, async) should move to `lib/color.ts` (§1.2).
- **`useServerCover.ts`, `usePlaylistStars.ts`, `useFavoriteIds.ts`, `useCanShare.ts`** are thin wrappers over `api/navidrome.ts`/`api/data.ts` — correct pattern, keep; but `useServerCover.ts:102` gates on `auth.ndPassword` where every other native-API entry accepts `password` too (identity drift, still present).
- **`usePodcastFavoriteIds` living in lib** (§1.1) is a duplicate *placement* of an otherwise-correct hook pattern (`useFavoriteIds.ts`).

---

## 6. `src/app/(tabs)` + settings structure

**Settings protocol exists**: `components/SettingsUI.tsx` exports `SettingsPage`, `SettingsSafeArea`, `ScreenHeader`, `settingsStyles`, `SettingRow`, `Field`, `SelectList`, `SwitchList`, `SliderRow`, `TextRow`. Compliance:
- 14 screens use `SettingsPage` (`about.tsx:80`, `diagnostics.tsx:53`, `downloads.tsx:154`, `equalizer.tsx:57/68`, `font.tsx:64`, `greeting.tsx:26`, `language.tsx:34`, `library.tsx:147`, `network.tsx:88`, `personalization.tsx:84`, `playback.tsx:90`, `player.tsx:129`, `quick-grid.tsx`, `song-lists.tsx`).
- **8 bypass it** with raw `SettingsSafeArea` + own header/scroll: `home-buttons.tsx:90`, `home-chips.tsx:106`, `home-sections.tsx:100`, `navigation-bar.tsx:85`, `explore-sections.tsx:111`, `player-buttons.tsx:86`, `lyrics-providers.tsx:75`, `settings/index.tsx:110` (with a comment admitting the split at `:113`). These are the “reorderable list” screens — the protocol gap is exactly a missing `ReorderableSettingsList` in SettingsUI: **7 screens duplicate the drag row verbatim** (`home-chips.tsx:61-68`, `home-sections.tsx:58-65`, `home-buttons.tsx:55-62`, `navigation-bar.tsx:51-58`, `explore-sections.tsx:68-75`, `player-buttons.tsx:49-56`, `lyrics-providers.tsx:42-49`: `onPressIn → haptic('medium'); drag();` + `reorder-two` icon + identical splice in `onReorder` + identical `hint/list/row/label` styles).
- **Protocol respected on the state side, not on the UI side**: settings *values* all live in `store/settings.ts` (screens call `setX` — good); but **the valid-value tables leak**: `settings/quick-grid.tsx:13 const SIZES = [4, 6, 8]` is the only settings option list not exported from `store/settings` (neighbours: `RADIO_COUNT_OPTIONS: radios.tsx:21` imports from store, `SONG_CACHE_LIMITS_GB` from settings.ts:118, `BITRATE_OPTIONS`).
- Settings tab aliasing is clean: `src/app/(tabs)/options.tsx` is 9 lines re-exporting `SettingsScreen` with `asTab` — good example of cross-layer reuse.
- `(tabs)/_layout.tsx` and `GlobalTabBar` split the tab vocabulary: labels in `_layout.tsx:23 TAB_OPTIONS` vs bar icons/labels in `GlobalTabBar` vs `settings/navigation-bar.tsx` chips vs `home-chips.tsx:35` “the same ones Home draws” — one label/icon registry keyed by `TabSegment` (already a `lib/tabOrigin.ts` vocabulary) is the fix.
- Home (`(tabs)/index.tsx`) and Explore (`(tabs)/explore.tsx`) each grow 1 300-line inline section ecosystems over the same data kinds and the same browse screens (`explore.tsx:38-42` imports `AlbumsBrowser/ArtistsBrowser/SongsBrowser/GenresBrowser/RadioBrowser` — the one genuinely shared-screen abstraction; Home instead re-fetches per section). A shared `sectionRegistry` consumed by Home/Explore/Library/search would collapse §2.7–2.8 and the Library tabs (§2.6).

**Init protocol** (`app/_layout.tsx:223-330`): the root screen hand-wires a hydration DAG over ~15 stores (`hydrate()` at `:245-252`, `:291 prune`, `:311 restoreQueue`) with `useEffect` dependency choreography and comments. Adding a store means editing this orchestration. Fix: a `store/bootstrap.ts` exporting `hydrateAll(ready)` with declared dependency edges, so `_layout` calls one thing; today the knowledge of “which store loads after which” lives in an `app/` file — the same kind of misplacement as §1 (app layer knowing store internals).

**Podcast subsystem (PR #273) specifically:**
- `api/podcasts.ts` is now netGate-disciplined (`:98 assertCanRequest` inside `get()`; `refreshAll:368` loops through it) — the old hygiene-review hole is fixed. But the module is *not an api module*: half of it is DB/persistence orchestration (`refreshChannel:196` writes channel + episodes + art + invalidations), half state-coupling (`queueEpisode:432`), half rendering inputs (`podcastCover:279`). Suggested boundary: `api/podcasts.ts` = feed fetch + parse (pure-ish); `store/podcasts.ts` = subscriptions/hearts/channels persistence (mirroring how `store/libraryMirror` + `lib/mirrorDb` pair music state); `queueEpisode` into `store/player` glue.
- `app/podcasts.tsx:201` re-draws the search bar (dup §4.4); `:118-133` the refresh-all cancellation flag is screen-owned (`const stop = useRef`), with `refreshAll`’s business loop called from the screen — store/action home (`store/podcasts.ts` or singleton action) so cancel survives navigation.
- `app/podcast/[id].tsx:45-90`: staleness rule (`STALE_MS:30`, 72 h) + background re-read + `invalidatePodcastCaches` inside a screen `queryFn` — the same staleness rule also exists conceptually in `refreshChannel` docs; one `podcastStaleness` constant/module.
- `components/PodcastMenuSheet.tsx:48` exports an imperative `openPodcastMenu` backed by a **module-level mutable singleton** (`let openMenu`) consumed from 5 screens (`podcasts.tsx:305/334`, `podcast/[id].tsx:194`, `library.tsx:562/901`, `search.tsx:514`) — while the song equivalent uses a proper store singleton (`store/songMenu`). Two mechanisms for the same “open this sheet from anywhere” protocol; unify via `store/mediaMenu.ts`-style picker (which itself imports `Icon` from components, §1.3).
- `PodcastRow.tsx:26` and `PodcastEpisodeCard.tsx:10` both consume `useEpisodeUnavailable` (good dedup), but both sheets also pull raw prefs (`useSettings` reads in `PodcastMenuSheet`, `PodcastAddSheet.tsx:41` debounce 400 ms vs the `useDebounce` hook default 350 used elsewhere — a third debounce delay in the app).
- `usePodcastFavoriteIds` in `lib/podcastFavorites.ts:10` (§1.1) — the single most visible layer slip of PR #273.
- `store/downloads.ts:83 PODCAST_ROOT` + `podcastScope():85`: podcast episode downloads were bolted onto the music downloads store (shared `slotsInUse` concurrency + retry engine with podcast scope dirs) — acceptable coupling but the DB-side (`lib/podcastDb.ts:63` handle cache, 5th copy of the SQLite opener shared by `localDb.ts:165`, `downloadsDb.ts:104`, `mirrorDb.ts:94`, `songCacheDb.ts:44`) confirms the known `sqliteDb.ts` extraction (hygiene doc §2d) as the concrete fix.
- `lib/query.ts:39-57`: podcast cache-key strings hand-duplicated (`invalidatePodcastLists` lists four literal keys, mirrored in `podcasts.tsx:94-96`, `podcast/[id].tsx`, `search.tsx`, `library.tsx` query calls) — exported key constants (or a `views/podcasts.ts` owner) kill the string-triplication.

---

## 7. Top boundary problems (ranked, concrete)

1. `hooks/useSelectionMenu.tsx:54-83,156-177` — hook running API business + returning Dialog JSX → move actions to `store/favorites.ts` actions + `components/SelectionDialogs`.
2. `api/podcasts.ts:13,432` + `api/data.ts:6,39,40` — api layer holding hidden store state; split network clients (pure `auth` args) from a `data/` orchestration layer that may read stores.
3. `store/player.ts` (4 898 LOC, 93 singletons) — split per §2.1 (15 modules).
4. `hooks → components` JSX (`useGridColumns.tsx:54-122`, three sort hooks) — hooks must return props, not nodes; components mount.
5. Settings protocol gap: 7 screens duplicate the drag row (`home-chips.tsx:61` etc.) → `ReorderableSettingsList` in `SettingsUI.tsx`.
6. `lib → store` reverse deps (15 modules) → lib takes inputs, returns data; store or `views/` glue wires it.
7. Search box duplicated 9× (§4.4) → `components/SearchBox.tsx` + shared style module.
8. Bottom-sheet family re-implements `SheetModal` (§4.1, ~350 lines) → all sheets compose on `SheetModal.tsx:20`.
9. Download `Dialog` quartet in 8 places + divergent delete paths (`favorites.tsx:306` vs `:334`) → `confirmDeleteDialog` helper + one delete path.
10. `store/settings.ts` 2 310 LOC, one preference = 3–5 edits (§2.2) → vocabulary/persist/singleton split + generated setters.
11. `app/_layout.tsx:245-311` hydration DAG inside the root screen (§6 init protocol) → `store/bootstrap.ts`.
12. Home/Explore/Library re-implement section ecosystems over the same data (§2.7, §2.8, §2.6) → shared section registry.
13. Identity rules triplicated: `libraries.ts:23`, `auth.ts:206`, `player.ts:3094` (+ `playCounts.ts` unpartitioned key) → one `profileScopeId`-derived key helper + capability checks.
14. Selection state machine 4× (`genre/[name].tsx:177`, `browse/songs.tsx:221`, `artist/songs/[id].tsx:171`, TrackListView) → `useSelection(shown)`; press feedback 82 inline vs `usePressFeedback` (0.62 vs 0.6/0.7/0.85) → one token.
15. Seek-by-n clamp in screen (`player.tsx:1602,1660`) → `store/player.ts` `seekBy(sec)` action; `app/player.tsx:70/138/617` disk persistence in a screen → `store/layoutMemo` or `lib/measured`.

Honourable mentions: `hooks/useGenreArt.ts:17` slots copy (`lib/slots.ts:6` exists); `useTabBar.ts:54` vs `useScreenSize().short`; `useScreenBottomPadding.ts:17/33` twin; `SORT_LABEL` ×3; `quick-grid.tsx:13 SIZES` off-protocol; `lib/podcastFavorites.ts:10` hook-in-lib; `store/mediaMenu.ts:2` Icon type import; dynamic `import('@/lib/navidromeRepair')` cycle hatch (`data.ts:1373`, `subsonic.ts:510`); `toast.ts` undo machinery + i18n in store layer; five SQLite handle caches (`podcastDb.ts:63` et al.); magic chunk sizes (`downloadsDb.ts:594`) vs `PARAM_CHUNK`.


---

# PART 4 — Dead & removable code — full evidence & method (basis of Part 1)

# Resonus dead-code hunt — branch main @ b1075627 (incl. podcast PR #273)

Method notes at the end. Every claim was grep-verified across the WHOLE repo
(`src/`, `test/`, `scripts/`, `modules/`, `patches/`, `plugins/`, `android/`
source, `ios-widget/`, `app.json`, `metro.config.js`, and — for framework
consumers — `node_modules/expo-*`).

---

## 1. Dead exports (never imported/referenced anywhere)

### 1a. Fully dead code — zero references anywhere, even in their own file (CERTAIN, delete)

| Item | Location | How verified dead | Delete |
|---|---|---|---|
| `artistsLabel` | `src/i18n/index.ts:129` (doc line 127) | word-search `artistsLabel` across src+test+scripts+modules → 1 hit (the declaration). `countLabel('artist',…)` has no other caller, so cascades: `PLURALS.artist` forms table `src/i18n/index.ts:62` (9 languages × forms) becomes unused once removed (grep `countLabel(` shows callers only from the other wrappers) | delete the function **and** the `artist:` entry at :62 |

plugin list + config, `modules/` (local native modules), `android/` source,
`ios-widget/`, `patches/`, and node_modules internal importers.

  0 references in src/modules/test/scripts/android-src/ios-widget; 0 importers
  in all of node_modules (searched `expo-router`, `@expo/router-server`,
  everything). Its only appearance in `app.json` is the bare plugin entry
  (line: `"expo-web-browser"`) **with no options** — a no-op config. Delete the
  package.json dep AND the app.json plugin entry. Linking/openURL in the app
  goes through `react-native`'s `Linking` (`PodcastMenuSheet.tsx:16`) — not this.

  carries it as a configured plugin (backgroundColor/splash image) consumed by
  `@expo/cli` prebuild and `@expo/fingerprint` sourcer (grep hits) — removing it
  changes fingerprinting/launch config. Keep.
- All of these have zero *direct* app imports but are framework consumers — keep:
  `expo-asset` (imported by `expo-font/src/FontLoader.ts`), `expo-linking`
  (imported by `expo-router/build/link/linking.js`, `useLinking.native.js`),

  `(tabs)/_layout.tsx:65` depends on it), `react-dom` + `react-native-web`
  (web target: `app.json.web.output="static"`, `scripts/web`,
  `@expo/router-server`), `expo-build-properties`/`expo-asset`/`expo-sqlite`/…
  plugin-listed AND imported.
- Everything else verified imported: expo-audio 7, expo-blur 1 (`BarBlur.tsx:14`),
  expo-crypto 3, expo-document-picker 2, expo-file-system 27, expo-font 5,

## 3. Unused i18n strings

- **`en.json`: clean.** `scripts/i18n-check.mjs` (ran it: "Translations check out ✓")
  already enforces both directions: "an English key nothing in the app asks for"
  fails, and "code asks for key missing from en.json" fails. No unused EN key
  can exist while CI passes.
- **Stale locale entries (provably unreachable — a key is looked up only by its
  English text; if it were in use, i18n-check would have failed on the en.json
  side, and baseKey∉en means no `t(...)`/`tg(...)` call can produce it).**
  Systematic repro: `pnpm i18n:status <locale>` → section "Stale (delete these)".
  Concrete counts: fr 16, it 35, pt-BR 16, tr 17, uk 16; ca/es/pl/ru/sv/tr?/zh-CN 0.
  - The same **16 podcast strings** in fr, pt-BR, uk, tr, it (leftovers from PR
    #273, whose cleanup commit `59ba627` removed the matching en keys):
    `This podcast's feed could not be read, so these episodes may be out of date.`,
    `Play latest`, `Episodes`, `Untitled episode`, `No audio available`,
    `Pull down to read the feed again.`, `Unsubscribed from “{name}”`,
    `Tap + to subscribe to a show.`, `Open website`, `Unsubscribe`,
    `Couldn't read that feed.`, `Search for a podcast`,
    `Couldn't search for podcasts.`,
    `No podcast found for “{term}”. Try pasting the feed address below.`,
    `Search for a show, or paste the address of a feed below.`, `Subscribe`
  - tr additionally: `Navigation bar blur`
  - it additionally 18 reworded-settings/share strings
    (`The shade of the greys…`, `Blur behind the bars`, `Share Resonus link`,
    `Check out {name} by {artist} on {server}`, `Open link`, …)
  - Cross-checked against `context.jsonc`: i18n-check reports zero orphan notes,
    so the note files need no changes; only the 5 locale JSON files lose entries.
    Safe to delete.

- **Settings screens: all 25 reachable** despite looking unreachable to a naive
  grep — the index navigates dynamically: `src/app/settings/index.tsx:182`
  `router.push(\`/settings/${s.key}\`)`, and every screen's key appears in the
  sections list (`network.tsx` is added conditionally on `auth` at :103, i.e.
  reachable for any signed-in profile). Nothing to delete here.
- **Tabs**: all 5 tab screens listable (`(tabs)/_layout.tsx:84` maps
  `bottomTabs`, `href: null` hides but route kept).
- **Home sections**: all 12 `DEFAULT_HOME_SECTIONS` keys have renderers in
  `(tabs)/index.tsx` (checked per key).
- **Commented-out code: none found.** Method: regex for code-shaped comment
  lines (`//` ending in `;`/`{`/`=>`) and `/*…*/` blocks containing code — the
  repo's comments are prose only (2,772 block comments, all doc/prose).
- **Unused `useState`: none.** Scanned all 216 `const [g, s] = useState` pairs:
  every getter and setter is referenced (≥1 internal use beyond declaration);
  no setter-never-called pairs.
- **Write-only model field (the only one found) — CERTAIN:**
  `PodcastEpisode.description`, `src/api/subsonic.ts:1959`.
  Written: `src/api/podcasts.ts:175` (`episode.description = item.description`,
  fed from `ParsedItem.description`, `src/lib/podcastFeed.ts:27` + parse at
  :179 area). Persisted inside the episode's whole-object JSON blob
  (`podcastDb.ts` INSERTs `JSON.stringify` into `data` column). Read: **nowhere**
  — the only `.description` render is the *channel's* at `podcast/[id].tsx:170`
  (`ch.description`); `PodcastRow.tsx`, `PodcastEpisodeCard.tsx`,
  `podcasts.tsx`, `episodeToSong` never touch it. (PR #273's cleanup commit
  `59ba622` dropped the feed's per-item art/length plumbing but left this field.)
  Delete: interface field (`subsonic.ts:1959`) + assignment (`podcasts.ts:175`);
  optionally the `ParsedItem.description` capture in `podcastFeed.ts` (keep the
  entity decode — the `<description>` tag parse at feed level is still needed
  for the channel). Old persisted JSON harmlessly carries the extra key.
- Unreachable-guard sweep: no `if (false)`/dead switch arms/never-matching enum
  comparisons found in the screens/stores sampled; `useTabBar.ts` NO_BAR/NO_MINI_PLAYER
  entries all name real routes (`player,queue,lyrics,favorites-add,login` exist).
- Confidence: exhaustive for exports; state-field write-onliness exhaustive for
  the podcast models + libraryMirror (`MirrorState` all 24 members called —
  verified individually); a full write-only sweep of settings.ts's ~100 fields
  needs maintainer tooling (not done).

## Appendix: verification methods (reproducible)

1. **Unimported module scan**: for each `src/{lib,hooks,store,api,theme,components}`
   file, searched `@/<dir>/<base>` plus same-dir relative forms `./<file>` and
   `./<base>` → no file-level dead module found (8 apparent hits all had relative
   importers: `localDb` via `./localDb` at `localLibrary.ts:36`;
   `castMedia` via `./castMedia`; `upnpRemoteSync` via `./upnpRemoteSync`
   (`player.ts:127`); `jellyfin` via `./jellyfin` (`backend.ts:9`); theme/index
   imported as `@/theme` itself).
2. **Export-symbol scan**: `^export (function|const|let|class|interface|async function)`
   extraction (979 unique names) + word-boundary scan across src+test; names in
   ≤1 file inspected for intra-file usage; re-export syntax (`export {} from`,
   bare `export {}`, `require('pkg')` property access, dynamic
   `await import(...)`) checked separately.
3. **Dependency scan**: per-package grep across src/modules/scripts/patches/
   plugins/ios-widget/app.json + node_modules internal importers.
4. **i18n**: ran `scripts/i18n-check.mjs` (passes ⇒ no unused en key) and
   `scripts/i18n-status.mjs` for stale entries (provably unreachable, see text).
5. **Icons**: parsed all 145 icon-map keys; exact-quoted-literal search;
   enumerated the only two dynamic icon-name templates.
6. **useState pairs**: 216 pairs, per-file word counts.

# PART 5 — Code-quality deep review — the nine core files

# Core files — deep code-quality review

**Baseline:** `main` @ `b1075627` (merge of PR #273, podcasts).
**Prior checklist:** `the removed hygiene review (reproduced in Appendix H)` (2026-10-21 @ `6d19644d`, pre-merge).
Every claim below was re-verified against current code; each finding is labelled
**still present** (verified at a current line) or **new since 0.8.4 merge**.

Diff of the nine files since `6d19644d`: `library.tsx` +427, `downloads.ts` +274,
`subsonic.ts` +27, `data.ts` +13, `player.ts` +43, `player.tsx` +41,
`TrackListView.tsx` +11, `settings.ts` +3, **`localQueries.ts` unchanged (±0)**.
So anything the prior doc claimed about `localQueries.ts` is, by construction, still
present — I re-verified the load-bearing ones anyway.

No files were modified during this review.

---

## Ranking

Ranked by (lines that would disappear) × (severity). "Impact" is my estimate of the
net edit that fixes it, in lines. Severity: **critical bug** > bug > high (structural,
hot path) > med (structural, cold path) > low (naming/comment).

| # | File:line | One-liner | Impact | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `store/player.ts:4766` | `reset()` hand-enumerates ~30 module globals and has drifted: ~20 survive a profile switch (`warmedIds` alone silently disables pre-warming for the new account) | ~120 | **critical bug** |
| 2 | `store/player.ts:3094` | `queueStorageKey()` asks mode before profile → every server account writes its queue into the shared `resonus.queue.offline` bucket | ~10 | **critical bug** |
| 3 | `lib/localQueries.ts:403` …(×22) | The DB→DB→memory tier cascade is hand-copied 22 times with 22 empty catches: no resolver, no error recorded, three different order-vocabulary tables per function | ~450 | high |
| 4 | `store/downloads.ts:1207` | `downloadPodcastGroup` is a near-verbatim 130-line copy of `downloadGroup`'s worker/retry/cancel/progress/toast machinery | ~170 | high |
| 5 | `store/settings.ts:1596` | `applySaved` is 418 lines of hand-written per-key validation; `autoSetters` (:1546) already derives that same per-key table | ~250 | high |
| 6 | `store/downloads.ts:192` | Seven module cache vars + two in-flight markers with hand-tagged dir keys and no eviction | ~140 | high |
| 7 | `app/(tabs)/library.tsx:288` | Five tab components repeat the same react-query keys, hook reads and FlatList scaffolding; the All-tab never refetches podcasts on pull-to-refresh | ~180 | high (bug + dup) |
| 8 | `components/TrackListView.tsx:229` | 1039-line component, 35 props, four parallel index spaces, the play button drawn twice with mirrored a11y | ~250 | high |
| 9 | `app/player.tsx:265` | 1465-line component (578-line `return`), import-time storage side effect writing a module global, dual `spins`/`spinsSV` counters | ~200 | high |
| 10 | `api/subsonic.ts:982` `:1528` | `reorderPlaylist`/`savePlayQueue` re-implement `request()` inline: no timeout, plain Spanish `Error`, migration retry skipped | ~90 | **bug** |
| 11 | `store/downloads.ts:1439` ×4 | The "involved albums" dedupe map is written identically in four entry points | ~24 | med |
| 12 | `store/downloads.ts:1528` vs `:1340` | Two delete paths (`deleteSongs` / `removeEpisodeDownloads`) that differ only in the DB they walk, written separately | ~60 | med |
| 13 | `api/data.ts:394`/`:438`/`:475`/`:764` | The four genre endpoints have no offline branch while every sibling list in the layer does | ~60 | bug |
| 14 | `api/data.ts:916` | `getMostPlayedSongs` duplicates `derivedSongList('frequent')` (:567) — same 15-album pool, same sort, same fallback | ~35 | med |
| 15 | `api/subsonic.ts:560` ×5 | Error messages in this module are bare English; `jellyfin.ts:214` wraps the identical strings in `tg()`, `navidrome.ts:52` hardcodes Spanish | ~40 | med |
| 16 | `lib/localQueries.ts:1283` | Memory-search branch builds artist-hit keys with `normKey(s.artist \|\| '')` while the DB branch uses `\|\| UNKNOWN_ARTIST` → unknown-artist songs never reach tier 3 | ~10 | **bug** |
| 17 | `api/subsonic.ts:1754` | `coverArtUrl(…, size = 300)` — the doc two lines above says "there are three of them on purpose"; the default is a fourth tier | ~4 | low |
| 18 | `api/subsonic.ts:1857` vs `:1903` | `hasJukeboxRole` swallows every failure, `hasShareRole` deliberately rethrows network ones — same lookup, opposite contracts | ~25 | med |
| 19 | `store/player.ts:334`/`:448` vs `:858`/`:887`/`:2175`/`:3994` | "Is this a podcast?" asked by two different fields (`podcastChannelId` vs `vod`) across 9 sites in 2 files | ~30 | bug-risk |
| 20 | `app/(tabs)/library.tsx:221` | `withPins` applied in 3 of 5 tabs; the two that skip it still compute pin keys that can never exist | ~15 | med |

Full findings follow, grouped by file, with the cleaner shape for each.

## `src/store/player.ts` (4898 lines) — module-global state machine

### P1 · `player.ts:4766–4836` · `reset()` has drifted — **critical bug** — *still present (worse than the prior doc claimed)*

`reset()` is a 70-line hand-written enumeration of what survives a profile switch.
I diffed it against the module's globals. Cleared (24): `autoplayFetchedFor`,
`autoplayRound`, `artistFill`, `similarArtistsCache`, `syncTimer`, `playedHistory`,
`streamOffsetSec`, `sourceHasLength`, `scrobbledThisTrack`, `transcodeOffset*`,
`failedSource`, `errorTrackId`, `errorAttempts`, `playbackReport*`, plus the ones
hidden inside helpers (`cancelSleepTimer`, `clearPlayedHere`, `cutCrossfade` →
`fadeTimer`/`fadeState`/`pauseFadeTimer`/`pendingFade`, `stopPeriodicSync` → `syncWanted`,
`clearLockScreen` → `lockOwner`).

**Survives a profile switch (verified absent from the body):**

| global | declared |
|---|---|
| `warmedIds` | `:1493` |
| `warmChain` | `:1552` |
| `radioRound` | `:1775` |
| `queuedNext` | `:1917` |
| `pendingSeek` | `:2581` |
| `stallSince` / `stallPos` / `stallProbed` / `stallFellBack` | `:2594–2597` |
| `gaveUp` | `:2668` |
| `bgRetryTimer` / `bgWaiting` / `bgRounds` | `:2673–2678` |
| `lastStatusAt` | `:2878` |
| `lastBgPositionPush` | `:2887` |
| `queueDirty` | `:3157` |
| `localSavedAt` | `:3168` |
| `lastAdoptCheck` / `wentAway` | `:3279–3282` |
| `lastOwnerCheck` | `:3392` |
| `appStateAttached` | `:3369` |
| `bootQuiet` / `bootQuietTimer` | `:1363–1364` |
| `nextLyricsTimer` | `:1347` |
| `lastGaplessSettings` / `lastReplayGain` | `:2094` / `:2142` |
| `queueOrigin` | `:531` |
| `resumeOnPhone` | `:933` |
| `audioModeReady` | `:236` |

Concrete consequence, not hypothetical: `warmUpcoming()` at `:1514` reads
`if (warmedIds.has(song.id)) continue;`. After an account switch, ids warmed for the
old profile block pre-warming for the new one. `warmUpcoming` clears the set at `:1496`
only on the *queue* path, not on the profile path, so this is reachable from
`player.ts:4417` / `:4479` (the two `await get().reset()` callsites).
Second consequence: `bgRounds` (`:2678`) is a counter of background retry rounds;
carried over, the new profile's first track gets fewer allowed rounds.
Third: `lastOwnerCheck` (`:3392`) caches "the server queue is ours" with a 10 s TTL —
across profiles that is a claim about a *different* account's queue, and
`markWeOwnServerQueue` writes it from the wrong profile's push.

Cleaner shape — one owner for the per-playback singletons, so the enumeration cannot
drift (the same suggestion the prior doc made, which is still unfixed):

```ts
// One object, one clear, so adding a singleton cannot forget reset().
const playback = {
  warmedIds: new Set<string>(),
  stall: { since: 0, pos: -1, probed: false, fellBack: false },
  pendingSeek: null as PendingSeek | null,
  queuedNext: null as QueuedNext | null,
  radioRound: null as RadioRound | null,
  gaveUp: null as GaveUp | null,
  bg: { timer: null, waiting: false, rounds: 0 },
  ownerCheck: { at: 0, theirs: false },
  // …the rest
};
function clearPlaybackState() {
  for (const k of CLEARABLE) playback[k] = INITIAL[k];   // table, not prose
  void import('@/lib/navidromeRepair');                   // n/a — illustrative
}
reset: async (forProfile = false) => {
  clearPlaybackState();
  // …only the things that are genuinely not playback state
}
```

If a full refactor is out of scope, the minimal fix is the same shape at zero cost:
keep the module singletons, but declare them in one object literal and reset by
`Object.assign(playback, initialPlaybackState())` — a new singleton added inside that
object is reset for free, whereas a new `let` at module scope is invisible to `reset()`.

### P2 · `player.ts:3094–3098` · offline-first queue key — **critical bug** — *still present*

```ts
3094  function queueStorageKey(): string | null {
3096    if (offline) return 'resonus.queue.offline';
```

The mode is consulted before the profile, so *every* server account that drops into
offline mode writes its queue under the one shared `resonus.queue.offline` bucket that
the local-files profile also uses. Two accounts, one queue: switching profiles
restores the other account's queue into this one (song ids are only unique per
account, which `:4797–4799` in the same file already admits: "song ids are only
unique within the account that issued them").

`playHistory.ts:24–41` documents exactly this hazard and refuses it ("asking about
the mode first put every account's offline listening into one shared bucket") —
the rule was never carried over to the queue. Fix: derive from
`profileScopeId()` and delete the offline-first branch, i.e.
`${KEY}.${hashKey(profileScopeId())}` like the seven stores that already do it.

### P3 · ~60 module-level `let` singletons — *still present*

`grep -c '^let '` on the file: 60 module-scope mutable singletons, 22 of them timers
(`sleepTimeout`, `sleepFadeTimeout`, `sleepFadeTimer`, `nextLyricsTimer`,
`bootQuietTimer`, `warmChain`, `autoplayRound`, `radioRound`, `fadeTimer`,
`pauseFadeTimer`, `handoffSub`, `bgRetryTimer`, `syncTimer`, `syncInterval`, …) and
8 of them a `Promise | null` "one in flight at a time" marker
(`autoplayRound`, `radioRound`, `transcodeOffsetAsking`, `playbackReportAsking`,
`warmChain`, plus the `stall*` quadruple).

Two concrete failure modes this shape enables, both already visible:
- **listener/timer leak class**: every one of these must be `clearInterval`/`clearTimeout`-ed
  on *every* exit path. `reset()` misses `bgRetryTimer` and `bootQuietTimer` (above),
  so a background retry round-robin keeps running into the next profile's queue.
- **race class**: `stallSince/stallPos/stallProbed/stallFellBack` (`:2594–2597`) is a
  4-field state machine mutated from `onStatus` listeners; it has no owner object, so
  no code path can assert it is coherent, and `reset()` cannot see it.

Ranking note: the *aggregate* impact of collapsing these into 4 named owners
(playback / warm / autoplay-radio / sync) is ~400 lines of easier code and deletes
the drift class outright; the individual P1 fix above is the subset that is provably
broken today.

### P4 · `player.ts:1179` `HISTORY_MAX = 100` caps entries, not songs — *still present*

Each undo entry holds two full `Song[]` references (`queue` and `originalQueue`), so
history can retain up to 100 whole album-sized queues. Cap total songs across entries,
or store `{source, sourceHref}` and re-fetch the queue.

### P5 · naming: `player.ts` mixes three contracts for the same concept — *new since merge*

The podcast PR added a second "is this an episode?" predicate in the same file:
`streamReachable(song)` (from `subsonic.ts:219`, keyed on `podcastChannelId`) is used at
`:334` and `:448`, while `!song.vod` is used at `:858` (`liveInfo`), `:887`
(`onStreamMetadata`), `:2175` and `:3994` (`useLiveInfo`). `vod` and
`podcastChannelId` are two independent optional fields on `Song` whose only producer,
`api/podcasts.ts:412 episodeToSong`, happens to set both — so the two spellings agree
today by accident, and any future caller that sets one without the other splits the
rule. `player.tsx:977` reimplements the reachability rule a third way
(`song.url && !song.vod` instead of `streamReachable(song)`) even though `subsonic.ts:219`
says "the one rule the rows, the queue marking and the player's source decision all read".
Fix: one exported predicate per concept (`isPodcastEpisode(song)`,
`streamReachable(song)`) and no inline `song.url && !song.vod` anywhere.
Impact ~30 lines, severity: latent bug.

### P6 · `player.ts:265 export function localSourceFor` — 'cellular' branch grew an episode special case — *new since merge*

```ts
return (song.podcastChannelId || useNetworkType.getState().cellular) ? file : undefined;
```
This is the correct behaviour but it belongs in the file's one quality decision table,
not inside a mode named `cellular`: the branch now answers "which copy do we prefer"
using a podcast fact that has nothing to do with cellular. With P5's single predicate
this reads `return (isPodcastEpisode(song) || cellular) ? file : undefined;` and the
exception stops looking like an exception to a quality rule.

## `src/store/downloads.ts` (1674 lines)

### D1 · `downloads.ts:1207–1335` ≡ `downloads.ts:971–1199` — **near-verbatim copy, new since merge**

`downloadPodcastGroup(groupKey, episodes)` is the podcast PR's second download pipeline.
Side-by-side, the two are the same function with different bytes-sources:

| `downloadGroup` (`:971`) | `downloadPodcastGroup` (`:1207`) |
|---|---|
| `if (get().active[groupKey]) return;` `:978` | identical `:1213` |
| `const seen = new Set<string>(); const pending = songs.filter(…)` `:981–986` | identical shape `:1215–1220` (only the "already local" test differs) |
| "Wi-Fi only" block `:990–993` | **byte-identical** `:1223–1227` (same comment line too) |
| `set((st) => ({ active: { … done: 0, total: pending.length, fraction: 0 } }))` `:996` | identical `:1230` |
| progress callback inside `createDownloadResumable` `:1083–1094` | identical except the file-name expression `:1244–1252` (incl. the `fraction - cur.fraction > 0.05` coarsening) |
| `if (!res \|\| res.status !== 200) throw new TransferError(…)`, `if (isErrorBody(res.headers)) throw new TransferError('error body, not audio', 200)` `:1102–1103` | same two guards, same message `:1258–1264` |
| `catch → await FileSystem.deleteAsync(file, { idempotent: true }).catch(() => {}); throw e;` `finally → tasks.delete(task)` `:1278–1283` | identical `:1279–1283` |
| worker pool `Array.from({ length: Math.min(maxSlots(), pending.length) }, …)` with `while (next < pending.length)`, `cancelling.has(groupKey)` break, `for (attempt = 1; attempt <= ATTEMPTS; attempt++)`, `await takeSlot()`, `finally freeSlot()`, `await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, RETRY_PAUSE_MS * attempt))` `:1139–1169` | **identical 24 lines** `:1288–1311` |
| `invalidate(); if (cancelling…) toast('Download stopped') else if (failed>0) … else …` `:1172–1189` | same epilogue with different i18n keys `:1314–1325` |
| `finally { cancelling.delete(groupKey); activeTasks.delete(groupKey); set(… delete active[groupKey] …) }` `:1190–1198` | **byte-identical** `:1326–1334` |

~130 lines of the podcast function are the song function with 3 real differences
(fetch-one, cancel-guarded `ensureAlbum` absent, delete-on-completion target). Every
change to retry policy, slot accounting, cancel semantics or progress coarsening now
has to be made twice, and the two already differ: the song path checks
`if (offline) return;` (`:977`) while the podcast path checks `isManualOffline()`
(`:1212`) — two spellings of the same gate, and the comment at `:974–976` even says
"this is that same rule, said here", i.e. the duplication is acknowledged in prose
rather than in code.

Cleaner shape — one pipeline, one transfer strategy per item:

```ts
type Item = { id: string; url: string; dest: string; commit: (bytes: number) => Promise<void> };

async function downloadItems(groupKey: string, items: Item[], opts: {
  mayStart: () => Promise<boolean> ?? () => !isManualOffline();   // the gate differs per source
  prepare?: (item) => Promise<void>;      // songs: ensureAlbum/ensureArtist; episodes: nothing
  statusOk: (res) => boolean;             // songs: === 200 ; episodes: 2xx
  doneToast: (n) => Localized;
}) {
  // one slot loop, one retry ladder, one cancel check, one progress reporter, one finally
}

const downloadSongs    = downloadItems(key, songs.map(toSongItem),    { …server opts });
const downloadEpisodes = downloadItems(key, eps.map(toEpisodeItem),   { …publisher opts });
```
Impact: ~170 lines gone, and the retry/slot/cancel invariants get one home.

### D2 · `downloads.ts:1439` / `:1449` / `:1497` / `:1510` — the same dedupe map four times — *new-ish (three of the four predate the merge; the file's shape is unchanged by it)*

`downloadSongs`, `downloadPlaylist`, `downloadGenre` and `downloadFavorites` each
re-declare, with the same comment above them, the "involved albums" map:

```ts
1439      // Involved albums: those of the songs (partial entry if needed).
1440      const byId = new Map<string, Album>();
1441      for (const s of songs) {
1442        const al = albumFromSong(s);
1443        if (!byId.has(al.id)) byId.set(al.id, al);
1444      }
```
(identical at `:1449–1454`, `:1498–1503`, `:1511–1516`; `downloadSong:1435` spells the
same rule as `[albumFromSong(song)]`.)

Cleaner shape: `const involvedAlbums = (songs) => Object.values(reduce(…, albumFromSong))`
next to `albumFromSong` (`:717`), and all five entry points become
`await downloadGroup(key(songs), songs, involvedAlbums(songs))`. Impact ~24 lines.

### D3 · `downloads.ts:192–212` — seven module cache vars with hand-tagged dir keys — *still present*

```ts
192  let cachedShelf: Omit<DownloadsCatalog, 'songs'> | null = null;
201  let shelfReading: { dir: string; work: Promise<…> } | null = null;
203  let songsReading: { dir: string; work: Promise<Song[]> } | null = null;
204  let cachedForDir: string | null = null;
208  let cachedSongs: Song[] | null = null;
209  let cachedSongsDir: string | null = null;
212  let cachedFull: DownloadsCatalog | null = null;
```
`cachedShelf` is validated against `cachedForDir` (`:293`), `cachedSongs` against
`cachedSongsDir` (`:261`), and `cachedFull` against **nothing** (`:278` — it is
recomputed only when falsy). So a profile switch that leaves `cachedFull` populated
while `cachedSongs` is re-read for the new dir hands back a catalog whose songs belong
to the previous profile, because the guard pair for `cachedFull` was never written.
The file compensates with `cachedFull = null` at `:276`, i.e. the invariant is real but
is enforced by a line 400 lines away from the declaration rather than by the shape of
the cache.

Cleaner shape: one keyed cache, one in-flight map — the same `profileCache<T>` idea the
prior doc asked for in `localQueries.ts`, applied here:

```ts
const shelfCache = new Map<string, Shelf>();          // keyed by dir, size 2
const reading   = new Map<string, Promise<…>>();      // the in-flight marker
function catalogFor(dir) { return shelfCache.get(dir) ?? (reading[dir] ??= …) }
```
This deletes the whole class of "which of these six variables is stale" bugs; impact
~140 lines, and it makes the DB failure observable (today `catch`-shaped like D1's).

### D4 · `downloads.ts:1528 deleteSongs` vs `:1340 removeEpisodeDownloads` — *still present / new*

Same 3-step shape (patch `files`, delete on disk, drop from the DB, `invalidate()`),
different DB (`Db.drop…` server catalog vs `dropDownloads(podcastScope(), …)`),
different return (`void` vs count) and different query invalidation
(`['podcastDownloads']` only in the episode one). The doc comment at `:1338` already
admits the shape ("The two delete actions differ only in where the ids come from").
One `removeDownloads(ids, drop: (ids) => Promise<void>, invalidate: key[])`.
Impact ~60 lines.

### D5 · `downloads.ts` hydrate: `Promise.all` over two DB reads plus two `if (run !== hydrateRun) return;` guards at `:1402` and `:1421` — *still present*

The second guard is inside a detached IIFE (`:1409–1423`) whose result nobody awaits —
`dlBitRates` is settled by whichever run finishes last, and the IIFE's rejection is
invisible. Prior doc's `hydrateGuard()` suggestion (a `src/lib/profileScope.ts` helper)
is still the right answer here; two hand-rolled shapes remain in this file. Impact ~40.

### D6 · `downloads.ts:971 downloadGroup` — 230 lines in one function — *still present*

Six named concerns in one body (gate, dedupe, wifi gate, cover/album side-effects with
its own nested `ensureAlbum`/`ensureArtist` closures at `:1019–1073`, per-song fetch
at `:1080–1134`, worker pool at `:1136–1170`, epilogue toast, cleanup). Splitting
`fetchSong`, `ensureAlbum` and the worker loop into module-level functions with an
explicit `Slot`/`Group` owner object is what makes D1's collapse possible at all —
they are nested closures today, so they capture `dir`, `auth`, `groupKey`, `tasks`,
`trackArt`, `albumDone`, `artistDone` implicitly, which is why they cannot be shared.
