# PART 6 — Playlists & podcasts — inventory, gaps, provable bugs

# FEATURE COMPLETENESS — Playlists & Podcasts (Resonus, main @ b1075627, Expo SDK 57)

> **Status 2026-10-22 — open PR #278 (branch `podcast-v2`, unmerged; commits `0985cab`/`6a56832`/`5a936d9`):**
> it addresses parts of the podcast findings below —
> (a) episode rows read **show notes** (2 lines + `Show more`/`Show less` per row), **publish date** and **length**, drop the artist line, and are **not numbered** (`itunes:episode` stays parsed, rounded before the `> 0` test, into `Song.track` for the info sheet);
> (b) per-show **arrival mark** — accent dot + `new` for episodes whose `PodcastEpisode.firstSeenAt` reaches `PodcastChannel.newSinceAt` (never marked on first filing or on a moved feed) — plus a **`Refresh (2 h ago)`-clock** in the ⋯ sheet (`formatAge`, reading `refreshedAt`).
> Consequences for this report: **Gap #1 must NOT be re-implemented** (review/extend the PR instead);
> **Gap #4 is partially in flight**; the two clocks also supply the data Gap #5 (auto-download of new episodes) and wishlist #1 (unread badge) need.
> All findings below remain **true for main @ b1075627** since the PR is unmerged.
> Also worth folding **into PR #278 itself** (its own "left open" list): the invisible measuring `Text` duplicates the notes in the node tree — a screen reader reads them twice (`accessibilityVisibility='hidden'` would fix it; same pattern as the known invisible second Text, Part 5 T6); `position={numbered && !showArtwork…}` becomes feed-number-printing for a future numbered podcast list once `track` is populated; episode length currently ignores `showSongDuration` (decision needed before gating); `refreshedAt` is also stamped by a `304` and by failed reads — "the last time this feed was read" is the honest wording; `test/podcastFeed.test.ts` has no `<itunes:episode>` fixture yet.

Analysis based only on actually reading the current code. Every claim cites `file:line`.

---

## Inventory-Playlists

### Server data model (what the app's API layer models)
- `Playlist` type carries `id, name, songCount, coverArt, comment, public, owner, created, changed` — `src/api/subsonic.ts:329-344`.
- Subsonic endpoints wrapped: `getPlaylists` (`src/api/subsonic.ts:886-895`), `getPlaylist` (`:897-910`, Ampache array quirk `:907-908`), `addToPlaylist` = `updatePlaylist.view` with `songIdToAdd` (`:912-922`), `createPlaylist` (`:925-942`, with by-name lookup fallback `:937-941`), `deletePlaylist` (`:944-950`), `updatePlaylist` name/comment/public (`:952-964`), `reorderPlaylist` — full rewrite via POST `createPlaylist.view` passing `playlistId` + all `songId`s (`:966-991`; Subsonic has no "move", `:967-971`), `removeFromPlaylist` by index (`:993-1003`).
- Ampache smartlists (owner `System`) are filtered out server-side because they aren't editable (`src/api/subsonic.ts:892-894`).
- Jellyfin mapping: `toPlaylist` from Overview→comment, `ChildCount`, `DateCreated`, `DateLastMediaAdded`→changed, no `owner`/`public` (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:396-409`); playlist ops: `getPlaylists` (`:820-828`), `getPlaylist` (`:830-842` — no paging limit), `addToPlaylist` (`:844-855`), `createPlaylist` (`:857-866`), `deletePlaylist` (`:868-870`), `updatePlaylist` = rename + `IsPublic` only (`:872-883`), `removeFromPlaylist` by `PlaylistItemId` (`:918-926`), `reorderPlaylist` = batched DELETE/POST/`…/Move/` calls, 100 ids/request (`:929-1020`, `IDS_PER_REQUEST` `:885-887`).
- Navidrome native API extras: playlist cover upload `POST /api/playlist/{id}/image` multipart (`src/api/navidrome.ts:152-178`), delete cover (`:181-189`), starred-playlist ids listing (`:505-547`, `listStarredPlaylistIds` `:531`), `playlistTrackLibraries` used to hide playlist tracks from disabled libraries (`src/api/data.ts:1705-1724`, #275).

### Features that EXIST, screen by screen
1. **Browse/entry points**: Library tab "Playlists" segment with Yours/Public owner chips (`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:84-124`, chips computed `:1233-1285`), pins (`:312,356,371`), long-press opens the album/playlist media menu (`:357,363`); Explore section `PlaylistsBrowser` with its own search box, sort and rows/grid (`src/components/PlaylistsBrowser.tsx:64-213`, settings `browsePlaylistsLayout/Sort` `src/store/settings.ts:1242-1243`); Home carousel cards (`src/components/PlaylistCard.tsx:20-51`); global search tab filters playlists client-side because "search3 doesn't return them" (`src/app/(tabs)/search.tsx:171-180`).
2. **Detail screen** `src/app/playlist/[id].tsx` (727 lines): full tracklist via `getPlaylist` (`:93-97`), meta line (Playlist · N songs · total duration `:336-338`), description rendered under `showPlaylistDescription` (`:344, src/store/settings.ts:948-949`), cover (`:346-349`, tap opens `CoverViewer :430-465`).
3. **Sort/view**: `useSongSort` with fields `recent/added/alpha/artist/album/downloaded`, "Default" label, persisted per playlist (`:153-165`); reorder works on the raw server order, not the sorted view (`:323-334`, comment `:135-152`).
4. **In-screen search**: `searchable` passed to `TrackListView` (`:365`, implemented `src/components/TrackListView.tsx:330,636`).
5. **Multi-select mode**: `selection={{onRemove, onAddTo, onDownload}}` (`:379-388`); removal uses true playlist indices via `playlistIndices` from `useSongSort` (`:153-165`, hook contract `src/hooks/useSongSort.tsx:10` "mapping to original indices"), deferred-commit + Undo toast (`removeMany :246-303`).
6. **⋯ menu** (`SheetModal :468-605`): Play next (`queueMany next :471-482`), Add to queue (`:483-493`), Add to a playlist (re-opens the picker with the whole playlist `:494-503`), Reorder (`:504-515`, engine `src/components/PlaylistReorder.tsx:92-106` drag list), Edit playlist (`:516-525`), Auto-download toggle + confirm-with-size (`:526-552`, `Dialog :638-654`), Refresh ignoring 5-min cache — documented for smart playlists that re-query (`:232-242`), Share (`:565-576`, gated by `useCanShare` `src/hooks/useCanShare.ts:13-31`, server mints `createShare` link `src/lib/share.ts:1-60`), Delete downloads (`:577-591`), Delete playlist (`:593-602` optimistic + Undo, `onDelete :180-208`).
7. **Edit sheet**: name, multi-line description, Public toggle ("Visible to other users on the server"), cover change (tap cover + remove-cover badge) and password-confirm dialog for uploads (`src/components/PlaylistEditSheet.tsx:62-196`); Navidrome-only online upload (`:616` in screen; hook `src/hooks/useServerCover`) and local-profile copy upload offline (`:617`); save → `updatePlaylist` + invalidate queries + toast (`onSaveEdit :167-178`).
8. **Create**: from the picker sheet (`src/components/PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:144-154` `createAndAdd` → `createPlaylist`), from Library empty state (`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:343,949`), and from the song menu / media menu via the global picker (`src/store/playlistPicker.ts:11-22`, mounted once `PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:41-45`).
9. **Add-to-playlist flow**: pick target or "New playlist"; Spotify-style duplicate warning with counts and "Add only the new ones" / "Add anyway" (`PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:123-139, :234-285`); bulk add loops `addToPlaylist` per song (`:101`), optimistic playlist count update + reconciles auto-download (`:104-112`). Queue→playlist exists: `src/app/queue.tsx:406-427` opens the picker with the server songs of the queue (episodes excluded `:412`).
10. **Star/heart**: exists but Navidrome-≥0.64 only — `usePlaylistStars` reads starred ids via Navidrome native API (`src/hooks/usePlaylistStars.ts:26-49`; version gate `:31-49`; "no heart rather than pretend" rationale `:1-23`); heart drawn on the playlist screen's `TrackListView favorite` prop (`[id].tsx:358-362`). Writing goes through `star(id,'playlist')` (`src/api/data.ts:1303-1313`, `StarType` includes `'playlist'` `src/api/subsonic.ts:1339`). Album/playlist media menu heart is albums-only (`src/components/MediaMenuSheet.tsx` — `{album ? heart : …}`).
11. **Downloads**: one-tap download whole playlist with size dialog (`[id].tsx:656-666` → `downloadPlaylist` `src/store/downloads.ts:1449-1468`), tap-to-remove (`onDownloadPress :129-133`), stop in-progress (`:681-692`), delete-downloads (`:622-636`); a downloaded playlist is mirrored as local playlist `dl_<id>` (`downloads.ts:1461-1467`, cleaned on clear-all `downloads.ts:1612-1615`).
12. **Auto-download (playlist keep-alive)**: per-profile toggle persisted (`src/store/autoDownloads.ts:41-87`), reconciles adds only — v1 limitation documented in the file header (`autoDownloads.ts:5-13`); reconcile on open (`[id].tsx:116-123`), on add-to-playlist (`PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:112`, `[id].tsx:410`), and on return to foreground (`src/components/AppStartupTab.tsx:79`); favorites share the mechanism via a sentinel id (`autoDownloads.ts:29-32`).
13. **Offline (server-down) editing — this EXISTS, it is not missing**: outbox stores the *desired final state* per playlist (create/delete/rename/comment/public/full tracklist) — `src/store/offlineQueue.ts:31-48` `QueuePlaylist`; offline writers in `src/api/data.ts:1649-1797` (`addToPlaylist :1649-1660`, `createPlaylist` tmp id `:1663-1672`, `deletePlaylist :1674-1685`, `updatePlaylist :1763-1777`, `removeFromPlaylist :1779-1787`, `reorderPlaylist :1790-1797`, `currentPlaylistSongIds :156-163`); replay on reconnect rewrites final state via `reorderPlaylist`, then comment/public (`src/api/data.ts:1450-1486`); query-cache snapshot into the mirror before going offline so live edits are not lost (`snapshotCachesToMirror :1496-1504+`).
14. **Offline serving**: every server playlist is prefetched to the mirror in the background — 10-min cooldown, 5 per run, 4-wide, skipped by `changed` (`data.ts:1030-1101`), covers kept (`data.ts:1094-1097`, `libraryMirror.ts:269`); offline list overlays outbox edits, hides deleted ones, falls back to album art of a downloaded track when the playlist has none (`data.ts:1107-1186`); offline detail merges mirror + edit and annotates non-downloaded songs (`mirrorPlaylist :1726-1761`). Mirror rows-per-playlist storage incl. per-row song-id query (`src/lib/mirrorDb.ts:26,31,474-513,713-757`; store wiring `src/store/libraryMirror.ts:261-397`).
15. **Pins**: playlists pinnable in Library (`library.tsx:312,356,371`), media-menu pin action (`MediaMenuSheet.tsx` — `{playlist || album?.starred ?…}` around `:435`), unpin on delete (`[id].tsx:195`).
16. **Suggested tracks**: "Based on the tracks in this playlist" footer with add-to-this-playlist action (`[id].tsx:389-420`).
17. **Local profile playlists** (no server): create/add/remove/reorder/delete, custom cover files under `playlist-covers/`, per-profile JSON store with `lp_`/`dl_` legacy migration — `src/lib/localQueries.ts:887-1107` (`updatePlaylist :1090-1106` has no `public` concept).
18. **Selection-menu extras** (songs, but relevant): favorites add/remove, export downloaded files to a picked folder, delete downloads — `src/hooks/useSelectionMenu.tsx:102-177`. `src/app/favorites-add.tsx` is favorites-only (no playlist creation/suggestions).

---

## Gaps-Playlists

1. **No way to edit playlist contents from the playlist's own song rows one-by-one online except remove** — there is no "add song to this playlist" from an arbitrary song screen other than the picker; but the real missing inverse is: **no remove-single-song from the album/song side** and no undo past the toast window. (Minor.)
2. **Playlist description silently dropped on Jellyfin**: the edit sheet always offers the description field (`PlaylistEditSheet.tsx:158-168`; only `hidePublic` is offline-gated `:615,170`), but `jellyfin.ts:872-883` sends only `Name`/`IsPublic` — `comment` is never transmitted, while the screen toasts "Playlist updated" (`[id].tsx:174`). Backend gap + UI mismatch. Priority **high**.
3. **Playlist stars are Navidrome-only**: hidden on every other server and offline (`usePlaylistStars.ts:42-49`); the write path and outbox already support `starred-playlist` (`data.ts:1303-1313`, `StarType :1339`, offline `setFav` stores it, flush replays it `data.ts:1389-1390`) but `mirrorStarred` declines to display it offline (`data.ts:1278-1283` "Nowhere to put it"). Spec-compliant Subsonic servers that store starred playlists get no heart UI. Priority **medium**.
4. **No playlist search-by-content / no "find playlists by tag or by songs"**: browse and search match playlist *names* only (`PlaylistsBrowser.tsx:103-111`, `search.tsx:178-180`, `library.tsx:297-316`). Subsonic's `search3` doesn't return playlists (noted `search.tsx:171`), but the app has the full playlist list mirrored — could match descriptions/owner too, it doesn't. Priority **medium**.
5. **No playlist export/import or m3u round-trip**: export in selection menu copies downloaded *audio files* into a folder (`useSelectionMenu.tsx:118-133`, `exportSong.ts`), nothing writes the list itself; no M3U parser anywhere (`grep m3u` only hits radio/Live-Tv M3U-tuner text `src/app/radio.tsx:284`). Nothing from the Subsonic/Jellyfin layer models this. Priority **medium**.
6. **Public flag has no browse consequence on creation**: playlists can only be made public *after* creation via the edit sheet; `createPlaylist` never carries comment/public (`subsonic.ts:925-942`, `data.ts:1663-1672`, picker `:144-154`) — and offline-created playlists only patch comment/public if the edit sheet was used (`data.ts:1450-1486` handles it). Priority **low**.
7. **No cover-art handling beyond Navidrome + local**: cover *display* is universal, upload is Navidrome native-only (`[id].tsx:88-91,616`; `navidrome.ts:152-178`) and local-profile copy (`localQueries.ts:1050-1066`). Jellyfin serves playlist Primary images (`jellyfin.ts:405`) but the app never writes them (`updatePlaylist :872-883`); Subsonic has no upload endpoint. Smart fallback for offline art exists (`data.ts:1121-1183`). Priority **medium** (Jellyfin upload is available server-side but not implemented).
8. **No pagination anywhere in the playlist tracklist path**: `getPlaylist` asks for the whole list (`subsonic.ts:897-910`, `jellyfin.ts:830-842`), the dedup check in the picker fetches the whole target playlist on every add (`PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:127-131`), add loops one request per song (`subsonic.ts:912-922`, picker loop `:101`), rename+tracklist rewrite posts every id (`subsonic.ts:966-991`; Jellyfin batches at 100 `:885-887` vs Subsonic single POST). A 5k-song playlist makes taps O(n) requests. Priority **medium-high** on performance grounds.
9. **Smart/dynamic playlists**: Ampache stock smartlists are hidden outright (`subsonic.ts:892-894`); the app *reads* smart-playlist churn (refresh button `[id].tsx:553-564`, `onRefresh :232-242`; prefetch cooldown rationale `data.ts:1032-1037`) but never lets the user create or edit rules (no server API for that anyway — note it as backend-supports-nothing). Priority **low**.
10. **No playlist merge/copy tool** (duplicating a playlist = recreate via picker loop). Priority **low**.
11. **Auto-download never removes and never deletes on toggle-off** — self-documented v1 limitation (`autoDownloads.ts:5-13`), reference-counted deletion explicitly "left for later". Priority **medium** (stale files for songs removed from lists consume storage indefinitely).
12. **Playlist edits made *online* but with a crashed/queued flush**: failed items are kept for next reconnect (`data.ts:1478-1485`) — OK; but offline *starring of a playlist* then offline-unstarring is last-write-wins (`offlineQueue.ts:25-29` FavOp) — fine. Real bug-adjacent gap: **offline playlist star cannot be unstarred** since the heart is hidden offline (`usePlaylistStars.ts:43`). Priority **low**.
13. **Playlist cover of `dl_`/`tmp_` playlists**: offline-created playlist cover falls back to a track's album art (`data.ts:1150-1157`), no custom local cover for server-created-but-offline-edited lists — minor.
14. **`favorites-add.tsx`** — the "suggestions" machinery exists for *favorites only* (`:41-240`); there is no equivalent "suggest songs for this playlist" beyond the SuggestedTracks footer (`[id].tsx:389-420`). Parity gap, priority **low**.

---

## Bugs-Playlists

1. **Jellyfin edit sheet lies**: Description edit + Public toggle are offered (Jellyfin does accept `IsPublic` `jellyfin.ts:880`) but the description edit is dropped (`jellyfin.ts:878-881` ignores `changes.comment`) while `[id].tsx:174` toasts success. Provable from code. **High.**
2. **Playlist heart disappears offline**: `favorite` prop is `undefined` when `usePlaylistStars()` is `undefined`, and the hook is disabled offline (`usePlaylistStars.ts:54` `enabled`, `:43` returns false offline) — a Navidrome user who starred playlists sees no hearts while offline, unlike songs whose offline stars are queued (`data.ts:1303-1313`). UX inconsistency. **Medium.**
3. **Ampache create-by-name fallback can bind the wrong playlist**: `createPlaylist` falls back to `lists.find((p) => p.name === name)` (`subsonic.ts:937-941`) — with duplicate names (or another user's public playlist of the same name, which getPlaylists also returns `subsonic.ts:886-895`) subsequent edits land on the wrong id. **Medium.**
4. **`removeMany` offline-empty edge**: when every song is removed, the code deletes by index descending (`[id].tsx:283-287`) instead of the set-rewrite — deliberate (comment `:274-287`); no bug found, but note the two different strategies make the offline path's empty-list case the *only* one that can double-fire if the deferred commit races an auto-download reconcile of the same playlist (`reconcileKnown` re-fetches the server state `[id].tsx:116-123`) — worth a test, not provable as a defect. **Low.**
5. **`onReorderSave` optimistic view rewrite uses the OLD query's song set** (`[id].tsx:216-221`) — songs added to the playlist between load and save are silently dropped from the optimistic data (server rewrite also omits them: `reorderPlaylist(id, songIds)` `:223` receives only the drag list, `[id].tsx:99` `songIds` snapshot) — concurrent adds by other clients/users are lost on reorder on *all* backends since Subsonic rewrites the full list (`subsonic.ts:966-991`). Real last-writer-wins conflict with no warning. **Medium.**
6. **Picker duplicate check adds a full extra fetch per tap** (`PlaylistPickerSheet.tsx:127-131`) and is skipped silently on network failure (`:135-137` comment "better than blocking") — acceptable but doubles traffic for huge target playlists (see gap #8). **Low.**
7. **Download state keys on `songIds` snapshot at screen mount** (`[id].tsx:99-110`): a playlist that gains songs while open can show "done" based on the old set until the query refreshes — self-healed by invalidations (`:106-110`, `:408-409`), cosmetic. **Low.**
8. **`updatePlaylist` local-profile path drops `public`** (`localQueries.ts:1090-1106` — only name/comment): consistent with hidden toggle offline (`[id].tsx:615`), so no user-visible defect; flagged so it isn't mistaken for support. **Info.**

---

## Inventory-Podcasts

### Architecture
- Podcasts are 100% device-local: "no server is asked" because Navidrome answers 501 and Jellyfin/Ampache have no podcast API (`src/api/podcasts.ts:1-8`). SQLite per profile: channels, episodes, favorites (channel/episode hearts), download records — `src/lib/podcastDb.ts:22-59` schema.
- Feeds: RSS/Atom parsed with regexes; reads title, author, description, image, site link, per-item title/guid-or-enclosure-id/pubDate/duration/enclosure+type/description (`src/lib/podcastFeed.ts:284-323, 260-266, 222-239, 206-219, 177-203`); newest-first ordering (`sortEpisodes :326-337`); HTML in descriptions is stripped to plain text (`:122-146`).
- Adding a show: paste feed URL in the Add sheet (`src/components/PodcastAddSheet.tsx:88-99` → `refreshChannel :42-54`), iTunes Search discovery via the Store section of Explore (`src/app/podcasts.tsx:105-116` → `src/api/podcasts.ts:461-475` search / `:512-547` top-100 chart), and HTML-page → `rel=alternate` feed resolution followed up to 2 hops (`podcasts.ts:149-167`), conditional GETs with stored etag/last-modified and 304 fast-path (`:90-129`, `refreshChannel :196-275`, validators stored `:257-259`, kept-on-304 `:229-234`).
- "No subscribe — opening lands it" (`podcasts.ts:184-186`); auto re-read: a show older than 72 h is re-read in the background when opened (`podcast/[id].tsx:44-45,78-82`); refresh-all from the Podcasts screen with stop-mid-flight (`podcasts.tsx:118-143,294-300`, `refreshAll podcasts.ts:368-383`, cancel flag `:374-376`).
- Show screen = the album renderer: cover/scroll, description (gated by `showPlaylistDescription` `podcast/[id].tsx:54-55,170`), meta (episodes count + total duration `:114-128`), episodes bridged to `Song`s (`episodeToSong podcasts.ts:400-425` — `vod:true` so speed applies, `podcastChannelId` links back), offline rule "only a downloaded episode plays" (`markUnplayableOffline` `:117`, hide setting `:118`, `useEpisodeUnavailable.ts:17-19`).
- Show ⋯ menu (`src/components/PodcastMenuSheet.tsx:150-262`): Play, Shuffle (dealt list `:158-163`), Play next, Add to queue (`:164-177` — episode queue into the global queue EXISTS), Download all episodes (`:181-197`, offline-guard toast `:186-189`), Refresh (`:199-204` with feed error message `feedErrorMessage :337-343`), Website (`:206-211`, `siteUrl` parsed `podcastFeed.ts:269-281`), heart add/remove (`:218-231`), Delete downloads (`:238-254`, record-driven `downloadedForChannel :312-317`), **Remove from this device** = full unsubscribe incl. files, hearts, downloads, art (`:256-297` → `removeChannel podcasts.ts:293-296` → `podcastDb.removeChannel :388-404`).
- Show download pill: one tap = download all (`:133-139` tap toggle/stop `podcast/[id].tsx`), whole-show download uses the DB not the screen (`downloads.ts:1470-1476` — "half-loaded show still downloads completely"), per-episode download/delete via the song menu (`src/components/SongMenuSheet.tsx:666-683, 818-834` — `downloadPodcastEpisode`, `deletePodcastDownloads`).
- Hearts: user's own SQLite favorites for shows AND episodes (`podcastDb.toggleFavorite :316-340`, read `favoriteIds :343-350`, favorite lists `:353-381`); rows light from one shared set (`usePodcastFavoriteIds` `src/lib/podcastFavorites.ts:12-19`; `TrackRow.tsx:51-55,154,177-179,312`, player header `src/app/player.tsx:396-397`, `heartedSong podcasts.ts:447-459`).
- Home integration: "Recent episodes" shelf (`src/app/(tabs)/index.tsx:547-585`), quick-grid podcast tiles from recents (`:152-190`), chips/sections (`:791,805,1183-1184`); Library Podcasts segment shows favorited shows + hearted single episodes with pin/dim/downloaded badges (`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:460-480+`, `src/components/PodcastRow.tsx:63-121`); global search matches shows by title/author (`search.tsx:203-210`).
- Playback: episodes queue like songs (`queueEpisode podcasts.ts:427-439`), speed sheet works on them (`vod` set `podcasts.ts:412`; `store/player.ts:2174-2176` keeps stations at 1×, lets `vod` files vary — PLAYBACK_SPEEDS `:2163`); episodes are NOT scrobbled (server listens skip url'd songs `store/player.ts:1307`).
- Storage accounting: per-episode bytes recorded at download (`podcastDb.saveDownload :411-426`), summed by SQL (`downloadedBytes :464-474`), storage screen bucket "Podcast subscriptions" (`src/app/settings/downloads.tsx:354`), dir under app storage (`src/lib/appStorage.ts:40-43`).
- Orphan hygiene: episodes pulled from a feed drop their rows AND their download rows AND files (`podcastDb.replaceEpisodes :168-216`); clear-all-downloads also purges podcast files and rows (`downloads.ts:1600-1622`); profile deletion removes subscriptions (`podcasts.ts:1-8` note about `profileData.ts`).

---

## Gaps-Podcasts

1. **Episode show notes never displayed** — data path complete, UI missing. Feeds parse per-episode descriptions (`podcastFeed.ts:302-304`), DB stores them (`podcastDb.save… data blob :185-193`), but `episodeToSong` (`podcasts.ts:400-425`) drops `description`, and no screen renders it (TrackListView rows show title only). Backend (the feed) already supplies it. Priority **high** — cheapest high-value win. **(2026-10-22: open PR #278 fixes exactly this — notes + `Show more`/`Show less`, length · date on the rows. Do NOT re-implement; review/merge it instead.)**
2. **No in-show search, sort or selection**: the show screen passes neither `searchable` nor `onSort` nor `selection` to `TrackListView` (`podcast/[id].tsx:167-199` — compare playlist screen `[id].tsx:365-388`); episodes are feed-newest-first only (`sortEpisodes :326-337`, DB order `listAllEpisodes`). Old feeds "list thousands" (`podcastDb.ts:240-242`) — finding an episode means no tool. Priority **high**.
3. **No mark-as-played / played-state at all**: DB schema has no played column (`podcastDb.ts:22-59`); nothing marks episodes seen; hearting is the only state. (No resume-position either.) Backend: RSS has no played flag but the device is the store — fully implementable locally. Priority **high** (core podcast UX).
4. **No new-episode notification/unread concept**: refresh is only manual (`podcasts.tsx:123-143` pull-action, `refreshAll :368-383`), auto re-read happens only when the show screen opens (`[id].tsx:78-82`) — no periodic refresh on foreground (auto-download reconcileAll `AppStartupTab.tsx:79` covers playlists/favorites only; `autoDownloads.ts` has no podcast ids), no badge counts, no push (no `expo-notifications` in `package.json`). Priority **medium-high**. **(2026-10-22: PR #278 adds the per-show `new` arrival mark (`firstSeenAt`/`newSinceAt`) and the `Refresh (… ago)` clock — the per-show piece is in flight; unread counts across shows, foreground refresh-all and the Home badge are still missing.)**
5. **No auto-download of new episodes for subscribed/favorited shows**: `autoDownloads.ts` ids map holds server playlists + favorites sentinel only (`:29-32`); episode downloads are manual (`PodcastMenuSheet :181-197`, `SongMenuSheet :675-683`). Backend: local + feed, implementable via existing `downloadPodcast`. Priority **medium-high**. **(2026-10-22: PR #278 introduces `firstSeenAt`/`newSinceAt` — the exact "arrived with the last read" predicate such an auto-download rule needs; the data hook now exists on that branch.)**
6. **No storage cap / auto-prune for episode downloads**: bytes are only accounted (`podcastDb.downloadedBytes :464-474`, settings bucket `:354`); nothing limits or auto-trims. Backend: local, implementable. Priority **medium**.
7. **No RSS authentication / private feeds**: `get()` sends only accept + `user-agent: Resonus` + conditional headers (`podcasts.ts:99-109`); no Authorization/user-password path anywhere, no per-feed credentials. iTunes returns only public feeds (`:461-504`). Priority **medium** (paid/private feeds are a whole podcast ecosystem; the fetch layer can't even store a key).
8. **No Podcast 2.0 extensions: chapters, transcripts, season/episode numbers, `podcast:guid`, value-for-scenes**: parser reads a fixed tag set (`podcastFeed.ts:284-323`); `<podcast:chapters>` / `<podcast:transcript>` are ignored; no `<season>/<episodeNumber>` either; nothing renders the parsed description in-episode. The app even ships TTML/LRC machinery for music lyrics (`src/lib/ttml.ts`, `src/lib/lrc.ts`) that is never wired to episodes. Backend: feeds can supply both. Priority **medium** (chapters) / **low** (transcription).
9. **Podcasts can't leave the phone's speakers**: no podcast mentions in the cast/UPnP/car pipeline (`src/store/castMedia.ts`, `src/store/upnp.ts`, `src/lib/carAuto.ts`, `src/lib/audioRoute*` — zero matches). An episode is a plain file URL (`episodeToSong.url :410`) that cast supports generically for server songs. Priority **medium** (Chromecast/UPnP of an episode URL is feasible; CarPlay/queue-elsewhere paths exist for songs only).
10. **Episodes are excluded from playlists by design**: song menu hides "add to playlist" for episodes (`SongMenuSheet.tsx:553-556` "A podcast episode belongs to no playlist"), queue action filters them out (`queue.tsx:412-427`). The local `lp_` playlist engine (`localQueries.ts`) stores arbitrary ids and could hold episode ids, but `resolveSongs`/`annotate` (`data.ts:118-150`) resolve server catalogs — server-side gap for server playlists, self-inflicted for local. Priority **low**.
11. **iTunes chart region is hardcoded US**: `ITUNES_CHARTS = …/us/rss/toppodcasts/…` (`podcasts.ts:52`); no country/locale parameter even though the app has 9 locales (`src/i18n/locales/*`). iTunes lookup/search support `country=`. Priority **low-medium**.
12. **iTunes search depth fixed at 25 and no paging** (`podcasts.ts:48-51`), charts capped 100 (`:51`), Store rows key on feedUrl with no re-paging (`podcasts.tsx:230-234`). Priority **low**.
13. **Single enclosure per item only** (`enclosure() :222-239` returns the first match; no quality/format choices, no video type filtering — a video enclosure's URL would be filed as-is and played as "vod" song `:412`). Priority **low**.
14. **No episode delete granularity from the show screen itself** beyond the pill (whole-show remove) and song menu per episode; no "delete all except last N" utility. Priority **low**.
15. **`listAllEpisodes` materializes the entire show in memory per access** (`podcastDb.ts:261-270` loop; screens `podcasts.tsx` menu, `downloads.ts:1473`) — DB paging exists (`listEpisodes :242-259`, 200/page) but no screen pages. Priority **medium** (perf on huge feeds, cf. the 5 MB Radiolab note `podcasts.ts:192-194`).

---

## Bugs-Podcasts

1. **Feed error written under a possibly-wrong feed key on first-add failures is avoided, but the reverse hazard exists**: `refreshChannel` on failure saves the error onto the *stored* row (`podcasts.ts:213-223`), keyed by the original address — fine; however a row whose feed *moved* (304 conditional path requires `stored.feedUrl === url` `:204`) loses its validators (`:204-206` comment) — every re-read of a moved feed pays the full body (up to 5 MB, `:193-195`). Performance defect, provable. **Medium.**
2. **`refreshAll` counts `failed === total` before any partial success messaging nuance**: `podcasts.tsx:138-142` — if all feeds fail it says "Couldn't reach any of your podcast feeds." even when the failure is HTTP not offline (code `'offline'`/`'notFeed'`/`'http'` stored per row `podcasts.ts:31-39` but refreshAll discards codes `:378-380`). The row-level error display (`podcasts.tsx:316-319`) covers UX, but the toast conflates causes — the per-row `Couldn't read this feed` never distinguishes a *bad feed* vs offline in the list (`feedErrorMessage :337-343` only splits offline). **Low.**
3. **Episode description exists in DB but is unreachable** — see Gaps #1 (a stored-data feature lost purely at the render layer; `toEpisode :173-181` keeps it, `episodeToSong` drops it). Restated here as the bug-shaped claim: parsing work is done and paid for, then thrown away. **High** (same as gap #1). **(PR #278 makes the DB field visible via an `episodeInfo` Map on the show's rows after merge; on main it is still lost.)**
4. **`listRecentEpisodes` and Home shelf ignore episodes without url** — good (`podcastDb.ts:285 WHERE e.url IS NOT NULL`); but the show screen's download group counts only `e.url` episodes (`[id].tsx:98 episodeIds = … filter((e) => e.url)`) with the comment `:95-97` — consistent. No bug; noted.
5. **Show-heart reuses `type: 'song'`** with a `podcast: true` flag (`podcast/[id].tsx:177`, consumed `TrackListView.tsx:1003`) — no server star is written (podcast hearts never call `star()` — grep `togglePodcastHeart` only hits SQLite paths `podcasts.ts:327-335`), so no leakage; but the type overload makes future `StarType` handling risky. **Info.**
6. **Remove-from-device while on another show's screen**: menu acts on the *target* channel and only pops back when `pathname.startsWith('/podcast/')` (`PodcastMenuSheet.tsx:69,280-295`) — removing a show opened from a *feed address* (`/podcast/<encoded url>`) backs to `/podcasts` (`:283-285`); the subsequent `removeChannel` (`:289`) plus `invalidatePodcastCaches` (`:290-291`) prevents the immediate re-file loop (comment `:280-281`). Verified sound; flagging the feed-address branch for test. **Low.**
7. **Favorites lists can go stale against removed shows**: `listFavoriteEpisodes` joins live episodes (`podcastDb.ts:365-381`), so hearted episodes of a removed show vanish — but `removeChannel` deletes favorites by `channel_id` (`:395-396`) only; a heart whose `channel_id` predates a feed-move re-key (`refreshChannel` re-files id under `feedId(read.url)` `:237-238`) leaves orphan favorite rows (never re-joined, never cleaned). Unbounded rows in `podcast_favorites`. Provable leak, cosmetic impact. **Low.**
8. **No `If-Modified-Since` when an etag exists but moved**: single-validator policy is deliberate (`podcasts.ts:103-107` comment) — documented tradeoff, not a bug.
9. **Podcast screen has no Undo toast for destructive ops**: `deletePodcastChannelDownloads` fires from the header pill second tap (`[id].tsx:136`) and menu row (`:239-253`) with no undo, whereas playlist download-delete has a confirm Dialog (`[id].tsx:622-636`). UX asymmetry. **Medium.**
10. **`PodcastsScreen.refresh()` reports nothing on total==0** (`podcasts.tsx:138` guards) — fine; but manual-offline early-return `:126-129` is the *only* offline guard for the pull action while per-feed failures offline toast per refresh-all (`:141`) — consistent enough. **Info.**

---

## Cross-cutting notes
- Playlists: fully mirrored, auto-prefetched, offline-editable, auto-downloaded. Podcasts: fully local, no offline queue needed, but refresh/auto-download are far weaker (see Gaps 4/5).
- Both entities share the `TrackListView` renderer and the queue (`playQueue` source hrefs `/playlist/<id>` `[id].tsx:422`, `/podcast/<id>` `[id].tsx:163`, `podcast/[id].tsx:163`), which is what feeds Home recents (`lastPlayed` forgetMissing for playlists `data.ts:969-979`).
- Neither entity participates in cast/UPnP/car (no matches in those files for either), and neither has an m3u/OPML import or export path.


---

# PART 7 — Albums, artists, genres, favorites — inventory, gaps, bugs

# E — Feature completeness: Albums · Artists · Genres · Favorites · Browse

Branch `main` @ `b1075627`, Expo SDK 57 (`AGENTS.md:1`). Read-only sweep; no source
touched. Every claim carries `file:line`. Gaps are labelled:

* **[N]** = the data is already returned by the current calls — **no new backend call needed**.
* **[C]** = needs a new/extra backend call or a field the API layer never models.
* **[X]** = not expressible against this API surface at all.

Files swept in full: `src/app/album/[id].tsx`, `src/app/artist/[id].tsx`,
`src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx`, `src/app/artist/songs/[id].tsx`,
`src/app/artist-radio/[id].tsx`, `src/app/genre/[name].tsx`, `src/app/genres.tsx`,
`src/app/browse/{artists,albums,songs}.tsx`, `src/app/browse/folder/[id].tsx`,
`src/app/favorites.tsx`, `src/app/favorites-add.tsx`, `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx`,
`src/hooks/{useAlbumSort.tsx,useBrowseSort.ts,useSongSort.tsx,useServerSort.tsx,useFavoriteIds.ts,useGenreArt.ts}`,
`src/lib/{releaseGroups.ts,artistAlbums.ts,librarySort.ts}`, `src/store/{pins.ts,sortPrefs.ts}`,
`src/components/{AlbumCard,AlbumRow,ArtistCard,ArtistRow,GenreCard,TrackRow,TrackListView,SortSheet,BrowseToolbar,BrowseFrame,MediaMenuSheet}.tsx`,
`src/api/{data,backend,subsonic,navidrome,jellyfin}.ts`.

---

## 0. The capability layer (who decides an order exists)

This is the hinge for every "is this feature missing, or is the server to blame"
judgement below.

| Order family | Deciding code | What it offers |
|---|---|---|
| Album browse orders | `src/app/browse/albums.tsx:76-83` (6 of 8 `AlbumListType`s), persisted via `src/hooks/useBrowseSort.ts:13-35` | recent, frequent, newest, byYear, alphabeticalByName, random |
| Navidrome native album lists | mapping declared in `src/api/data.ts` (used at `:303 const native = ND_ALBUM_LIST[type]`, `:313 canListNative(a)`) | see below |
| Artist browse orders | `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:73-79`, client-side only (`:62-67` comment) | recent, frequent, newest, alpha, random |
| Song browse orders | `src/app/browse/songs.tsx:367` ← `songListSorts()` in `src/api/data.ts` | server/frequent/alpha/added/recent/random |
| Genre album orders | `src/api/data.ts:426-436 genreAlbumSorts()` — **Navidrome only** (`canListNative`, `src/api/data.ts:520-522`) | 6 orders |
| Genre song orders | `src/api/data.ts:736-741 genreSongSorts()` — **Navidrome OR Jellyfin** (`:739`) | 6 orders |
| Library sorts | `src/lib/librarySort.ts:15-19` (recent/added/alpha) + `src/store/settings.ts:1226` `librarySort`, `:1228 libraryLayout` | 3 orders, list or grid |
| Album list sorts (artist/discography) | `src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:26 FIELDS` = year, alpha only; persisted `src/store/sortPrefs.ts:15 AlbumSortField` | 2 orders |
| Song list sorts (favorites/albums) | `src/hooks/useSongSort.tsx:33 DEFAULT_FIELDS` = recent/alpha/artist/album/downloaded | 5 orders |
| Server sorts (genre) | `src/hooks/useServerSort.tsx:61-108` — **deliberately unpersisted** (`:78-80` comment) | per-visit |

Server capability matrix, verified per backend:

* **Navidrome** is the only backend with native album lists: chosen at `src/api/data.ts:303`
  (`const native = ND_ALBUM_LIST[type]`) and `:313 canListNative(a)`, where
  `src/api/data.ts:520-522 canListNative` is `serverType === 'navidrome' && (ndPassword ?? password)`.
* **Jellyfin** implements `getAlbumList` for **all 8** `AlbumListType`s including `starred`
  (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:413-425`, `:445 ...ALBUM_SORT[type]`) and can sort a genre's albums
  (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:463-481`, incl. `:480 ...ALBUM_SORT[sort]`, `:481 SortOrder`) — a capability
  the UI never uses (see G-3).
* `src/api/backend.ts:104-114` already carries `sort`/`dir` into `getAlbumsByGenre`
  ("Jellyfin's alone, like the songs below", `backend.ts:110-111`) — the dispatcher is ready,
  `src/api/data.ts:479-497 subsonicGenreAlbums` throws the arguments away (called from `:466`).
* Album model fields the unified layer already carries: `src/api/subsonic.ts:223-295`
  (`userRating:230`, `songCount:238`, `year:239`, `starred:240`, `originalReleaseDate:247`,
  `releaseDate:250`, `created:256`, `played:258`, `playCount:260`, `genres:264`,
  `recordLabels:266`, `releaseTypes:278`, `isCompilation:281`, `explicitStatus:288`, `discTitles:294`).
* Artist model fields: `src/api/subsonic.ts:307-327` (`albumCount:311`, `starred:312`, `sortName:318`, `userRating:326`).
* Genre model fields: `src/api/subsonic.ts:808-811` (`songCount:810`, `albumCount:811`), passed through
  untouched by `src/api/subsonic.ts:814-817 getGenres`, mapped by Jellyfin at `src/api/jellyfin.ts:459`.
* Song model fields incl. `artists/albumArtists/contributors` `src/api/subsonic.ts:97-101`,
  `starred:108`, `playCount:110`, `userRating:112`.

Backend field-fidelity gaps (proof for the "[N]/[C]" labels below):

* `src/api/jellyfin.ts:362-381 toAlbum` **omits** `userRating`, `releaseTypes`, `isCompilation`,
  `discTitles`, `recordLabels`, `originalReleaseDate`/`releaseDate`, `explicitStatus`; it **does** fill
  `songCount:373`, `year:374`, `starred:375` (`favDate`), `created:376`, `played:377`, `playCount:378`, `genres:379`.
* `src/api/jellyfin.ts:383-394 toArtist` **omits `sortName` and `userRating`**.
* `src/api/jellyfin.ts:814 setRating` is a no-op → the app gates rating on
  `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:138` (`serverType !== 'jellyfin'`) and `src/components/MediaMenuSheet.tsx:108`.
* No album-level MusicBrainz/release id anywhere (`src/api/subsonic.ts:223-295` has none; only
  `Song.musicBrainzId:138`) → duplicate-edition collapsing is **[C]/[X]**.

## 1. ALBUMS

### Inventory

**Album detail — `src/app/album/[id].tsx`**
* Header meta line: `Album · {year} · {n} songs · {duration}` — `:259-262`, passed at `:277`.
* Artist subtitle (`:268`), artist *list* (`:271 artists={data.album.artists}`), optional artist photo
  (`:272-276`, setting `:133 showArtistPhoto`).
* Optional description from `album.comment` or a comment every track repeats (`:219-229`), gated by
  `:136 showDescription` → `:269`.
* Genre chips, server list first then per-track union, deduped, cap 50 (`:100 MAX_GENRES`,
  `:102-121 albumGenres`), gated `:135 showGenreChips` → `:279 genres`.
* Advisory: album's own `explicitStatus` else "any track is explicit" (`:254-256`) → `:278`.
* Disc headers: title or "Disc N", `discNumber` or track-number-reset inference (`:43-82 discHeadersFor`,
  `:178-184` memo) → `:288`.
* Release/label line `℗ {year} {labels}` (`:215-218 recordLabels`) rendered in the footer at `:305-315`.
`* Heart: `:289-293 favorite` reads the shared favorites list via `:149 useFavoriteIds(canFetch,'album')`
  (fallback `album.starred`) — the `useFavoriteIds.ts:8-12` staleness note explains why.
* Download group state + dialog: `:188 groupDownloadState(s,'album:'+id,songIds)`, action `:196-200`,
  dialogs `:337-371` (download / remove / stop).
* Multi-select: add-to-playlist `:321`, download `:323-326` (no "remove": `:319` comment).
* Play / shuffle via `TrackListView.onPlay` (`:329-330`, `:231-237 playAlbum`), current-song marker `:286`.
* Footer shelf "More from this artist" (`:296-303 MoreFromArtist`) — an album→artist link already exists
  here, in addition to the long-press "Go to artist" (`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:393-415`).
* Cover viewer (`:332-336`, `:281 onCoverPress`); long-press → media menu (`:239-242`, `:284 onMenu`).
* Vanished-album exit (local only) `:164-167`, with the last-good freeze `:174-176`.

**Long-press menu — `src/components/MediaMenuSheet.tsx` (album branch)**
Play (`:289-293`), Shuffle (`:294-304`), screen-supplied extras (`:305-317`), Play next (`:318-327`),
Add to queue (`:328-337`), Add to a playlist (`:338-342`), Download (`:343-351`, size dialog `:478-492`),
Export (`:355-357`, dialog `:496-514`), Share (`:361-372`), Animated artwork (`:375-389`), Go to artist
(`:393-415` incl. `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:144` pin/unpin block, `:462-468` delete downloads,
`:257-286` the `StarRating` row and `:423-431` its entry (`canRate:108`).
Songs are fetched with the *same* query key the screen uses (`:63-76 fetchSongs`) — cache shared.

**Cards/rows**
* `src/components/AlbumCard.tsx:23-59` — cover, name, explicit badge, artist; long-press → menu `:38-41`; memoized `:21-22`.
* `src/components/AlbumRow.tsx:28-74` — cover, name, explicit badge, artist, optional pin icon `:59-61`;
  long-press `:47-50`; `pinned` is a caller prop (`:23-26`, rationale `:4-6`).
* Library album rows/cards: `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:640-778 AlbumsTab` (starred albums only),
  pinned first via `:672 withPins`, grid or list `:1036-1051 LayoutToggle`.

### Gaps

**G-1 [N] `starred` is never an album order.** `AlbumListType` has `starred`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:681`), Jellyfin serves it with `Filters: 'IsFavorite'`
(`src/api/jellyfin.ts:424`), Navidrome's native list mapping covers it
(`src/api/data.ts:303 ND_ALBUM_LIST`), and the multi-library merge sorts it
(`src/api/data.ts:2046-2048 return … sort by (b.starred …)`). `src/api/data.ts:295-296 getAlbumList`
will happily page it. But `src/app/browse/albums.tsx:76-83 SORTS` lists 6 keys and the dropped
`alphabeticalByArtist` is justified at `:72-75` while **`starred` is never mentioned**. Result:
"your favourite albums" can only be read from the Library (`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:654-780`),
never browsed A-Z or interleaved with the rest of the catalogue.

**G-2 [N] A genre's albums have one order on Subsonic, six on Navidrome, and the menu is
invisible on Jellyfin.** `src/app/genre/[name].tsx:145 useServerSort(auth ? genreAlbumSorts() : [], …)` —
`genreAlbumSorts` (`src/api/data.ts:426-436`) returns `[]` unless `canListNative` (`:520-522` Navidrome-only),
so Jellyfin gets no sort control at all (`useServerSort.tsx:81-83` "no menu to open"). Yet
`src/api/jellyfin.ts:463-481` accepts `sort`+`dir` and `src/api/backend.ts:104-114` forwards both.
The songs side already does it: `src/api/data.ts:736-741 genreSongSorts()` includes Jellyfin
(`:739 a.serverType !== 'jellyfin' && !canListNative(a)`). One-line asymmetry inside one screen
(`genre/[name].tsx:134-148` puts the two hooks side by side).

**G-3 [N] Album play counts / last-played are dead data.** `Album.playCount`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:260`), `Album.played` (`:258`) are filled by Navidrome and by Jellyfin
(`src/api/jellyfin.ts:377-378`), and `src/api/data.ts:1971-1977 ALBUM_SORT_FIELD` already uses
`played`/`playCount` for the *recent* and *frequent* shelves — but no album surface ever displays them.
Grep across `src/app` and `src/components` finds `playCount` only for songs
(`src/components/SongInfoSheet.tsx:208 t('Plays'), song.playCount`) and rating for songs
(`SongInfoSheet.tsx:209`, `src/components/TrackRow.tsx:315-317`). So Home's "Most played"
proves the data is on screen, while the album it opens says nothing about plays.

**G-4 [N] Album rating is only reachable inside the long-press sheet; album rows/cards never show it.**
`src/components/TrackListView.tsx` has **no** rating affordance at all (grep for `rating`/`Rating` in that
file: 0 hits), and `src/app/album/[id].tsx:266-330` passes no rating prop — meanwhile
`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:836-838 <StarRating … rating={data.artist.userRating}` paints stars on the
artist screen. The album's stars can be set but never seen where the album is open
(`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:266-286` reads `album.userRating` from cache, `src/api/subsonic.ts:230`).
Asymmetric affordance between two sibling entities, no new call needed.

**G-5 [N] Release date is displayed as a bare year.** `Album.originalReleaseDate`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:247`) and `releaseDate` (`:250`) are read for *sorting* only
(`src/api/data.ts:1989-1993 releaseValue`, used by `:2005-2009 byRelease`/`:2055 byYear`), while the
album header prints `data.album.year` (`src/app/album/[id].tsx:260`) and the label line repeats it
(`:216-217`). Every "New releases" shelf is already ordered by day but nothing on screen can show it.

**G-6 [N] An album never says what kind of release it is.** `Album.releaseTypes`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:278`) and `isCompilation` (`:281`) drive the artist's shelves
(`src/lib/releaseGroups.ts:140-166 isTyped/releaseGroupOf`, `:187-202 groupArtistAlbums`,
rendered at `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:684-692`) but the album's own header
(`src/app/album/[id].tsx:259-330`) has nothing — no "Single"/"Live"/"Compilation" marker, and therefore
no way to jump from one edition to the rest of its set.

**G-7 [N] Multi-disc artwork is carried but never drawn.** `Album.discTitles[].coverArt`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:294`, per-disc art) is ignored: `src/app/album/[id].tsx:52-55 titleOf` reads
`.title` only, and `:280 coverUri` uses the album cover for the whole list. Navidrome sends it.

**G-8 [N] Genre cards under-report and over-count.**
`src/app/genres.tsx:84` hands `GenreCard` only `albumCount`
(`src/components/GenreCard.tsx:105-111 signature`, rendered `:157-161`) though `Genre.songCount`
exists and is already fetched (`src/api/subsonic.ts:810-811`, `src/api/jellyfin.ts:459 g.songCount`).
Also `src/app/genres.tsx:77` sorts A-Z only — the cards carry counts (`:158-160`) yet there is no
"biggest genre first" order and no sort control at all on the screen.

**G-9 [N] Genre lists have no pin affordance, genre screen has no favorite.** `src/store/pins.ts:3`
keys are `playlist:`/`album:`/`radio:`; `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:142 pinKey` builds only `album:`/`playlist:`.
Fine for genres (**[X]**: the server has no starred-genre concept — `StarType` at
`src/api/subsonic.ts:1339` is song/album/artist/playlist).

**G-10 [C] Album editions / duplicate releases cannot be collapsed.** No album-level
identifier in the model (`src/api/subsonic.ts:223-295`, nothing MusicBrainz-shaped); the only id is
`Song.musicBrainzId` (`src/api/subsonic.ts:138` = recording mbid). So the "same album in 3 editions"
problem the discography shelves currently paper over (`src/lib/releaseGroups.ts:198 if (byGroup.size === 1 …)`)
would need `album.musicBrainzReleaseGroupId` (OpenSubsonic) — a new field/call, **[C]**.

**G-11 [C] Starred playlists are invisible everywhere.** `StarType` includes `playlist`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:1339`), starring them works (`src/api/data.ts:1303-1313 star`), and Navidrome can
list them (`src/api/navidrome.ts:531-535 listStarredPlaylistIds`, read by `src/hooks/usePlaylistStars.ts:69`),
but `src/api/data.ts:1278-1283` explicitly drops them from `getStarred` ("Nowhere to put it"). No screen
lists them; on Jellyfin even the listing is unimplemented (**[C]** for both Navidrome and Jellyfin: the
call exists for Navidrome, none for Jellyfin).

### Bugs / inconsistencies (all provable from source)

**B-1 [N] `Show all` is offered when nothing is hidden.**
`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:950-972 AlbumRow` draws `albums.slice(0, ROW_LIMIT)` (`:952`, `ROW_LIMIT = 50` at
`:97`) but shows the "Show all" link at `:961` on `albums.length > 2`. The comment at
`:958-960` reasons about "a couple of records are all on screen" — the constant it means is
`ROW_LIMIT`, not 2. With 3–50 albums the header advertises a link into
`/artist/discography/{id}` (`:694`) / `?group=` (`:690`) that contains exactly what is already on
screen. Affects both shelves: `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:684-692` (release groups) and `:697-703` ("Appears on").

**B-2 [N] Two different A–Z orders for the same artists.** Library's Artists tab sorts
by raw name (`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:408 (a) => a.name` inside `sortItems`, `src/lib/librarySort.ts:42-62`),
Browse Artists sorts by sort-name (`src/app/browse/artists.tsx:207 const byName = byArtistSortName`,
`src/lib/librarySort.ts:160-167`), and the merged server list is pre-sorted with the latter
(`src/api/data.ts:378 merged.sort(byArtistSortName)`). `Artist.sortName` is already fetched
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:318`). "The Knife" sits under T in one screen and K in the other
(the `#275` rationale lives at `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:206`).

**B-3 [N] Artists cannot be pinned, albums can.** `src/store/pins.ts:3` has no `artist:` key,
`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:142` builds no artist key, and `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:385-454 ArtistsTab`
never calls `withPins` — while `PlaylistsTab` does (`:299`), `AlbumsTab` does (`:672`) and `AllTab` does
(`:863`, `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:221 withPins`). Favourite artists therefore cannot be lifted out of
an alphabetical list of hundreds, and `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:221-250` has no affordance to pin them
from either (`:896-904 onLongPress` opens the media menu, which has no pin action for artists).

**B-4 [N] Artist tiles are the only interactive tiles in the app with no long-press menu.**
`src/components/ArtistCard.tsx:26-37` and `src/components/ArtistRow.tsx:27-42` have no `onLongPress`,
no `albumCount` on the card (`ArtistCard.tsx:32-34` vs `ArtistRow.tsx:38 albumsLabel(artist.albumCount…)`),
and no star indicator — whereas `src/components/AlbumCard.tsx:38-41` / `AlbumRow.tsx:47-50` long-press
into `MediaMenuSheet` (`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:78-537`). Consequence: from Browse Artists
(`src/app/browse/artists.tsx:316-318 renderItem`) and Library grid
(`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:429-436 renderItem` → `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:1054-1111 GridCard`),
there is **no way to star an artist** except by opening every artist (`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:562 starred=`
is the only star affordance for the entity). Same for the album cards on browse screens:
`AlbumCard`/`AlbumRow` never show `album.starred` (`src/api/subsonic.ts:240`) even though
`src/hooks/useFavoriteIds.ts:37` exists and is already used at `src/app/album/[id].tsx:149`.

**B-5 [N] Discography rows are a fourth, hand-rolled album row.**
`src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx:196-206` builds its own `<Pressable>` printing name + year
(`:203 {item.year ? …}`) — but not artist, not the explicit badge, and **no `onLongPress`**, whereas
`src/components/AlbumRow.tsx:47-50` has the menu and `:57-67` artist + badge. The grid branch
(`:193-194 <AlbumCard …/>`) *does* get the menu (`AlbumCard.tsx:38-41`). So the same entity loses its
menu depending on layout, and three album-row renderers disagree on what an album shows
(`AlbumRow` year-less, `AlbumCard` year-less, discography row year-only).

**B-6 [N] "Yearless" albums sort to opposite ends in the two places that order an artist's records.**
`src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:58` treats them as `year ?? 0` (deliberate, justified at
`:56-57` "the split that feeds these lists says the same", i.e. `src/lib/artistAlbums.ts:4 (b.year ?? 0) - (a.year ?? 0)`)
— but `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:419 (a.year ?? Infinity) - (b.year ?? Infinity)` sends them **last**
when building the chronological "Play discography" queue. Two documented intents, one entity, opposite
answers; the row you can see (`useAlbumSort`) and the queue you hear (`:418-421`) disagree.

**B-7 [N] `useAlbumSort` has no year-with-date option and only two fields.**
`src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:26 FIELDS = ['year','alpha']` persisted as
`src/store/sortPrefs.ts:15 AlbumSortField = 'year'|'alpha'`. The data for a better order is already
there (`src/api/data.ts:1989-1993 releaseValue`, `:2005-2009 byRelease`), and the discography
screens are exactly where "New releases" ties are most visible
(`src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx:212 sortSheet`).

**B-8 [N] Genre counts contradict the list they head.**
`src/app/genre/[name].tsx:326-338` prints `counts.albumCount · counts.songCount` from
`getGenres` (`:327-329`, `src/api/data.ts:393-396` — the comment at `:393` says it is **global**,
"the API doesn't filter genres by library", `src/api/subsonic.ts:814-817` takes no folder param),
while the albums under it are library-scoped (`src/api/data.ts:485-487 enabledFolderIds(a)` →
`Subsonic.getAlbumsByGenre(…, ids[i])`). On any multi-library profile with a folder disabled the header
is wrong. Fixing the *display* needs extra calls (**[C]**: one `getAlbumList2 byGenre` per folder per
genre, or a new endpoint) — the inconsistency itself is provable now.

**B-9 [N] "Download this genre" silently truncates at 5000 songs.**
`src/app/genre/[name].tsx:87 GATHER_CAP = 5000`, loop breaks at `:223 if (page.length < GATHER_PAGE ||
all.length >= GATHER_CAP) break` and returns `all` (`:225`); the dialog then quotes
`useDownloadMessage(pending ?? [])` (`:166`, dialog `:478-492`-equivalent in this screen) with no
"…and there are more" notice. Same for `src/app/artist/songs/[id].tsx:13` comment claiming a full
discography — the artist path has no such cap, the genre path does.

**B-10 [N] `MediaMenuSheet` offers an album rating that the album screen can't confirm.**
`MediaMenuSheet.tsx:266-269` falls back to `album.userRating` from the item it was opened with;
`src/app/album/[id].tsx:239-242 openAlbumMenu` hands over `data.album` straight from
`getAlbum` (`:151-155`), so a rating set in the sheet is visible in the sheet
(`:273-283 write-back`) but there is no header stars to compare it against (see G-4/B-4).
Not a crash — a confirmation gap.

**B-11 [N] Album screen shows `℗ {year} {labels}` (`src/app/album/[id].tsx:215-218`) with a
"·"-joined label list that can be empty-of-year yet still print `℗`** — `:217` guards the year
but not the fact that `℗` is the *label* glyph while the string leads with a date; on a label-only
release with no year the line reads `℗ Impulse Records` (fine) but with a year and no label it prints
nothing at all (`:215 labels.length` gate), so the album's year disappears from the footer and
appears only in the meta line (`:260`). Cosmetic; listed for completeness.

**Deliberate, not bugs** (do not report): server sorts unpersisted
(`src/hooks/useServerSort.tsx:78-80`); `alphabeticalByArtist` dropped from the Albums menu
(`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:72-75`); no play button beside Browse Artists
(`src/app/browse/artists.tsx:290-291`); `genreAlbumSorts()` empty on plain Subsonic
(`src/api/data.ts:398-436` + `:430-434`); `AlbumRow.pinned` optional
(`src/components/AlbumRow.tsx:4-6`); `getAppearsOn` name-search approximation
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:1104-1142`, incl. `:1105-1107 isRemixer` and `:1138 confirmed`,
consumed by `src/lib/artistAlbums.ts:16-28 splitArtistAlbums` / `:20 confirmedIds`,
`:25-26` own/guest split); rating suppressed on Jellyfin
(`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:138`, `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:106-108` matching
`src/api/jellyfin.ts:814` no-op).

## 2. ARTISTS

### Inventory

**Artist detail — `src/app/artist/[id].tsx` (1095 lines)**
* Header: full-bleed artist photo that opens on tap (`:527-541`, `setPhotoOpen`), name over it
  (`:549-555 Animated.Text`), `FavoriteButton` heart (`:559-564 type="artist"`, state from
  `:295 useFavoriteIds(canFetch,'artist')` → `:562`), download button + dialogs
  (`:567-…`, gate `:567` `!offline && albums.length > 0`). **Nothing in the header states how big
  the artist is** — no `albumCount`, no plays (see G-14). `StarRating` row `:836-857`
  (rating read `:838 data.artist.userRating`, write-back `:843-847`), entered from
  `:908-914` gated by `canRate` (`:138`).
* Sections: "Popular" top tracks with `:654 top.slice(0, songsExpanded ? 10 : 5)` and
  Show more/less `:665-675`; "All songs" copy at `:648`; release-group shelves
  `:684-692` (via `src/lib/releaseGroups.ts`), single "Discography" shelf `:694`,
  "Appears on" `:697-703`; "About/ biography" `:705-726` (clamp `:708 numberOfLines 4`,
  expand `:711-720`); "Similar artists" `:727-…`.
* Actions sheet: `:859-863 Play discography`, `:865-873 Go to artist radio`
  (gate `:867 offline || !radiosEnabled`, `src/app/artist-radio/[id].tsx:1-11` builds
  the definition via `src/lib/radioBuild.ts buildRadioDef/radioTracks`), download
  (`:799 startDownload`, `:424-441 gatherSongs/startDownload`), delete downloads
  (`:811`, `:403-417`), Rate (`:908-914`).
* Plays: `:316-325` play from popular or from current track, shuffle variant
  `:334-351` ("own albums only; Appears on is left out" comment `:334`).
* Discography chronology for playing: `:418-421`.

**Sub-screens**
* `src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx` (242): full own+guest list with
  `?group=` / `?section=appears-on` params (`:690`, `:701`), grid or rows
  (`:192-207`), sort via `useAlbumSort` (`:212 sortSheet`), columns via
  `useGridColumns`. No search box, no download, no selection, no play-everything.
* `src/app/artist/songs/[id].tsx` (521): every song of the artist,
  `:149 useSongSort(all, 'artistSongs:'+id, …)`, `:152 fields: ['recent','alpha','album','downloaded']`,
  download of the whole artist (`:159 groupDownloadState(…, 'artist:'+id, [])`, `:449 downloadArtist(id, all, albums)`),
  selection menu (`:188 useSelectionMenu`), multi-select dialogs (`:409 selectionMenu.dialogs`).
  Header comment `:6` promises "sorted, played, shuffled, downloaded or picked through"; `:13` explains
  the list shape is shared with the download path.
* `src/app/artist-radio/[id].tsx` (182): artwork edge-to-edge, radio colour tint
  (`:24 tintForMode`), tracks list, `:36-45 shuffled()` for shuffle, suggestions
  (`:22 SuggestedTracks`), long-press menu via `:21 openRadioMenu`; list is built once and
  stored (`src/store/radios.ts useRadios`), rationale `:7-11`.
* Artist tiles: `src/components/ArtistCard.tsx`, `src/components/ArtistRow.tsx` (see B-4).
* Library artists tab: `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:385-454 ArtistsTab` (starred artists only,
  `:397-401 ['starred']`, subtitle `albumsLabel(item.albumCount…)` at `:435`).

### Gaps

**G-12 [N] No artist "sort" affordance anywhere for the album shelves except year/alpha.**
Covered by B-7; additionally the artist screen offers no *label* grouping although
`src/api/subsonic.ts:266 recordLabels` is fetched for its own albums
(`src/app/album/[id].tsx:215` prints them) — the artist screen prints none
(`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:679-703`).

**G-13 [N] Starred artists are invisible on every tile.** `Artist.starred`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:312`) exists on both backends (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:388 starred: favDate(it)`),
`useFavoriteIds.ts:37 type='artist'` is already wired at `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:295`,
`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:853-861` lists them — but `ArtistCard.tsx:30-35` and
`ArtistRow.tsx:33-39` print neither heart nor date. Paired with G-1/G-4 this is the same missing
indicator for all three entities on browse tiles.

**G-14 [N] `Artist.albumCount` is wired into two tiles and out of two others.** Printed by
`ArtistRow.tsx:38 albumsLabel(artist.albumCount ?? 0, lang)` and by the Library grid
(`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:435 albumsLabel(item.albumCount ?? 0, lang)`); **absent** from
`ArtistCard.tsx:31-34` (cover + name only) and from the artist's own screen
(`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:549-555` prints the name, and grepping `albumsLabel` in that file finds
nothing) —   the place where the number matters most. Fetched either way
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:311`, requested at `src/api/jellyfin.ts:457 Fields: ITEM_COUNTS`, mapped at
`:459`), so no new call.

**G-15 [N] Artist A–Z on Jellyfin is silently name-order.**
`src/api/jellyfin.ts:383-394 toArtist` omits `sortName` → `byArtistSortName`
(`src/lib/librarySort.ts:160-167 ka = a.sortName || a.name`) falls back to the name, so "The Knife"
sorts under T on Jellyfin and under K on Navidrome. Same code, different server, different order —
the fallback is defensible, the divergence is the bug. (Jellyfin *does* expose `SortName`
elsewhere — see `src/api/jellyfin.ts:422 SortBy: 'SortName'`, `:419`/`:473` — so a
field mapping, not a new call, closes it: **[N]**.)

**G-16 [N] No "appears-on" sort control.**
`src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx:212 sortSheet` sorts the merged own+guest list through
`useAlbumSort` (`src/lib/artistAlbums.ts:4 byYear` pre-sort), so guests and own albums are
interleaved by year inside one shelf rather than by participation role; nothing orders by
"recently added to my library".

**G-17 [C] No way to browse "artists I never opened".** The inferred
"Recently added"/"Most played" artist orders (`src/app/browse/artists.tsx:154-182`) are derived
from albums only; an artist with no albums in the top pool (`:82 FREQUENT_POOL = 50`) scores 0
(`:171-180 scoreByBestAlbum`, `:211-217`) and falls into alphabetical order
(`:216` comment "the many artists with no plays or counted albums don't get an arbitrary order").
A better answer exists without a new endpoint by paging the album list deeper
(`src/api/data.ts:295-296 getAlbumList` paginates), so this is **[N]**-improvable; a true
artist play-count is **[X]** (Subsonic has none, `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:62-67`).

**G-18 [N] `favorites-add` cannot favourite an album or an artist.**
`src/app/favorites-add.tsx` is song-only end to end: candidate pools `getMostPlayedSongs`
(`:83`, cap 50), play history (`:121 history.map((e) => e.song)`), `getSimilarSongs`
(`:93`, 3 seeds × 20), `search(...).songs` (`:106`); the row toggle calls `star(song.id)` /
`unstar(song.id)` (`:135-136`) with the default type
(`src/api/data.ts:1303-1313 star(id, type?)`). `star` already accepts `'album'|'artist'`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:1339 StarType`) — no new call needed to add the other two tabs.

### Bugs / inconsistencies

**B-12 [N] "Show all" bug** — see B-1 (the discography screen exists precisely to be the target of
that link, and it opens when the link is a no-op).

**B-13 [N] `Appears on` can be silently incomplete.**
`src/api/subsonic.ts:1115-1121 getAppearsOn` uses `search3.view` with
`songCount: 200, albumCount: 0, artistCount: 0` and dedupes per album
(`:1123-1140`), so participation albums whose tracks fall past the 200-song window never appear;
`confirmed` (`:138`) only distinguishes *known-wrong* discography entries
(`src/lib/artistAlbums.ts:20 confirmedIds`, `:26 guest: guests.filter(a.confirmed || !ownIds.has(a.id))`).
Paginating the same endpoint closes it (**[N]**).

**B-14 [N] Similar-artist tiles inherit the artist card's missing affordances** —
`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:727-…` draws `ArtistCard`, so the "go to this artist's radio"/star path
available at `:865-873` is available for *this* artist only, never for the similar ones.

**B-15 [N] Artist screens disagree on the discography's order.** The artist screen's shelves come
pre-sorted `byYear` descending (`src/lib/artistAlbums.ts:4`, applied `:25-26`), then
`src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:44-59` re-sorts client-side on the **discography screen**
(default `{field:'year',dir:'desc'}` at `src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:29`), while the
*play-discography* queue is chronological ascending (`src/app/artist/[id].tsx:419`). Three
orders for one shelf family; at least one of them must be wrong for the user, and the yearless
edge (`:419 Infinity` vs `src/hooks/useAlbumSort.tsx:58 ?? 0`) makes it visible.

**B-16 [N] `src/app/artist/songs/[id].tsx:152 fields` drops `added` and `artist`** from
`src/hooks/useSongSort.tsx:33 DEFAULT_FIELDS` — correct for `artist` (one artist per row) but
`added` there means "reverse of server order" (`src/hooks/useSongSort.tsx:94-96`), which for an
artist's songs is the server's own track order reversed; silently unavailable while the Favorites
screen has it (`src/app/favorites.tsx:109-113`).

## 3. GENRES

Inventory covered above (cards `src/components/GenreCard.tsx:103-166` with derived hue
`:23-27 genreHue`, luminance solve `:68-92`, two fanned covers `:148-152`, count `:157-161`;
screen `src/app/genres.tsx` A-Z `:77`, filter `:78-80`, `useGenreArt` covers `:118`;
detail `src/app/genre/[name].tsx` tabs albums/songs, `useServerSort` ×2 `:134-148`,
one sort slot `:150`, download-the-genre gather `:212-232` w/ `GATHER_CAP`,
multi-select `:173-189`, header swap `:344-350`, counts `:331-338`).

Gaps and bugs: G-8, G-2, B-8, B-9 plus:

**G-19 [N] A genre has no album→song drill-down.** `src/app/genre/[name].tsx:301 getAlbumsByGenre(...)`
albums and `:315 getSongsByGenre` songs are two independent lists (documented at
`src/api/data.ts:499-504` "Not the same list as its albums") — there is no "this genre, grouped by
album" view, even though the genre's album list is right there and each album's songs are one shared
query away (`src/api/data.ts:349-359 getAlbum`). The songs side already defaults to album-then-disc-then-track
order (`src/api/data.ts:743-753`), so the grouping is display-only work.

**G-20 [N] Genre cards always fan out the alphabetically first two albums of the genre.**
`src/hooks/useGenreArt.ts:15 GENRE_ART_COUNT = 2`, requested at `:48 getGenreArt(name, GENRE_ART_COUNT)`,
and `src/api/data.ts:476 getGenreArt` picks the order itself — `'alpha'` whenever the server can sort
(`canListNative`), so the two covers on a card are A–Z's first, never the newest or most played.
The better orders already exist (`src/api/data.ts:438-467 getAlbumsByGenre` + `:426-436 genreAlbumSorts`).
Also `src/hooks/useGenreArt.ts:40-53` takes `albumCount` only to test `!== 0` (`:53`) — the count the
card knows is otherwise unused.

**B-17 [N] Genre album "Most played" label differs from the album screen's.**
`src/app/genre/[name].tsx:146 { frequent: 'Most played::albums' }` vs
`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:78 'Most played::albums'` (same key) but the genre also renames
`server` to 'By album' via `src/api/data.ts:743-753`/`useServerSort.tsx:32 server: 'Default'`
override at `genre/[name].tsx:136` — the browse screens say 'Recently played' for the *same
server order* (`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:77 recent`). Two names for one server order across two screens.

**B-18 [N] `src/app/genres.tsx:77-80` re-sorts A-Z on every query keystroke** and
`src/app/genres.tsx:83-86 renderGenre` is `useMemo`/`useCallback([])`-stable while
`GenreCard` is memoized (`GenreCard.tsx:103`) — fine; but the screen's own `key={columns}`
(`:136`) with `numColumns` (`:137`) means a rotation remounts the whole grid; noted as a
perf/UX edge, not a feature gap.

## 4. FAVORITES

Inventory: `src/app/favorites.tsx` — songs-only list from `getStarred` (`:63-67`), sort via
`useSongSort` `:109-113` (label swap `starredByDate()` `:112`), download group `'favorites'`
(`:75`, dialog `:201-204`, remove/stop `:95-97`), auto-download reconciliation (`:81-90`,
gate `:84 canAutoDownload`), batch unstar with undo (`:118-149 removeMany`,
`src/lib/favoritesCache.ts applyStarChange/resyncFavorites`), ⋯ sheet
(`:54 menuRef`, `:222-240+`: play next / add to queue / clear downloads / delete),
"Add to favorites" jump (`:199 addAction` → `src/app/favorites-add.tsx`), in-list search
(`:195-196 searchable`), indigo header (`:33-34 HEADER_COLOR`, `:191 accentColor`).
`src/app/favorites-add.tsx` — 3 tabs (`:41 Tab`, `:150-154 tabs`, `suggested` hidden offline
`:153`/`:100`), search debounce 300 ms (`:63-65`), ⊕/✓ row toggle (`:275-286 AddRow`,
`:126-148 toggle` w/ rollback `:138-146`), exclusion snapshot on entry (`:73-79 initialFavIds`),
radio rows filtered out (`:124 !s.url`).

Gaps: G-11 (starred playlists), G-18 (album/artist candidates) plus:

**G-21 [N] Favorites screen shows songs only.** `src/app/favorites.tsx:69 songIds = data?.songs ?? []`
and `:110 data?.songs` are the only reads; `getStarred` answers `{songs, albums, artists}`
(`src/api/data.ts:1300`), surfaced only in `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:842-861` and
`:403-414`. There is no Favorites entry point to your starred albums/artists and no tab bar, while
`src/app/favorites-add.tsx:150-154` proves the screen family already has a tab pattern. **[N]**.

**G-22 [N] No "Recently added" *date* order for favorites of albums/artists** — n/a (see G-21).
**G-23 [N] `favorites-add` never proposes a song from an album you starred.**
Candidate pools are `:83 getMostPlayedSongs(50)`, `:121 history`, `:93` similar-of-3-random-favorites.
`getStarred().albums` is already in hand on that very screen (`:67-71 starred = getStarred()`),
and `getAlbum` shares cache (`src/api/data.ts:349-359`) — so "songs from your starred albums" is
free of new calls. **[N]**.

**G-24 [N] Rating is invisible in the Favorites list even though the sort could use it.**
`src/hooks/useSongSort.tsx:33 DEFAULT_FIELDS` has no rating field; `Song.userRating`
(`src/api/subsonic.ts:112`) is displayed per row
(`src/components/TrackRow.tsx:315-317`, gated `showListRating`). Sorting favorites by rating
needs no new call (**[N]**) — declaring a field is enough.

Bugs:

**B-19 [N] A signed-in user with no favorites gets an empty page with no guidance.**
`src/app/favorites.tsx:169 if (displaySongs.length === 0 && offline)` returns the EmptyState only
offline; online the list is handed to `TrackListView` (`:187-218`) which passes no `emptyState`
prop, and `src/components/TrackListView.tsx:1173-1179` (`:1173 ListEmptyComponent`,
`:1174` search branch, `:1176 : emptyState ? <>{emptyState}</> : null`) renders **null**. Everything else is suppressed at the same
time (`:197 onMenu` needs length>0, `:198 onSort` needs length>1, `:201 download` needs length>0),
so the screen is a header, a search box, and nothing.

**B-20 [N] `favorites-add`'s "Most played" pool is a fixed 50 with no paging**
(`src/app/favorites-add.tsx:83 getMostPlayedSongs(50)`), while the songs browse screen pages the
same order (`src/app/browse/songs.tsx:367` sorts + `:373 play` via `src/components/BrowseToolbar.tsx:77`,
`BrowseToolbar.tsx:38 PLAY_SIZE = 200`). Same criterion, three different depths in the app
(50 / 200 / `GATHER_CAP` 5000 at `src/app/genre/[name].tsx:87`), none of them surfaced in a label.
`getMostPlayedSongs` takes the size as a parameter
(`src/api/data.ts:916 getMostPlayedSongs(size = 50)`), so raising 50 is free of new endpoints (**[N]**).

**B-21 [N] `favorites-add.tsx:138-146` rollback re-adds the id optimistically even when
`star()` succeeded but the response was slow** — bounded, cosmetic; noted, not asserted.

## 5. BROWSE (artists / albums / songs / folders + Explore embedding)

Inventory: `src/app/browse/albums.tsx` (PAGE 30 `:51`, `SORTS:76-83`, `useBrowseSort('browseAlbums')
:108-113`, shelf-driven initial sort via `:86-88 sortFromParam` + `:112`, infinite paging
`:124-136` with `:135 refetchOnMount` for `recent`, server search
`:156-165 searchAlbums(…,50)` `:67`, `BrowseToolbar :263-269`, grid/rows `:118-122`,
skeletons `:271-276`, `:299 onEndReached`), `src/app/browse/artists.tsx` (full index client-side
`:125-129 getArtists`, filter `:184-187`, 5 sorts `:73-79` `:203-218`,
inference pools `:154-182`, toolbar `:291` no play), `src/app/browse/songs.tsx`
(`:367` sorts, `:373 play={{sort,…}}`, selection `:369-372`), `src/app/browse/folder/[id].tsx`.

Gaps: G-1, G-17, G-25, G-26, G-27 plus the genre-screen gaps G-2/G-8/G-19/G-20:

**G-25 [N] The folder browser is the only list in the app with no order, no filter, no grid and no
selection.**
`src/app/browse/folder/[id].tsx:64-73 rows = dirs then songs` straight from
`getFolderIndexes`/`getMusicDirectory` (`:51-61`, one request `:53`), no `useBrowseSort`,
no `BrowseToolbar` (`src/components/BrowseToolbar.tsx:50-152` exists for exactly this row),
no grid (`:93-135 FlatList` single column), no selection. Its own comment at
`:165-167` claims "the folder view sorts by it [the year]" — there is no sort control in the file
(`dirYear` is display only, `:121`).

**G-26 [X] Folder rows cannot say anything an album row says.** `src/api/subsonic.ts:748-756 FolderEntry`
models only `id`/`name`/`coverArt`/`year` — no count, no artist, no rating — and the row prints
name + year + chevron (`src/app/browse/folder/[id].tsx:112-123`). Everything else the app shows for a
record needs an extra request per folder (**[C]**), and the year the comment at `:165-167` speaks of
is only carried, never sorted on (**[N]**: a client-side sort of `data.dirs` at `:64-73` is free).

**G-27 [N] Browse screens never show an album's `songCount` or full release date, and the
year appears on only one of four row renderers.** `src/api/subsonic.ts:238 songCount`,
`:239 year`, `:247 originalReleaseDate` are fetched (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:373/374` fills two of them)
but `AlbumCard.tsx:44-56` and `AlbumRow.tsx:52-69` print name + artist (+ badge, `:49`/`:62`), while
`src/app/artist/discography/[id].tsx:203` prints the year and `src/app/album/[id].tsx:260-262` prints
year and count. No new call.

Bugs:

**B-22 [N] Pressing Play on the Albums browse screen ignores the chosen order.**
`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:267 play={{ source: t('Library'), href: '/browse/albums' }}` passes
**no `sort`**, so `src/components/BrowseToolbar.tsx:77 getSongList(play.sort ?? 'server', PLAY_SIZE, 0)`
asks the server's default order while the toolbar label beside it reads "Most played"
(`:113-115`, options `:77-83`). The songs screen forwards it correctly
(`src/app/browse/songs.tsx:373 play={{ sort, … }}`), and the documented contract in
`BrowseToolbar.tsx:33-38` says "in the order the list is set to". Mapping exists
(`src/api/data.ts:1971-1977 ALBUM_SORT_FIELD`, `src/api/subsonic.ts:690-696 SongListSort`),
so no new call is needed for the 3 of 6 orders that translate.

**B-23 [N] Browse Artists' "Recently added"/"Most played" are inferred from 50 albums**
(`src/app/browse/artists.tsx:82 FREQUENT_POOL = 50`, `:155-156`/`:164-165` pools,
score `:176 FREQUENT_POOL - i`) — artists whose albums sit outside the pool score 0
(`:211-213 ?? 0`) and are silently alphabetical. The pools are single-shot
(`getAlbumList(…, FREQUENT_POOL)`), never paged (`src/api/data.ts:295-320` paginates), so
raising the pool or paging needs no new endpoint (**[N]**), whereas the *concept* is
unfixable exactly (Subsonic has no artist ordering, `:62-67`).

**B-24 [N] The folder screen answers "This folder is empty." when you have no server.**
`src/app/browse/folder/[id].tsx:42 canFetch = !!s.auth` (no `|| offline`, unlike
`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:100` / `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:97`), so the query is disabled
(`:60 enabled`), `data` stays undefined, `isLoading`/`isError` stay false and the list draws
`:134 ListEmptyComponent → t('This folder is empty.')`. Three browse siblings treat
"no account" as fetchable, one treats it as "an empty folder".

**B-25 [N] `src/app/browse/albums.tsx:290 key` = `` `${sort}-${layout}-${columns}` ``
while `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:309` and `src/app/browse/songs.tsx:398` do the same shape but the
artists screen's key omits the search state; `:290` remount is documented (`:286-289`). Not a bug —
recorded as the deliberate pattern so a reviewer doesn't flag it.

**B-26 [N] Artist "sort name" ordering is applied in two of three places** — see B-2
(`src/api/data.ts:378`, `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:207`, missing at
`src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:408`).

**B-27 [N] "Play" on a browse screen queues 200 songs (`BrowseToolbar.tsx:38 PLAY_SIZE`)
while the album/playlist headers queue the whole list** (`src/app/album/[id].tsx:231-237 data.songs`)
and genre's own download walks up to `GATHER_CAP`. Three different "how much does Play start"
answers inside the same app; the 200 cap is at least *not* surfaced in any label
(`BrowseToolbar.tsx:119-141`).

**B-28 [N] Explore's embedded browse frames hide the sort control while searching**
(`src/app/browse/albums.tsx:262 {isSearch ? null : <BrowseToolbar …>}`,
`src/app/browse/songs.tsx:365 {isSearch || selecting ? null : …}` — artists have no such second
condition), so the Artists section keeps a sort pill over a selection and the Songs section does
not. Same row, two rules.

## 7. Top 10 gaps (compact)

1. **Starred albums are not a browse order** though the whole pipeline supports it — `src/api/subsonic.ts:681`, `src/api/jellyfin.ts:424`, `src/api/data.ts:2046-2048`, `src/app/browse/albums.tsx:76-83`. No new call.
2. **A genre's albums get no sort menu on Jellyfin** while Jellyfin can sort them — `src/api/data.ts:426-436` vs `src/api/jellyfin.ts:463-481` + `src/api/backend.ts:104-114`. No new call.
3. **Album play counts / last played are never displayed** — `src/api/subsonic.ts:258-260`, `src/api/jellyfin.ts:377-378`, `src/api/data.ts:1971-1977`. No new call.
4. **Album rating can be set but never seen**: no rating in `TrackListView` (`src/components/TrackListView.tsx` grep = 0) vs artist stars at `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:836-838`. No new call.
5. **No star indicator on any browse tile** (albums or artists) — `AlbumCard.tsx:44-56`, `AlbumRow.tsx:52-69`, `ArtistCard.tsx:31-34`, `ArtistRow.tsx:33-39` vs `src/hooks/useFavoriteIds.ts:37`. No new call.
6. **Artists cannot be pinned** — `src/store/pins.ts:3`, `src/components/MediaMenuSheet.tsx:142`, `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:385-454` (no `withPins`, cf. `:672`). No new call.
7. **Artist A–Z disagrees across screens** (name at `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx:408` vs sort-name at `src/app/browse/artists.tsx:207`, `src/lib/librarySort.ts:160-167`) and Jellyfin never sends `sortName` (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:383-394`, though `SortName` is used at `:422`). No new call.
8. **Release kind of an album is only used for the artist's shelves** (`src/lib/releaseGroups.ts:140-166`) — the album's own header prints nothing (`src/app/album/[id].tsx:259-330`). No new call (Jellyfin: `toAlbum:362-381` omits the field).
9. **Genre counts are library-global but the lists are library-filtered** — `src/api/data.ts:393-396`, `src/api/subsonic.ts:814-817`, `src/app/genre/[name].tsx:331-338` vs `src/api/data.ts:485-487`. Needs extra calls to fix the display.
10. **Genre/favorites surfaces drop a field already fetched**: `Genre.songCount` (`src/api/subsonic.ts:811`, `src/api/jellyfin.ts:459`) never printed (`src/app/genres.tsx:84`, `GenreCard.tsx:105-111`); `favorites-add` never offers songs from your starred albums although `getStarred()` is already on that screen (`src/app/favorites-add.tsx:67-71`). No new call.

## 8. Provably deliberate (excluded from the report above)
