# PART 8 — Feature wishlist — what a music app should still add

# Resonus — Feature Wishlist Analysis (F-wishlist)

Generated 2026-10-21. All inventory claims spot-checked in `src/` (SDK 57, `expo ~57.0.15`).

---

## Section 1 — Verified feature inventory (what Resonus ALREADY has)

Confirmed from README.md "Features" **and** spot-checked in code:

| Feature | Code evidence (spot-check) |
| --- | --- |
| Multi-server / multi-profile, auto URL switching | `src/api/backend.ts` (dispatch by server type), `src/api/netGate.ts` |
| Local mode / local files | `src/app/offline.tsx`, local-http module |
| Offline mode (auto + manual) | `src/store/settings.ts:1028-1033` (`autoOfflineSwitch`), `src/api/netGate.ts` (`isManualOffline`) |
| Downloads (album/playlist/discography/song, bitrate/transcode, Wi-Fi-only, used-space disk bar) | `src/app/settings/downloads.tsx:1-90` (BITRATE_OPTIONS, TRANSCODE_FORMATS, disk bar) |
| Song cache w/ size limit + browsable cache screen | `src/app/settings/cache.tsx:48` (`songCacheLimitGb`), `src/lib/songCacheDb.ts` |
| Synced lyrics / karaoke / LRCLIB + lrc.red | `src/app/lyrics.tsx`, `src/app/settings/lyrics-providers.tsx` |
| Internet radio (managed stations) | `src/app/radio.tsx`, `src/app/settings/radios.tsx`, `src/store/radios.ts` |
| Casting: UPnP/DLNA + Sonos w/ room grouping | `src/store/upnp.ts`, `src/store/upnpRemoteSync.ts`, `modules/upnp-cast`, `docs/SONOS_INTEGRATION.md` — **no Chromecast** (no google-cast anywhere) |
| Gapless + crossfade | `src/store/player.ts:1902` (Gapless section), `player.ts:9` header |
| Built-in equalizer **with presets** | `src/app/settings/equalizer.tsx:43-90` (`presets`, `applyPreset`), `modules/audio-eq` — **Android-only** (`modules/audio-eq/expo-module.config.json:2`) |
| ReplayGain normalization | `src/store/player.ts` (album gain comment :2114); Jellyfin lacks per-track RG (`src/api/jellyfin.ts:353`) |
| Sleep timer, playback speed, queue undo, shuffle, repeat | `src/store/player.ts` (`shuffleOn:81`, `undo` in queue controls) |
| Autoplay / mixes (similar-songs continuation, affinity pools) | `src/store/player.ts:1658` ("affinity sources … single pool"), `getSimilarSongs` `src/api/data.ts:910` |
| Play history (per-profile, cap 100) | `src/store/playHistory.ts:9` comment, `:22` `MAX = 100` |
| Favorites, star ratings, pins, multi-select | `src/app/favorites*.tsx`, `StarType` in `src/api/subsonic.ts`, ratings surfaced (Jellyfin lacks: `jellyfin.ts:810`) |
| Queue sync across devices / saved queue | `src/api/subsonic.ts:1539` (`getPlayQueue`), `SavedQueue` :1492; Jellyfin: none (`jellyfin.ts:1101`) |
| Artist radio + similar artists | `src/app/artist-radio/[id].tsx`, `src/app/artist/[id].tsx:727` |
| Discography grouping by release type (album/single/EP/live/compilation…) | `src/lib/releaseGroups.ts:1-41` (MusicBrainz `releaseTypes`) |
| Podcasts as first-class media (feed parse, iTunes discovery, SQLite, episodes play like songs) | `src/api/podcasts.ts`, `src/lib/podcastFeed.ts`, `src/lib/podcastDb.ts`, `src/app/podcast/[id].tsx` |
| Scrobbling *rules* (percent + flat-seconds threshold → server scrobble) | `src/app/settings/scrobbling.tsx:1-11` — Last.fm/ListenBrainz handled by server setup, not app |
| Themes, fonts, i18n (13 langs), home reorder, mini player, swipe actions | `src/app/settings/theme.tsx`, `font.tsx`, `language.tsx`, `home-sections.tsx`, `player-buttons.tsx` |
| Android Auto, landscape/tablet layouts, lock-screen/background controls | `modules/car-auto`, layout screens; queue sync README |
| Album "chapters" concept exists only as release-grouping comment | `src/lib/releaseGroups.ts` (no M4B/audiobook chapters anywhere) |

Notably **absent** (verified by grep across `src/` + `modules/`): Chromecast/Google Cast, listening-statistics screen, M3U/XSPF playlist **import** (only Jellyfin M3U-tuner radio mentions, `src/api/jellyfin.ts:1115`), credits UI (`src/api/subsonic.ts:100` parses role credits, no renderer found), album-edition collapsing, podcast subscriptions/unread badge, mid-track playback resume (`player.ts:2667-2669` resume is queue-level only), CarPlay (zero hits anywhere), download transfer **resume** at byte offset (only retry-from-scratch, `src/store/downloads.ts:1109/1267`), EQ preset import/export.

iOS gaps per README (`README.md:51`): CarPlay, casting, equalizer, gapless — EQ module config confirms Android-only; casting partially bridged already (`modules/audio-route/expo-module.config.json:2` declares `["apple","android"]`).

Capability deltas already documented in `src/api/jellyfin.ts` (Jellyfin vs Navidrome/OpenSubsonic): star ratings :810, per-track ReplayGain :353, server queue :1101, favorite-date :313, artist/genre counts :79.

---

## Section 2 — Missing features, ranked

Priority = value ÷ (effort × duplication risk). All plug-in points verified file:line.

### 1. Podcast subscriptions + "new episode" badge
- **Why**: podcasts today are "open a show ⇒ it reads the feed" (`src/app/podcast/[id].tsx:9`: "Nothing is subscribed: opening lands it."). No watch-list, no unread count, no Home shelf of new episodes — the one thing that makes a podcast app usable daily.
- **Plug in**: `src/api/podcasts.ts` (add subscribedChannels set + lastSeen pubDate in `src/lib/podcastDb.ts`), Home shelf `src/lib/homeWidget.ts`, badge render in `src/components/PodcastRow.tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: trivial — feed parsing (`src/lib/podcastFeed.ts:173` already parses pubDate) and SQLite exist; badge is plain RN.
- **Already in flight (2026-10-22)**: open PR #278 introduces `PodcastEpisode.firstSeenAt` + `PodcastChannel.newSinceAt` (the arrival clocks) and renders a per-show `new` dot/mark. What remains for this feature: the watchlist/subscription concept (shows nobody opens still refresh), unread counts across shows, and the Home-shelf badge.

### 2. Mid-track playback resume ("resume where you left off")
- **Why**: essential for long-form audio (podcasts, audiobook-ish tracks, classical >10-min). Existing resume (`src/store/player.ts:2667-2669`) is *queue*-level auto-resume after a cast handoff, not in-track position. OpenSubsonic even has a `position` for `streamUrl`; Navidrome supports it.
- **Plug in**: `src/store/player.ts` start position (`seek`) at resume, persist in `src/store/playHistory.ts` rows; expose as `positionSeconds` when building stream URL in `src/api/subsonic.ts`.
- **Feasibility**: expo-audio `PlaybackState.requestPlaybackState({positionMillis})` — straightforward.

### 3. Google Cast (Chromecast) alongside UPnP/Sonos
- **Why**: UPnP/DLNA + Sonos (`src/store/upnp.ts`, `modules/upnp-cast`) misses the largest renderer population on the market the app targets (Chromecast-branded speakers/TVs); no `cast(` API or google-cast code exists anywhere.
- **Plug in**: new sibling to `modules/upnp-cast` feeding the same `src/store/castMedia.ts` metadata pipeline (`castMedia.ts:1-8`); renderer list UI `src/components/OutputSheet.tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: medium — Cast is a proprietary protobuf/CAES stack; no mature Expo/RN library, but SSA+CAES bridge exists in OSS (e.g. port Homebridge/python-cast logic) or reuse the UPnP bridge for cast-for-DLNA devices. Worth a design decision before code.

### 4. iOS parity (cast / equalizer / gapless / CarPlay)
- **Why**: README `:51` advertises four gaps on iOS; EQ module config is literally `platforms: ["android"]` (`modules/audio-eq/expo-module.config.json:2`); gapless is native-jump logic in `src/store/player.ts:1902`. `modules/audio-route/expo-module.config.json:2` already declares apple — the bridge is half-built.
- **Plug in**: port `modules/audio-eq` to iOS Audio EQ (native Unity sample rate, per-track) and gapless via two decoders in `player.ts`; CarPlay as `car-auto` sibling (`modules/car-auto`).
- **Feasibility**: EQ/gapless realistic on iOS (WebView-style native module exists); CarPlay (AutoPlay API) is high effort and license-friction — consider deferring.

### 5. Listening statistics (top artists/albums/tracks, play counts, listening time)
- **Why**: only history is the last 100 songs (`src/store/playHistory.ts:22` `MAX = 100`); no aggregate anywhere. Server-side play counts are already trusted (used for 'frequent' sort `src/api/data.ts:592-595`).
- **Plug in**: aggregate from scrobble history at `src/store/playHistory.ts` (raise MAX, add SQLite stats) + surface a "Your listens" tab in `src/app/(tabs)/library.tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: pure TS/SQLite; trivial. Navidrome exposes per-user scrobble counts via `scrobbles/lastplay`? — otherwise local history suffices.

### 6. Credits / featuring UI (performers, roles)
- **Why**: OpenSubsonic role credits are already *parsed* (`src/api/subsonic.ts:100` "Other credits by role") but never displayed anywhere — a free data win; users ask "who played on this / who's the feature?"
- **Plug in**: render in `src/components/TrackListView.tsx` (row meta) and `src/app/album/[id].tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: trivial once parsed; Navidrome populates it, Jellyfin maps partly.

### 7. Album-edition collapsing (deluxe/same-album versions dedup)
- **Why**: discography is already typed-grouped (`src/lib/releaseGroups.ts`) but a reissue/deluxe pair still shows twice; `musicBrainzId` is parsed (`src/api/subsonic.ts:137`) but unused for dedup.
- **Plug in**: `src/lib/releaseGroups.ts` (merge key = musicBrainzReleaseGroup + a primary-type pick) then filter in `src/app/artist/[id].tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: moderate heuristic; OpenSubsonic `musicBrainzReleaseGroup`/`MusicBrainzReleaseGroupIds` fields are the safe key when present, fallback name+year.

### 8. M3U/XSPF playlist import
- **Why**: radio mentions M3U only as a Jellyfin config hint (`src/app/radio.tsx:284`); users with local `.m3u`/server-exported playlists have no path in. `expo-document-picker` is already a dependency.
- **Plug in**: new `src/lib/playlistImport.ts` + menu action in `src/app/playlist/` sheet; match track ids via `src/api/data.ts` search (`SearchResult` type exists `backend.ts`).
- **Feasibility**: good — matching by artist–title is the known-hard part; Subsonic `search3` batching helps.

### 9. Offline-first download scheduling
- **Why**: while manual-offline, downloads are greyed *and dead* (`src/app/settings/downloads.tsx:85` `downloadsLive = !isManualOffline()`). Queueing "download this album when I get signal" is the classic failure mode of commute-dead-zone users.
- **Plug in**: `src/store/downloads.ts` accept pending entries when `isManualOffline()`; fire on reconnect hook in `src/api/netGate.ts` (`settings.ts:1028` auto-switch).
- **Feasibility**: small — the downloads state machine already survives restarts (`downloads.ts:1109` comments).

### 10. Transfer pause/resume (byte-offset resume)
- **Why**: downloads retry-from-scratch with backoff (`src/store/downloads.ts:129 RETRY_PAUSE_MS`, :1109 "already downloaded items survive a restart" — files, not *partial* transfers). On flaky self-hosted servers a 100 MB FLAC re-downloads entirely after one 4xx.
- **Plug in**: HTTP Range in the fetch loop of `src/store/downloads.ts` (server side `supportsTranscodeOffset` already probed `src/api/backend.ts:333`), keep partial file in `src/lib/downloadsDb.ts`.
- **Feasibility**: Range requests are the known Subsonic-weak spot (not all servers send Content-Range); degrade to restart when absent.

### 11. Smart shuffle (affinity/seeded shuffle, favorites-weighted)
- **Why**: shuffle is a plain redeal (`src/store/player.ts:1176 shuffle: boolean`); the affinity machinery for mixes already exists at `player.ts:1658` ("three affinity sources … shuffled"). A "shuffle my library but like my radio" mode is the most-requested queue feature in peer apps.
- **Plug in**: reuse affinity pool builder when `shuffleOn` redeals in `src/store/player.ts:1203`.
- **Feasibility**: no new network calls — `getSimilarSongs` + playCount already in hand.

### 12. Podcast chapter / M4B position handling
- **Why**: podcasts play as songs; no chapter markers, no M4B seek-to-chapter (`grep chapter` hits only `releaseGroups.ts`). Pairs with #2 (resume) to make long-form audio decent.
- **Plug in**: `src/lib/podcastFeed.ts:89` regex parser to lift `chapters:` tag + MP4 chapters atom; markers in `src/components/TrackListView.tsx`.
- **Feasibility**: feed-level chapters trivial; MP4 atom parsing needs a small native or JS box-parser — moderate.

### 13. EQ preset import/export (share/restore presets)
- **Why**: presets are device-local only (`src/store/equalizer.ts:20 EqBand`, applied at `settings/equalizer.tsx:91 applyPreset(v)`); a new phone silently loses curated presets.
- **Plug in**: serialize `presets` in `src/store/equalizer.ts` to a shareable file + `expo-document-picker` import; keep Android-only guard (config `:2`).
- **Feasibility**: near-trivial JSON.

### 14. Cross-profile media matching (same media on several servers)
- **Why**: favorites/downloads/history are per-profile and never reconciled (`src/store/playHistory.ts:9` "persisted per profile … to avoid mixing"); users with mirrored libraries (common for self-hosted redundancy) duplicate everything.
- **Plug in**: match key = (artist, album, name)+duration in `src/api/data.ts` + `src/store/downloads.ts`; optional "also favorite on other profiles".
- **Feasibility**: fuzzy-matching risk; ship behind an opt-in setting. Low effort, medium UX risk.

### 15. Server storage quota surfaced next to app storage
- **Why**: the disk bar (`src/app/settings/downloads.tsx:40-50` diskSpace) covers the *device*; nothing shows the *server's* remaining capacity or the app's footprint against it, which is what remote-server users actually ration.
- **Plug in**: OpenSubsonic server-stats endpoints (`backend.ts:261 supportsPlaybackReport` pattern shows the capability-probe style already used) and extend `downloads.tsx` bar legend.
- **Feasibility**: Navidrome exposes some stats; Jellyfin `Storage/Query` endpoint does too; per-backend probes needed in `src/api/backend.ts:333`-style. Low priority, decent payoff.

---

### Explicitly *not* recommended (already exist)
Gapless, crossfade, EQ (+presets, Android-only caveat), ReplayGain, sleep timer, queue undo, shuffle, repeat, casting UPnP/Sonos, internet radio, synced lyrics/karaoke/LRCLIB, favorites, playlists, downloads (+quality, Wi-Fi rule, disk bar), offline mode, multi-server, local files, Android Auto, mini player, themes, fonts, i18n, autoplay/mixes, play history, pins, star ratings, multi-select, similar-artist browsing, artist radio, discography release-type filtering, queue sync across devices, podcasts-as-media, scrobble-threshold settings, song-cache management view.


---

# APPENDIX H — Former hygiene review, inline (removed from repo)

The file `docs/CODE-HYGIENE-FINDINGS.md` (dated 2026-10-21, branch `6d19644d`,
**pre-merge** — it predates podcast PR #273) was listed in `.git/info/exclude`
and has since been deleted from the working tree. Its §1 (real bugs) and §2a
(API-layer &amp; hooks duplication) are reproduced here **verbatim**; where the
fresh post-merge agents confirmed or corrected an item, the verification table in
**Part 5** and the corrections table in the header say so. The old sections
§2b–§2e (components/store/lib/app duplication) and §3 (removable list), §4
(boundaries), §5 (feature gaps) were re-researched from scratch by the six
post-merge agents (Parts 1–8 of this report) — those parts carry the current
line numbers and supersede the old text.

## §1 Real bugs &amp; inconsistencies (highest value) — verbatim

### Profile identity is re-implemented four times, three of them wrong
- `src/store/libraries.ts:23` `profileKeyOf()` is byte-identical to `auth.ts:208 profileScopeId()` but returns `null` for Jellyfin → every Jellyfin profile shares one album cache entry (`src/api/data.ts:329`, `:2099`). Keep one identity helper in `auth.ts`; express the Jellyfin restriction as a separate capability check, not a null identity.
- `src/store/player.ts:3073` `queueStorageKey()` asks the mode before the profile: an account that drops offline writes its queue into the shared `resonus.queue.offline` bucket the local-files profile uses. `playHistory.ts:24-41` documents exactly this hazard and refuses it ("asking about the mode first put every account's offline listening into one shared bucket"); the rule was never carried over. Derive from `profileScopeId()`, delete the offline-first branch.
- `src/store/recentSearches.ts:66` `currentKey` is only consulted inside `hydrate`; `add/remove/clear` call `persist()` with no ownership check → items loaded for profile A get written under profile B's key after a switch. Use `profileScopeGuard()` (already used by `settings.ts:1338`, `lastPlayed.ts:75`, `pins.ts:49`, `autoDownloads.ts:41`) and gate `persist()` on `scope.owns(key)` (as `pins.ts:58` does). **Not shipped 2026-10-22: writers are all `Pressable` handlers, so the race is not reachable on command — deferred to the store-cluster merge.**
- `src/store/playCounts.ts:10` `KEY = 'resonus.localPlayCounts'` is the only persisted collection in the store cluster that is not partitioned per profile (`pins.ts:25`, `lastPlayed.ts:44`, `settings.ts:67`, `autoDownloads.ts:38` all use `${KEY}.${hashKey(profileScopeId())}`) — "Most played" mixes song ids across accounts, which `player.ts:4772-4774` says are only unique per account. Also the only unbounded one: `bump` (:31) adds an entry per song ever played, no cap, whole blob re-serialized on every save. Adopt the scope suffix and cap like `lastPlayed.ts:98-99` caps at 300.

### The netGate contract is not enforced where it claims to be
- `src/api/podcasts.ts` is the only module under `src/api/` that never imports `assertCanRequest` from `./netGate`; `get()` (:48) goes straight to the socket, and `refreshAll()` (:205, called from `src/app/podcasts.tsx:24`) and `subscribe()` (`PodcastAddSheet.tsx:64`) fire live requests in offline mode. `netGate.ts:14` exists precisely so "forgetting a check upstream is a bug that shows itself" — this module is the counterexample. Add `assertCanRequest()` at the top of `get()` and the iTunes search (:256).
  — **CORRECTED 2026-10-22:** already fixed by the podcast PR (import at `:11`, gate at `:98` `assertCanRequest(!isManualOffline())` with the documented design reason). Keep as history only.
- `src/api/navidrome.ts:323` `ndJson` calls `ndLogin(auth, fresh=false)`; `ndLogin` returns the cached JWT at :54 *before* `assertCanRequest()` at :56 — a warm token means a live fetch with no gate at all. Today masked by upstream `isOffline()` guards in `data.ts`, exactly the "something always does" failure mode the gate was written to eliminate. Move the gate above the cache check. **Fixed 2026-10-22 by PR #284** (`navidrome-gate-before-jwt`, `418f2670`) — the claim reproduced exactly as written against the current file.
  — **RE-VERIFIED 2026-10-22, still present** (the `for (const fresh of [false, true])` loop in `ndJson` reaches the cache-return path first).

### Error messages exist in three localization conventions
- `src/api/subsonic.ts:550` throws bare English (`'Could not connect to the server'`, `'Server took too long to respond'`, `'Unexpected server response'`) and never imports `tg`; `src/api/jellyfin.ts:214` wraps the identical strings in `tg(...)`; `src/api/navidrome.ts:52` hardcodes Spanish (`'Sin contraseña guardada'`, `'No se pudo connectar…'` etc.). All reach the UI verbatim (`ErrorBoundary.tsx:28` renders `error.message`, `login.tsx:196` renders `e.message`). One shared `apiError()` helper in `src/api/` taking an i18n key and returning a `SubsonicRequestError`; delete the Spanish literals.
- `src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:27` hardcodes Spanish (`Algo ha fallado`, `Reintentar` :32) and never imports `useT` — the screen ignores the 12 other locales.

### Cross-backend mapping drift (real, small, fixable by hand)
- `src/api/jellyfin.ts:347` `toSong` maps `starred: favDate(it)` from `UserData` but omits `playCount` and `played`, while `toAlbum` (:377-378) maps both from the same block and `JfItem` (:110) declares all three → `SongInfoSheet.tsx:208 add(t('Plays'), song.playCount)` shows nothing for Jellyfin songs, and `data.ts` sorting by `playCount` silently degrades. Add `playCount: it.UserData?.PlayCount, played: it.UserData?.LastPlayedDate`.
- `src/api/subsonic.ts:962` and `:1500` `reorderPlaylist` and `savePlayQueue` bypass `request()` (to avoid long URLs) and re-implement its POST/response/error logic inline: they throw plain `Error` (`Error de red (${res.status})`, `'Error de Subsonic'` — untranslated, not even English), so callers can't branch on `.network`, and they skip the `retryWithCanonicalId` migration retry at :580 → playlist reordering fails against stale Navidrome ids while everything else self-heals. `savePlayQueue` additionally swallows every error (:1523). Add a `requestPost(auth, endpoint, params)` reusing the retry/parse/error path.
- `src/api/jellyfin.ts:234` `makeAuth` hand-rolls the AbortController + `setTimeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS)` + `catch { if (aborted) … }` scaffolding `request()` already has (:197-219) and throws bare `Error` (:259-267) instead of `SubsonicRequestError` → `store/auth.ts:341` branches on `e instanceof SubsonicRequestError && e.network` and takes the wrong branch for an unreachable Jellyfin server. Factor a shared `fetchWithTimeout()` used by both. **Corrected 2026-10-22 — the consequence does not hold: `auth.ts:341`'s `try` wraps `ping(profile)` at `:336`, which goes through `request()` (`backend.ts:57` → `jellyfin.ts:290`) and already throws the right type; `makeAuth` has one caller (`auth.ts:268 login:` via `backend.ts:53`), whose error `login.tsx:196` renders as `e.message` without type-branching, and the strings are `tg()`-wrapped. Duplication + type inconsistency stay as hygiene; no behaviour payoff, not shipped.**

### Silent data loss &amp; fragile state
- `src/lib/localQueries.ts:1090` `updatePlaylist` accepts `{name?, comment?, public?}` but spreads only `name` and `comment` into the stored record — `public` is silently dropped. `api/data.ts:1754` forwards `public` to the server *and* into the offline-queue patch (:1763), so an offline "make public" edit is queued and then lost. Apply `public` (or reject it explicitly). **Corrected 2026-10-22 — nothing is lost. A server account never reaches this function: `data.ts:1774` queues the patch and reconnect replays it through `Subsonic.updatePlaylist` *with* `public` (`data.ts:1461–1470` newly created / `:1474–1480` existing → `subsonic.ts:952–964` sends the param), which the server applies. The local-files profile does reach it (`data.ts:1767`) and there the toggle is hidden (`app/playlist/[id].tsx:622 hidePublic={offline}`, `PlaylistEditSheet.tsx:35`/`:170`); a local profile is always offline. `toPlaylist` (`:954`) never carries the field either, so it is unobservable: dead parameter, severity **Info**, not BEHAVIOR. Honest fix = drop `public` from the local signature or comment it. **Not shipped; close with the local-playlist cluster.****
- `src/lib/downloadsDb.ts:578` `albumsByIds` builds one `IN (…)` statement from every id with no chunking (`ids.map(() => '?').join(',')`), while `songsByIds` (:590) chunks at 400 and `localDb.ts:481 byIds` chunks at `PARAM_CHUNK` → an artist with &gt;~1000 downloaded albums throws, and the caller in `localQueries.ts:621` treats it as "no rows". Reuse the existing `chunked()` helper (:317). **Severity corrected 2026-10-22:** the throw needs ≈32k ids (vendored `SQLITE_MAX_VARIABLE_NUMBER 32766`), not >~1000 → latent hygiene, closed with the handle-cache/chunk extraction rather than as its own PR.
- `src/lib/localQueries.ts:733` `getArtistInfo(_id)` is a stub returning `{ similarArtists: [] }`, but `radioBuild.ts:157` consumes it as if real → offline radios silently lose their whole similar-artist tier. Return a distinguishable "unknown" (`null`) so `radioBuild` can fall back to its local seed pool, or implement it from the local catalog's co-occurrence data.
- `src/lib/navidromeRepair.ts:220` and `:224` `await remapMirrorIds(...).catch(() => {})` and `clearSongCacheFor(auth).catch(() => {})` — a failed remap (including `CatalogCollisionError`, thrown by `mirrorDb.ts:839` as a plain `Error` while `downloadsDb.ts:782` has a named class) is indistinguishable from a clean repair, and the verdict reported to the UI says the profile was repaired. Collect errors into the returned `Verdict` (`failed: true`); export one error class and throw it in both.
- `src/store/player.ts:4741` `reset()` hand-enumerates what it clears and has already drifted: `warmedIds` (:1471), `stallSince/stallPos/stallProbed/stallFellBack` (:2572-2575), `pendingSeek` (:2559), `queuedNext` (:1895), `radioRound` (:1753), `gaveUp` (:2646) survive a profile switch → `warmUpcoming()` (:1492 `if (warmedIds.has(song.id)) continue`) silently skips pre-warming the new account's tracks. Group the per-playback singletons into one object with a single `clearPlaybackState()` so the enumeration cannot drift.
- `src/store/upnp.ts:292` `upnpDisconnect()` resets its own module state (:304-308) but not `lastQueueSignature`/`lastPlayMode`/`inFlightSync`, which live in `upnpRemoteSync.ts` (:19-38) and are reset only by the exported `resetUpnpRemoteSyncState()` (:34), called from `player.ts:3612/3639`. The silent paths (`upnp.ts:314 if (!silent)`, called from `player.ts:4755`, `OutputSheet.tsx:241`) skip it → a signature describing a renderer that no longer exists. Move the reset into `upnpDisconnect()` and drop the exported free function.
- `src/components/Cover.tsx:92` `const memo = new Map&lt;string, { path?: string; at: number }&gt;()` keeps hits "for good" and never prunes expired misses — unbounded for a 15k-song library for the whole session. Same at `PlayingElsewhereCard.tsx:53 const taken = new Set&lt;string&gt;()`, never pruned. Cap (LRU or periodic prune on the existing `MISS_TTL` sweep); clear `taken` per session/offers refresh.
- `src/store/player.ts:1157` `HISTORY_MAX = 100` caps entries, not songs — each undo entry holds two full `Song[]` references (`queue` and `originalQueue`), so history can retain up to 100 whole album-sized queues. Cap total songs across entries, or store `{source, sourceHref}` and re-fetch the queue.
- `src/app/favorites.tsx:306-320` / `:334-345` two `Dialog`s with the same title/message but different semantics: `confirmDeleteDl` deletes `songIds.filter((sid) =&gt; !!files[sid])` (only what is on disk) while `confirmRemoveDl` calls `void deleteSongs(songIds)` unfiltered. Collapse to one confirm flag with one delete path.

### App-level correctness
- `src/app/login.tsx:64` re-derives the language list with `a.name.localeCompare(b.name)` — reintroducing the locale-dependent sort that `src/app/settings/language.tsx:17-23` documents as a bug ("choosing Русский jumped it and Українська to the top of the list under the finger"). Export `LANGUAGE_OPTIONS` from `@/i18n/languages` and import it in both.
- `src/app/history.tsx:43` hardcodes `d.toLocaleDateString(lang === 'es' ? 'es-ES' : 'en-US', ...)` — German, Russian and Turkish dates render in US order, while `settings/cache.tsx:36` and `podcast/[id].tsx:39` pass the app language straight through. One `formatDate` in `@/lib/format` with a single "omit the year when it is current" rule (there are also two `dayLabel`/`shortDate` implementations with different year-suppression rules).
- `src/app/settings/network.tsx:64` renders an empty settings page for a state that cannot be reached (`settings/index.tsx:103` only lists Network `...(auth ? […] : [])`, and the guard sits *after* the `useEffect` health check that already bails on `!auth`). Drop the branch or make it an `EmptyState` explaining why there is nothing to configure.
- `src/hooks/useServerCover.ts:102` gates the password dialog on `if (!auth.ndPassword)` while every other native-API entry treats the cleartext `password` as equivalent (`navidrome.ts:51 auth.ndPassword ?? auth.password`, `data.ts:511 canListNative`, `usePlaylistStars.ts:45 canAsk`) → a profile storing only `password` can upload covers but the hook demands a prompt first. Export one `canUseNativeApi(auth)` predicate from `navidrome.ts` and use it in all four places.
- `src/hooks/useBrowseSort.ts:28` casts browse-order strings (`ArtistSort`, `AlbumListType`, `SongListSort`) into the `SortField`-typed prefs map with `as unknown as SortField`, sharing the namespace and value space with list-sort keys → cross-key collisions possible. Widen `SortField` to a union of all order vocabularies, or key the prefs map by `${key}:${field}`.

## §2a Duplication &amp; refactoring — API layer &amp; hooks — verbatim

1. **Multi-library dispatch ladder hand-written 11× in `src/api/data.ts`** (:366/367 `getArtists`, :478, :638/641, :797, :880/881, :912/913, :941, :1203/1205, :1523/1524, :1535/1536, :1546/1547): `if (!ids) …; if (ids.length === 1) …; return Promise.all(ids.map((id) => …)).then(dedupeById…)` — each differs only in endpoint and merge rule (flat-dedupe, `{artists,albums,songs}` split, sort-by-`playCount`, shuffle). One `perLibrary(a, fetchOne, merge?)` combinator plus `mergeStarred`/`mergeSearch` variants; the three ad-hoc `.then` bodies become named merge functions (~60 lines, and the merge rules become reviewable in one place).
2. **Capability checks half-abstracted**: `backend.ts:261 supportsPlaybackReport` and `:333 supportsTranscodeOffset` are proper predicates, but every other backend capability is re-derived by string-comparing `serverType` at the screen — `serverType !== 'jellyfin'` at `MediaMenuSheet.tsx:108`, `SongMenuSheet.tsx:720`, `player.tsx:363`, `artist/[id].tsx:138`, `settings/player.tsx:63`, `radio.tsx:74`, `useCanShare.ts:21`, `PlayingElsewhereCard.tsx:155/162`, `explore.tsx:107`, `settings/personalization.tsx:46`, `data.ts:1191 starredByDate`. Rating, radio management, sharing, folder browsing, now-playing and starred-ordering are the same concept expressed six ways; a new backend means hunting twelve call sites. Extend `backend.ts` with `supportsRating/supportsRadio/supportsSharing/supportsFolders/supportsNowPlaying`.
3. **Two independent implementations of "most-played songs, composed from frequent albums"**: `subsonic.ts:1141 getMostPlayedSongs` (getAlbumList 'frequent', 15, flatten, sort by `playCount` with album-order fallback) vs `data.ts:558 derivedSongList('frequent')` (`DERIVED_POOL = 15`, same filter/sort/fallback, comment says so). `data.ts:907` calls the former; `data.ts:633` routes the Songs screen to the latter. Keep one (the `data.ts` one — it goes through the query cache and the library merge).
4. **`src/hooks/useGenreArt.ts:17` is a line-for-line copy of `createSlots` from `src/lib/slots.ts`** — `let inFlight` + `const waiting: (() => void)[]` acquire()/release() incl. the hand-off comment appearing in both files almost verbatim; `subsonic.ts:445` already uses the shared abstraction (`createSlots(2)`). `const gate = createSlots(MAX_IN_FLIGHT)` at module scope; delete 21 lines.
5. **`src/hooks/useTabBar.ts:54` re-implements `useScreenSize().short`**: `COMPACT_BELOW = 500` + `useWindowDimensions().height &lt; COMPACT_BELOW` with the same rationale written twice. `return useScreenSize().short;` and delete the local constant.
6. **`src/hooks/useScreenBottomPadding.ts:17` and `:33`** recompute the identical base (`withBar ? barHeight + MINI_PLAYER_GAP + insets.bottom : insets.bottom + spacing.sm`), differing only in the tail — and the comment at :14 says the calculation "mirrors the MiniPlayer position in `GlobalMiniPlayer`", i.e. a third copy. One `miniPlayerBottom()` internal helper; both public hooks add their own tail.
7. **Three `SORT_LABEL` tables over an overlapping vocabulary** (`useAlbumSort.tsx:21`, `useSongSort.tsx:23`, `useServerSort.tsx:31`): `alpha`→'Alphabetical' ×3, `artist`→'Artist' ×2, `year`→'Year' ×2, `added`→'Recently added' ×2; `recent`→'Default' collides with `server`→'Default' while `recent`→'Recently played' lives in the same table. One label table in `src/store/sortPrefs.ts` keyed by the union of sort keys.
8. **The sort hooks share ~30 lines of identical plumbing** (`useAlbumSort.tsx:40`, `useSongSort.tsx:63`, `useServerSort.tsx`: `useSortPrefs` read, `setPref`, `useState` fallback, `openRef`, `useMemo` over `[source, field, dir]`, the same `<SortSheet options field dir onPick openRef>` block) while answering four genuinely different questions. A shared `useSortMenu(persistKey, fields, fallback, default)` returning `{field, dir, setSort, openSort, sortSheet}`; comparator tables stay local. (`useAnimatedArtwork` and `useAnimatedCover` are unrelated features sharing a name — rename, not merge.)
9. **`data.ts:996` and `:1050` prefetch schedulers are the same background scheduler**: identical `if (busy) return; quiet-check; cooldown-check; busy = true; try { batches } finally { busy = false }` with two parallel module-flag/counter sets (`prefetchingAlbums`/`lastAlbumPrefetch`/`ALBUM_PREFETCH_PER_RUN` vs `prefetchingPlaylists`/`lastPlaylistPrefetch`/`PREFETCH_PER_RUN`). One `schedulePrefetch(kind, stale, perRun, concurrency)`; callers keep only their "what's stale" predicate.
10. **`useSelectionMenu.tsx:63` serialises one star request per selected song** — a 40-song multi-select is 40 round trips before the toast fires. `Promise.all` in small batches with the `slots.ts`-style limiter.
